## 连续语言模型与语言流 [[Back]](#toc)

连续 embedding/latent 空间中的语言建模，包括 ELF、CoLa、扩散语言模型与 flow matching language model。

### [1] [Observable Patterns Are Not Explanations: A Causal-Geometric Analysis of Latent Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12689)
*Darpan Aswal, Thomas Palmeira Ferraz, Yongxin Zhou, Maxime Peyrard*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (95.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文对潜在推理模型（LRM）中的可观测模式（如 BFS 前沿、可解码算术计算）进行了因果-几何分析，发现这些模式在缺乏所提议循环或课程学习的对照模型中也出现，且并非总是因果影响行为。因果干预表明潜在思维的利用是分级的而非二元的，几何分析揭示其影响集中在低秩方向上。因此，潜在思维应被视为隐藏计算而非隐藏解释，LRM 可解释性需要匹配对照和因果测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将潜在推理模型（LRM）中可观测的潜在状态模式（如 BFS 前沿、可解码算术计算）视为内部推理机制的证据，但这些模式是否真正反映模型的因果推理机制尚不清楚。作者旨在通过严格的因果分析和几何分析，检验这些模式是否真正构成解释。

Method: 作者选取两个 LRM（Coconut 和 CODI），并构建缺乏所提议循环或课程学习的对照模型。通过因果干预实验（如消融特定潜在思维步骤）测量潜在思维对模型行为的因果影响，并利用几何分析（如 SVD 分解、步骤间几何结构分析）研究潜在表征的结构与行为影响之间的关系。

Result: 可观测模式（BFS 前沿、可解码算术计算）在对照模型中也出现，且并非总是因果影响行为。因果干预揭示潜在思维的利用是分级的（graded）而非二元的，其因果效应大小与思维对行为的影响成正比。几何分析表明这种效应集中在低秩方向上，且步骤间几何结构随行为影响增加而变得更加结构化。

Conclusion: 潜在思维应被视为隐藏计算（hidden computation）而非隐藏解释（hidden explanation），仅凭可解码性、注意力或静态结构不足以建立机制。LRM 可解释性需要匹配对照和因果测试来验证机制性主张。

Classification reason: 该论文研究的是 latent reasoning models (LRMs)，即用连续思维替代显式思维链的连续语言模型，具体分析了 Coconut 和 CODI 两种模型。这与 [CANONICAL] 连续语言模型与语言流 (continuous_language_flow) 高度相关，该子主题涵盖连续 embedding/latent 空间中的语言建模，包括 ELF、CoLa 等。

Importance reason: Directly challenges interpretability claims in continuous language models, a top priority subtopic, with rigorous causal-geometric analysis. Signals: continuous language model, latent reasoning, causal intervention, geometric analysis, interpretability

Abstract: Latent reasoning models (LRMs) replace explicit chain-of-thought with continuous thoughts. Recent work treats observable latent-state patterns, such as BFS-like frontiers and decodable arithmetic computation, as evidence for internal reasoning mechanisms. Evaluating two LRMs (Coconut and CODI) against controls lacking the proposed recurrence or curriculum, we find these patterns also appear in the controls and do not always causally affect behavior. Causal interventions reveal that latent-thought utilization is not binary but graded, scaling with a thought's causal effect on model behavior. Geometric analyses reveal this effect concentrates in low-rank directions whose step-to-step geometry grows more structured as their behavioral influence increases. Latent thoughts should therefore be treated as hidden computation, not hidden explanation: decodability, attention, or static structure alone cannot establish mechanism. LRM interpretability thus requires matched controls and causal tests.

</details>

### [2] [Foresight: Iterative Reasoning About Clues that Matter for Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12550)
*Arthur Zhang, Carl Qi, Donne Su, Xiangyun Meng, Amy Zhang, Joydeep Biswas*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 值得关注 (65.8) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Foresight 是一个测试时框架，通过微调 VLM 在图像空间中交替进行运动规划提案和基于语言目标与视觉上下文的批评，利用人类反馈的奖励模型进行强化学习后训练，实现机器人无地图导航中稀疏语言指令下的迭代运动优化，在真实环境中任务成功率提升37%，干预次数减少52%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界无地图导航中，稀疏语言指令往往存在目标未完全指定（underspecified）的问题，机器人需要推断哪些环境线索（如坡道、标志、绕行路线）与到达目标相关。现有方法依赖已知的导航因子和封闭集合类别，或在运动规划前识别线索而忽略规划依赖的线索，缺乏灵活性。

Method: Foresight 框架在测试时让微调后的 VLM 交替执行两个步骤：(1) 在图像空间中提出运动规划（propose motion plans）；(2) 使用语言目标和视觉上下文对规划进行批评（critique）。后续规划基于先前的批评进行条件化，实现执行前的迭代运动优化。为了将规划批评和修正与开放集行为偏好对齐，从人类反馈中学习奖励模型，并用强化学习在规划-批评循环中对 VLM 进行后训练。

Result: 在离线评估和6个真实世界环境中，Foresight 相比最先进的测试时推理和基础模型基线，平均任务成功率提升37%，每次任务干预次数减少52%，并在 Jetson AGX Orin 上实时运行。

Conclusion: Foresight 证明了利用 VLM 进行测试时迭代推理可以有效解决稀疏语言指令下的无地图导航问题，通过规划-批评循环和人类反馈强化学习，使机器人能够发现并利用与环境相关的开放集线索，显著提升导航性能。

Classification reason: 该论文提出Foresight框架，利用VLM在机器人导航中进行迭代式运动规划与修正，属于具身智能中的规划控制，与[CANONICAL]具身世界模型与规划控制高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong world model and VLM reasoning for navigation, but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: world model, VLM reasoning, robot navigation, test-time optimization, reinforcement learning from human feedback

Abstract: Open-world mapless navigation from sparse language instructions requires resolving underspecified goals and inferring which environmental cues are relevant for reaching the goal. For instance, reaching an out-of-view destination may require interpreting ramps, signs, or detours that reveal where to go or which route to take. Prior works are limited by their reliance on known navigation factors and closed-set factor categories, or identify cues before motion planning and miss plan-dependent cues. We argue that pretrained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) can discover novel instruction-relevant cues, but require adaptation to focus on which cues matter and how they should influence motion planning. We realize these ideas in Foresight, a test-time framework in which a finetuned VLM alternates between proposing image-space motion plans and critiquing them using the language goal and visual context. Subsequent plans are conditioned on prior critiques, enabling iterative motion refinement before execution. To align plan critiques and refinements with open-set behavior preferences, we learn a reward model from human feedback and use it to post-train the VLM with reinforcement learning in the plan-critique loop. In offline evaluations and 6 real-world environments, Foresight improves average task success by 37% and reduces interventions per mission by 52% relative to state-of-the-art test-time reasoning and foundation-model baselines, while running in real-time on a Jetson AGX Orin. We will release code, data, and training details to support future work on test-time reasoning for robot motion refinement. Additional videos at:

</details>


### [3] [From Imitation to Alignment: Human-Preference Flow Policies for Long-Horizon Sidewalk Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12603)
*Honglin He, Zhizheng Liu, Yukai Ma, Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出FlowPilot，一种仅使用单目RGB相机的无地图长时程人行道导航策略。通过锚定流匹配进行大规模机器人数据预训练，再通过人在环路的偏好学习进行对齐，显著提升了导航成功率和社会合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程人行道导航对微移动机器人（如送餐机器人、电动轮椅）至关重要，但面临人行道地形复杂、行人不可预测等挑战。模仿学习策略存在复合误差、缺乏社会合规性和反事实推理能力等问题，亟需更鲁棒的导航策略。

Method: 提出FlowPilot，包含两个阶段：(1) 使用锚定流匹配（anchored flow matching）作为动作表示，在大规模机器人车队数据上进行策略预训练，以捕捉人行道导航行为的多模态分布；(2) 设计人在环路的偏好学习方案，利用少量人类干预数据对策略进行微调，增强反事实推理和社会合规性。

Result: 在仿真中，FlowPilot达到42%的成功率和66%的路线完成率。在真实世界中，FlowPilot-HP（偏好学习版本）相比基础模型将干预率（IR）降低40.0%，将接近干预率（NIR）降低52.1%，显著提升了鲁棒性和社会合规性。

Conclusion: FlowPilot证明了流匹配动作表示与人类偏好学习相结合可以有效解决长时程人行道导航中的模仿学习局限，在仅使用单目RGB相机的轻量级感知条件下实现了鲁棒、高效的导航性能。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于单目RGB相机的长时程人行道导航策略，使用锚定流匹配（anchored flow matching）进行策略预训练，并通过人类偏好学习进行对齐。这属于具身智能中的机器人导航与规划控制，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]（embodied_world_model）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong robotics world model work but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: world model, flow matching, human preference alignment, sidewalk navigation, monocular RGB

Abstract: Autonomous long-horizon sidewalk navigation is essential for micro-mobility applications such as robotic food delivery and assistive electronic wheelchairs. Unlike autonomous driving on the road, long-horizon sidewalk navigation requires precise maneuvering through unpredictable sidewalk terrains and pedestrians, with a lightweight perception stack as minimal as a single monocular RGB camera. While imitation learning (IL) from demonstrations offers a practical solution, the resulting autopilot policy often suffers from compounding errors, a lack of social compliance on sidewalks, and deficiencies in counterfactual reasoning to handle complex situations. To address these challenges, we introduce FlowPilot, a mapless navigation policy that achieves robust and efficient long-horizon navigation performance using only a monocular RGB camera. We first propose to use anchored flow matching as an action representation for policy pre-training on large-scale robot fleet data and to capture the diverse, complex, multimodal distribution of sidewalk navigation behaviors. To bridge the gap between imitation and alignment, we further design a human-in-the-loop preference learning scheme to tune the policy on a small amount of human intervention data. It strengthens the model's counterfactual reasoning and social compliance on sidewalks. We evaluate FlowPilot through extensive simulation and real-world experiments in diverse sidewalk environments. FlowPilot achieves 42% success rate and 66% route completion in simulation, while FlowPilot-HP further improves real-world robustness and social compliance, reducing IR by 40.0% and NIR by 52.1% relative to the base model.

</details>


### [4] [EWAM: An Enhanced World Action Model for Closed-Loop Online Adaptation in Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12690)
*Xin Zhou, Cong Miao*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 值得关注 (74.18) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: EWAM 提出一种基于冻结 Cosmos3 骨干网络的闭环在线适应架构，通过插入四个轻量级神经层（经验记忆层、异常检测层、策略路由层、动作修正层）在推理时进行协同推理，在零样本协议下减少对新任务布局的部署数据需求，无需微调骨干网络或额外演示集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体在部署到新环境时，通常需要大量任务特定的演示数据或对骨干网络进行微调，这限制了其泛化能力和部署效率。本文旨在减少对新任务布局的额外部署数据需求，实现零样本条件下的在线适应。

Method: 基于预训练且完全冻结的 Cosmos3 骨干网络，在 DiT 中间层插入神经经验记忆层提供任务上下文，在状态预测头后插入神经异常检测层实时监控预测与真实状态的偏差，神经策略路由层根据异常严重程度动态选择直接执行、保守重规划或回滚恢复，神经动作修正层利用执行诊断优化生成的动作块。记忆、异常检测和修正模块以可微方式深度集成到 Cosmos3 前向路径中，仅路由决策为离散监督信号。

Result: 在零样本任务协议下，EWAM 无需额外演示集和骨干微调即可适应新任务布局，性能提升完全来自推理时的协同推理机制。

Conclusion: EWAM 通过轻量级神经层的推理时协同推理，有效减少了具身智能体在新环境中部署所需的数据量，为闭环在线适应提供了一种高效且无需微调骨干网络的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出增强世界动作模型（EWAM），基于Cosmos3骨干网络构建闭环在线适应架构，面向具身智能中的动作规划与控制，属于世界模型方向下的具身世界模型与规划控制（embodied_world_model）规范子主题。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and efficient inference priorities, using a frozen backbone with lightweight online adaptation for embodied intelligence. Signals: world model, closed-loop online adaptation, frozen backbone, zero-shot task protocol, lightweight neural layers

Abstract: In this paper, we propose the Enhanced World Action Model (EWAM), a closed-loop online adaptation architecture built upon a pretrained and fully frozen Cosmos3 backbone network. Evaluated entirely under a zero-shot task protocol, EWAM is centrally focused on reducing the amount of additional deployment data required to adapt to new task layouts. Notably, no extra task-specific demonstration sets were introduced in any of the evaluations, and no fine-tuning was performed on the backbone network. Its performance gains stem entirely from an inference-time co-reasoning mechanism composed of four inserted lightweight neural layers: the Neural Experience Memory Layer located in the intermediate layers of the Diffusion Transformer (DiT) provides task-relevant execution context; the Neural Anomaly Detection Layer after the state prediction head monitors the divergence between predicted and actual states in real time; the Neural Policy Routing Layer dynamically selects direct execution, conservative replanning, or rollback recovery based on the anomaly severity; and the Neural Action Correction Layer refines the generated action chunks using execution diagnostics. Unlike naive feature fusion, the memory, anomaly detection, and correction modules are deeply integrated into the Cosmos3 forward path in a differentiable manner, with only the final routing decision being a discrete supervised one.

</details>


### [5] [Sparse2Act: Learning Action-Aligned Sparse 3D Representations for Cross-Domain Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12759)
*Yu Guo, Chang Yu, Siyu Ma, Yunuo Chen, Yin Yang, Ying Nian Wu, Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (61.46) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Sparse2Act 提出一种观察-动作对齐框架，通过任务空间末端执行器动作作为几何监督来预训练稀疏点云编码器，使编码器学习与动作对齐的3D场景表征。在LIBERO-10上达到86.9%成功率，并支持跨域迁移（LIBERO→Meta-World 73.4%）和sim-to-real迁移（真实世界72.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的稀疏3D编码器通常通过下游任务目标学习，导致表征与特定数据分布、策略架构和动作参数化绑定，缺乏可迁移的通用3D表征。作者希望利用机器人动作本身作为几何监督信号，预训练出可复用的稀疏3D表征，使其能跨域、跨架构迁移。

Method: Sparse2Act 的核心是观察-动作对齐框架。在预训练阶段，对稀疏3D token进行掩码，然后利用与观察配对的末端执行器动作（task-space end-effector actions）作为几何监督信号，训练模型从掩码token中重建与动作对齐的场景特征。预训练完成后，仅编码器初始化被下游策略复用，下游策略可保留自己的架构和动作空间（包括关节空间命令）。

Result: 在LIBERO-10基准上，500步微调后平均成功率达86.9%。同一预训练编码器支持LIBERO到Meta-World的跨域迁移，在Meta-World-5上平均成功率达73.4%。消融实验表明增益来自掩码动作对齐信号。真实世界实验中，仿真预训练加少量真实数据微调在四个任务上平均成功率达72.5%，展示了有效的sim-to-real迁移。

Conclusion: 机器人动作可以作为紧凑的几何监督信号，用于学习可复用的稀疏3D表征。Sparse2Act 证明了动作对齐的预训练策略能够产生跨域、跨架构、跨动作空间迁移的通用3D场景表征，为机器人操作中的表征学习提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究机器人操作中的稀疏3D表征预训练，利用末端执行器动作作为几何监督信号，属于具身智能与机器人操作领域，与[CANONICAL]具身世界模型与规划控制高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong robotics manipulation paper but outside user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: cross-domain robot manipulation, sparse 3D representation, action-aligned pretraining, sim-to-real transfer, LIBERO benchmark

Abstract: Explicit 3D representations are attractive for manipulation because they expose object shape, workspace geometry, and robot-object relations in metric coordinates. However, sparse 3D encoders are often learned through downstream task objectives, tying the representation to a particular data distribution, policy architecture, and action parameterization. We introduce Sparse2Act, an observation-action alignment framework for pretraining sparse point-cloud encoders. The key idea is to use task-space end-effector actions as geometric supervision: masked sparse 3D tokens are trained to organize scene features around the workspace motion paired with the observation. After pretraining, only the encoder initialization is reused by downstream policies, allowing them to retain their own architectures and action spaces, including joint-space commands. On the LIBERO-10 benchmark, our method achieves 86.9% average success after 500 fine-tuning steps. The same pretrained encoder supports LIBERO-to-Meta-World cross-domain transfer, achieving 73.4% average success on the Meta-World-5 benchmark. Ablations on the objective and decoder capacity show that the gains come from the masked action-alignment signal and remain useful across downstream action decoders. In real-world experiments, simulation pretraining followed by limited real-data fine-tuning achieves an average success rate of 72.5% across four tasks, demonstrating effective sim-to-real transfer. These results suggest that robot actions can provide compact geometric supervision for reusable sparse 3D representations.

</details>

Result: 在 90 次真实机器人试验中，GRASP 在三个难度级别上实现了 73.3% 的总体成功率，无需任何任务特定训练。

Conclusion: GRASP 展示了利用预训练 VLM 结合神经符号规划实现零样本开词汇桌面抓取的可行性，为轻量级、可泛化的机器人操作提供了一条有前景的路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 GRASP 框架，利用 VLM 将自然语言查询转化为神经符号目标状态，结合边界框检测实现机器人桌面抓取与任务规划，属于具身智能中的机器人规划与控制，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Abstract: For robotics to be effectively integrated into household or industrial environments, machines must adapt to natural-language prompts in real time. Although Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have enabled zero-shot generalization in robot task and motion planning (TAMP), current state-of-the-art approaches often remain computationally "heavyweight" or require extensive training on thousands of demonstrations. We present GRASP (Grounded Reasoning and Symbolic Planning), a framework designed as a step toward open-vocabulary tabletop manipulation. Our approach leverages a pretrained VLM to translate natural-language queries into neuro-symbolic goal states, grounded in the physical world via a bounding-box detection pipeline. Unlike methods that rely on fixed color lists or hard-coded coordinates, GRASP enables robots to interpret abstract spatial concepts such as "top shelf" and execute tasks without additional fine-tuning. We achieve 73.3% overall success across 90 real-robot trials at three difficulty levels, requiring no task-specific training.

</details>

Method: 提出Pipette平台，包含：(1) 43个开源可编辑湿实验室3D资产及可扩展资产构建管线；(2) 基于仿真回放的数据增强管线，将人类演示在仿真中回放，施加光照、相机、速度和动作扰动，并通过自动任务成功检查过滤生成片段；(3) 11任务湿实验室具身基准，涵盖样本处理、培养器皿操作、设备操作和精密放置；(4) 支持自然语言驱动的场景构建和任务注册。

Result: 仅用30个演示/任务，ACT达到65.5%平均成功率。仿真数据增强将SmolVLA从44.1%提升至74.7%（+30.6%），π0从40.4%提升至46.5%（+6.1%），验证了Pipette在数据高效VLA训练和评估中的有效性。

Conclusion: Pipette作为一个面向湿实验室机器人的仿真平台、基准和数据增强框架，通过仿真回放增强和自动任务检查，显著提升了VLA模型在有限演示下的学习效果，并降低了非专家用户定义新湿实验室机器人任务的门槛。

Abstract: Wet-lab robots can improve the reproducibility, throughput, and safety of biomedical experiments, but scaling their learning requires customizable simulators for safe and reproducible task generation, open editable laboratory assets, and efficient pipelines that turn limited demonstrations into usable training data. We present Pipette, an embodied simulation platform, benchmark, and data-efficient augmentation framework for wet-lab robot learning. Pipette releases over 43 open-source and re-editable wet-lab assets, together with an extensible asset-building pipeline. A key component of Pipette is its simulation-based data augmentation pipeline, replaying human demonstrations in simulation, applies lighting, camera, speed, and action perturbations, and filters generated episodes with automatic task success checks, rapidly expanding usable training data from limited manual demonstrations. We further introduce an 11-task wet-lab embodied benchmark covering sample handling, culture-ware manipulation, device operation, and precision placement. With only 30 demonstrations per task, ACT achieves 65.5% average success rate, while simulation augmentation improves SmolVLA from 44.1% to 74.7% and {\pi}0 from 40.4% to 46.5%, validating the effectiveness of Pipette for data-efficient VLA training and evaluation. Pipette also supports natural-language-driven scene construction and task registration, lowering the barrier for non-expert users to define new wet-lab robotic tasks.

TL;DR: EA-WM 是一个事件感知世界模型框架，在预训练视觉特征空间中展开候选未来状态，解码为结构化事件状态，并通过任务进度、语义一致性、物理可行性和不确定性项进行评分验证，从而引导采样规划，提升长时程操作任务的可靠性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: EA-WM 在冻结的预训练视觉特征动力学模型上，增加任务规范接地的事件预测与验证模块。具体包括：(1) 在视觉特征空间中展开候选未来轨迹；(2) 将特征解码为结构化事件状态；(3) 使用任务进度、语义一致性、物理可行性和不确定性四个评分项对候选未来进行验证；(4) 验证器引导基于采样的规划，门控候选动作，并在接触敏感场景中筛选PPO生成的提议。

Result: 在导航、可变形物体、墙壁约束和语言描述操作等多个任务上验证，EA-WM 的事件感知验证机制使特征空间世界模型更可解释，且与任务进度的对齐程度显著提升。

Conclusion: 事件感知验证能够有效增强特征空间世界模型在长时程操作任务中的可靠性和可解释性，使模型更好地与任务进度对齐，为机器人规划提供更结构化的指导信号。

Classification reason: 该论文提出EA-WM，一个面向长时程操作任务的事件感知世界模型框架，结合视觉特征动力学与任务规范事件预测/验证，用于机器人规划与控制。这直接对应[具身世界模型与规划控制]（embodied_world_model）这一规范子主题。

Importance reason: Strong world model contribution for robotics but lacks direct alignment with priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: world model, long-horizon manipulation, event-aware verification, task-progress grounding, feature-space dynamics

Abstract: Pretrained-feature world models provide a useful substrate for robot imagination, but visual or latent prediction alone does not determine whether an imagined future satisfies task-relevant events. Long-horizon manipulation requires progress signals that are relational, predicate-level, and physically grounded: whether an object has moved, whether a drawer or contact state has changed, whether a placement predicate is satisfied, and whether a candidate future is reliable enough for execution. We introduce EA-WM, an event-aware world-model framework that augments frozen visual-feature dynamics with task-specification-grounded event prediction and verification. EA-WM rolls out candidate futures in pretrained visual-feature space, decodes them into structured event states, and scores them using task-progress, semantic-consistency, physical-feasibility, and uncertainty terms. The verifier guides sampling-based planning, gates candidate actions, and, in the contact-sensitive LIBERO wine-rack setting, selects among PPOgenerated proposals. Across navigation, deformable-object, wall-constrained, and languagedescribed manipulation studies, EA-WM shows that event-aware verification can make featurespace world models more interpretable and better aligned with task progress.

</details>

Abstract: Distinguishing self from others is a prerequisite for social intelligence, yet humanoid robots that increasingly share workspaces with humans still lack this ability. Here we show that a humanoid robot can learn self-other distinction from proprioceptive-visual correspondence, without any identity labels or kinematic models. Once established, this distinction bootstraps a predictive self-model that maps joint configurations to three-dimensional body occupancy, capturing how the robot's body changes with action. In multi-agent scenes involving humans or morphologically identical robots, the system reliably identifies itself, learns a 3D self-model, and supports downstream tasks including target reaching, collision-aware motion planning, and human-to-robot motion retargeting. Together, these results outline a route toward bodily self-representation in robots that act and coordinate alongside others in shared physical environments. Project page: .

Signals: tactile interface, whole-body manipulation, force-supervised planning, contact-aware planning, humanoid teleoperation

Abstract: Whole-body humanoid manipulation of bulky, deformable, and shared-load objects requires distributed contact sensing and explicit force regulation, yet most imitation policies treat contact force only implicitly. On the other hand, different demonstration sources provide complementary modalities with inherent trade-offs: human demonstrations capture natural contact forces but not robot-executable actions, while teleoperation directly records robot actions but with less natural force regulation. This paper presents \textbf{WT-UMI}, a wearable whole-body tactile interface worn by human operators or mounted on humanoids, providing accurate observations of tactile images, contact forces, and end-effector poses across both human demonstration and humanoid teleoperation modes. We introduce a force-conditioned target-pose correction module that converts measured human poses into contact-aware robot targets by learning corrections from teleoperation data. To leverage the natural force interaction in human data, we propose a force-supervised planner that predicts end-effector pose chunks and contact-force trajectories. The predicted contact force serves as the reference for a tactile-based admittance controller. Across five contact-rich tasks spanning deformable objects, bulky rigid objects, and human--humanoid collaboration, WT-UMI improves success rate and reduces contact-position tracking error over four policy baselines. Our project page is available at .

*Sangkyu Lee, Seohyeon Park, Tackgeun You, Avi Caciularu, Idan Szpektor, Hwasup Lim, Youngjae Yu*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

TL;DR: 本文证明自回归策略（Autoregressive Policies）通过调整 tokenization 窗口和约束解码，可以在保证严格延迟边界的前提下实现实时执行，在仿真和真实环境中一致优于同等水平的流匹配策略，并具有更快的收敛速度和更好的指令跟随泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模 Vision-Language-Action 模型在实际部署中需要实时执行能力，但现有实时执行工作主要关注扩散策略变体，而自回归策略由于同步推理速度较慢，其实时执行能力尚未得到充分验证。本文旨在证明自回归策略也能实现实时执行并保持竞争力。

Method: 通过调整 tokenization horizon（token 化窗口）和施加约束解码来保证严格的延迟边界，从而支持多轨迹解码以最大化性能。在仿真和真实环境中，将自回归策略与同等水平的流匹配策略进行对比实验。

Result: 自回归策略在任务完成速度上显著优于同步推理的流匹配策略，同时保持更好的收敛速度和指令跟随泛化能力。实验覆盖仿真和真实环境，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 自回归策略通过适当的 tokenization 和约束解码设计，可以成为支持实时执行的竞争性策略类型，与扩散/流匹配策略相比具有独特优势。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 Vision-Language-Action 模型在机器人具身任务中的实时执行策略，属于具身世界模型与规划控制方向，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "具身世界模型与规划控制"。

Importance reason: Directly addresses real-time execution for autoregressive policies, a key efficiency challenge in world models and embodied AI. Signals: autoregressive policy, real-time execution, world model, efficient inference, flow-matching comparison

Abstract: Real-time execution, enabled by asynchronous inference that ensures both smooth action trajectories and fast reactivity, is critical for realistic deployments of large-scale Vision-Language-Action models. However, recent work on real-time execution primarily focuses on variants of diffusion policies, even though it is more critical for autoregressive policies given their slower rollout speed in synchronous inference. In contrast, we demonstrate that autoregressive policies can achieve real-time execution by adjusting the tokenization horizon and applying constrained decoding, thereby guaranteeing strict latency bounds that enable multi-trajectory decoding to maximize performance. Across simulated and real-world environments, we find that the autoregressive policy consistently outperforms its equivalent-level flow-matching policy counterpart while achieving significantly improved task completion speeds from synchronous inference. Coupled with the inherent advantages of autoregressive policies, such as faster convergence and better generalizability in instruction-following, these results confirm that autoregressive policies can remain a competitive policy type supporting real-time execution.

</details>


### [12] [GIVE: Grounding Human Gestures in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13435)
*Pengfei Liu, Gen Li, Junqiao Fan, Boyu Ma, Jindou Jia, Yang Xiao, Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GIVE 提出一种通过视觉和语义两条路径将人类手势信息注入预训练 VLA 模型的方法，无需修改模型架构即可提升机器人对模糊语言指令的理解和操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 VLA 模型将机器人操作视为纯文本驱动任务，忽略了手势等非语言线索在人类交互中的重要作用，导致语言指令模糊或不完整时意图理解不准确、操作不可靠。

Method: GIVE 通过两条互补路径增强 VLA 模型：1）视觉路径——在机器人观测图像上叠加手部骨架和指尖射线，显式指示目标物体；2）语义路径——生成人类手势的高层描述和任务指令，提供鲁棒的意图理解。两条路径联合指导 VLA 策略将手势与操作行为关联。

Result: 在真实世界人机交互实验中，GIVE 相比基线将目标物体识别准确率提升 40%，整体任务成功率提升 80%，并展现出对未见空间布局和不同参与者的强鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: GIVE 验证了将手势理解融入 VLA 模型的有效性，为更自然、鲁棒的人机交互提供了可行方案，且无需修改模型架构即可实现显著性能提升。

Classification reason: 该论文研究人机交互中手势引导的机器人操作，属于具身智能与机器人规划控制范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on human-robot interaction and gesture grounding, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and generative world models. Signals: Robotics, Human-Robot Interaction, Gesture Grounding, VLA Models, Embodied AI

Abstract: Human communication is inherently multimodal, where language is often accompanied by non-verbal cues such as gestures to convey intentions. However, current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models treat robotic manipulation as a pure text-driven task, overlooking the important role of gestures in Human-Robot Interaction (HRI). This often leads to inaccurate intent grounding and unreliable manipulation when language instructions are ambiguous or underspecified. To address this challenge, we propose GIVE (Gesture Intent via Visual-Semantic Enhancement), an effective approach that enhances pre-trained VLA models with human gesture understanding without architectural modifications. Specifically, GIVE incorporates gesture information through two complementary pathways: a visual pathway that overlays hand skeletons and fingertip rays onto robot observations for explicit object grounding, and a semantic pathway that generates high-level descriptions of human gestures and task instructions for robust intent grounding. By jointly leveraging visual and semantic guidance, GIVE enables VLA policies to better associate gestures with manipulation behaviors and adapt to dynamic interaction intents. In real-world HRI experiments, GIVE substantially outperforms the baseline, improving target object recognition accuracy by 40% and overall task success rate by 80%, while demonstrating strong robustness and generalization to unseen spatial layouts and diverse participants.

</details>

TL;DR: NavWAM 是一个导航世界动作模型，通过扩散Transformer将未来观测、目标进度值和动作块编码为共享潜序列，使视觉预测直接用于机器人闭环控制，在仿真和真实机器人导航任务上优于基于规划的世界模型基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: NavWAM 采用扩散Transformer架构，将未来观测（egocentric图像）、目标进度值（goal-progress value）和动作块（action chunks）编码为共享的潜序列，联合学习未来预测与动作/价值目标。通过仿真预训练+真实机器人适配，在图像目标导航任务上进行评估。

Result: 在离线基准和闭环真实机器人部署中，NavWAM 在使用默认策略模式（无需CEM式动作搜索）的情况下，优于基于规划的世界模型基线和代表性直接导航策略。

Conclusion: NavWAM 证明了将世界模型的视觉预测与动作决策联合学习可以有效提升导航性能，使视觉预测直接用于机器人控制，为具身导航提供了一种新的端到端范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 NavWAM，一个面向目标条件视觉导航的扩散Transformer策略，将导航世界模型预测直接转化为可执行动作，属于具身智能中机器人导航与规划控制范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model for embodied navigation with a novel action model that unifies prediction and control, aligning with high-priority world_model and efficient inference directions. Signals: world model, diffusion transformer, visual navigation, closed-loop control, simulation-to-real

Abstract: Goal-conditioned visual navigation requires a robot to act under partial observability by anticipating how its motion will change the future egocentric view and whether that change brings it closer to the goal. Navigation world models provide such visual foresight, but they remain prediction modules that require an external planner to convert predicted futures into closed-loop control. We propose Navigation World Action Model (NavWAM), a diffusion-transformer policy that turns navigation world-model prediction into executable action by representing future observations, goal-progress values, and action chunks in a shared latent sequence. By learning future prediction jointly with the action and value targets that determine closed-loop behavior, NavWAM makes visual foresight directly usable for robot control. We build NavWAM through simulation pretraining and real-robot adaptation, and evaluate it on image-goal navigation against planning-based world models and a representative direct navigation policy. Across offline benchmarks and closed-loop real-robot deployment, NavWAM improves over planning-based world-model baselines in our evaluations while using the default policy mode without CEM-style action search. *Baochang Ren, Xinjie Liu, Xi Chen, Yanshuo Liu, Chenxi Li, Daqi Gao, Zeqin Su, Jintao Xing, Zirui Xue, Rui Li, Xiangyu Zhao, Shuofei Qiao, Minting Pan, Wangmeng Zuo, Lei Bai, Dongzhan Zhou, Ningyu Zhang, Huajun Chen*

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: 本文提出 LabVLA，一个面向科学实验室场景的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型。为解决实验室数据稀缺和机器人形态多样性的问题，作者构建了 RoboGenesis 仿真数据引擎，并设计了两阶段训练策略：FAST 动作 token 预训练使 Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct 骨干具备动作感知能力，随后通过 flow matching 后训练附加 DiT 动作专家。在 LabUtopia 基准上，LabVLA 在分布内和分布外设置下均取得了最高平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型主要训练于家庭和桌面场景，难以应对科学实验室中特有的仪器、透明液体和固定协议流程。AI 可以辅助阅读文献、生成假设和规划实验方案，但物理执行仍需人类操作员。因此需要实验室专用的 VLA 模型和统一的学习框架来弥合这一差距。

Method: 1) 构建 RoboGenesis 仿真数据引擎，从原子技能组合配置实验室工作流，验证过滤后导出结构化演示数据；2) 提出 LabVLA 两阶段训练：第一阶段 FAST 动作 token 预训练使 Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct 骨干具备动作感知能力，第二阶段 flow matching 后训练在知识隔离条件下附加 DiT 动作专家进行连续控制学习。

Result: 在 LabUtopia 基准上，LabVLA 在分布内和分布外设置下均取得最高平均成功率，优于所有对比基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过 RoboGenesis 数据引擎和两阶段训练策略，LabVLA 有效弥合了 VLA 模型在科学实验室场景中的空白，验证了仿真数据生成与动作 token 预训练+flow matching 后训练范式的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 LabVLA，一个面向科学实验室的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，将机器人动作规划与执行作为核心，属于具身智能与机器人控制范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。同时论文涉及多模态理解（VLM backbone）与动作生成，也匹配 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on scientific lab robotics, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: VLA model, scientific lab domain, simulation data engine, flow matching action expert, low relevance to priority subtopics

Abstract: Scientific laboratories increasingly rely on AI systems to reason about experiments, but the physical act of doing science remains largely outside their reach. AI can help read literature, generate hypotheses, and plan protocols, yet the execution of those protocols at the bench still requires a human operator. Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide one possible interface between written protocols and robot execution, but existing policies are trained mostly on household and tabletop demonstrations and rarely encounter the instruments, transparent liquids, or fixed protocol workflows found in scientific laboratories. Closing this gap requires both laboratory-specific supervision and a unified learning framework that can accommodate the diverse robot embodiments used to execute experimental protocols. We therefore identify data and embodiment as central bottlenecks alongside model design. To address the data side, we build RoboGenesis, a simulation-based workflow and data engine that composes configured laboratory workflows from atomic skills, validates and filters rollouts, and exports structured demonstrations across supported robot profiles. On the policy side, we present LabVLA, trained with a two-stage recipe: FAST action token pretraining first makes the Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct backbone action aware before any continuous control is learned, and flow matching posttraining then attaches a DiT action expert under knowledge insulation. On the LabUtopia benchmark, LabVLA achieves the highest average success rate among all evaluated baselines under both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings.

</details>


### [15] [SPARC: Reliable Spatial Annotations from Robot Demonstrations at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13497)
*Nils Blank, Paul Mattes, Maximilian Xiling Li, Jakub Suliga, Thomas Roth, Moritz Reuss, Pankhuri Vanjani, Rudolf Lioutikov*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (61.46) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SPARC 是一个风险感知框架，能够自动为机器人演示数据生成结构化空间注释（边界框、物体轨迹、操作阶段标签），并为每个注释分配可靠性分数。相比纯检测基线，SPARC 在定位精度上显著提升，同时在高精度操作点保留三倍以上的样本。基于 SPARC 注释微调的模型在物体定位和指向基准上达到同类模型最优，在杂乱真实场景中训练的机器人策略也优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人演示数据中自动生成结构化空间注释（如边界框、物体轨迹）对训练具身策略和基础模型至关重要，但现有自动化管线缺乏可靠的质量信号——检测器置信度与注释正确性校准不良，导致要么接受噪声标签，要么丢弃有用样本。需要一种能利用机器人任务时空结构来生成可靠性信号的方法。

Method: SPARC 利用机器人演示中固有的时空结构（如物体轨迹的连续性、操作阶段的时序一致性）来生成可靠性校准信号。具体包括：1) 使用检测器生成初始空间注释；2) 利用时空一致性约束（如轨迹平滑性、交互阶段边界）计算每个注释的可靠性分数；3) 基于可靠性分数进行风险感知的样本筛选。同时构建了 IA-Bench 基准用于评估交互物体定位精度。

Result: 在 1.7k 个人工标注的演示数据上，SPARC 在定位精度上显著优于纯检测基线，在高精度操作点保留三倍以上样本。微调后的模型在物体定位和指向基准上达到同类模型最优，在更广泛的空间推理套件上保持竞争力。在杂乱真实场景中，基于 SPARC 注释训练的策略优于基线方法。

Conclusion: SPARC 通过利用机器人任务的时空结构生成可靠性信号，有效解决了自动注释管线中噪声标签与样本利用率之间的权衡问题，为大规模机器人演示数据的高质量自动标注提供了可行方案，有助于训练更可靠的具身策略和基础模型。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 SPARC 框架，自动为机器人演示数据标注结构化空间注释（边界框、物体轨迹、操作阶段标签）并校准可靠性分数，用于训练具身机器人策略和基础模型。这属于具身智能中从演示数据中提取结构化标注以支持策略学习与规划控制的核心问题，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong robotics annotation pipeline but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: robot demonstration annotation, reliability calibration, spatial grounding, object detection, policy training

Abstract: This work introduces Spatial Annotations from Robot Demonstrations with Reliability Calibration (SPARC), a risk-aware framework that automatically labels robot demonstrations with structured spatial annotations and assigns each annotation a reliability score. Structured spatial annotations, such as bounding boxes, object trajectories, and manipulation phase labels, benefit a broad range of robotics applications from training grounded robot policies and embodied foundation models to motion planning and hierarchical task composition. Existing automated pipelines generate such annotations at scale but provide no reliable quality signal: detector confidence is poorly calibrated for annotation correctness, forcing a choice between accepting noisy labels or discarding useful samples. In contrast to existing automated pipelines, SPARC leverages the spatio-temporal structure inherent to robot tasks to generate a reliability signal, reducing noisy labels and retaining more useful samples. We further introduce Interaction-Aware Bench (IA-Bench), a benchmark that measures model accuracy in grounding the locations of interacted objects in robot demonstrations. On 1.7k human-annotated demonstrations spanning diverse embodiments and scenarios, SPARC significantly outperforms detection-only baselines in localization accuracy while retaining three times more samples at high-precision operating points. Our experiments demonstrate that models finetuned on our annotations achieve state-of-the-art results on object-grounding and pointing benchmarks among similarly sized models, while remaining competitive on broader spatial-reasoning suites without manually verified or annotated training data. Furthermore, policies trained on SPARC-generated annotations outperform baselines in cluttered, visually ambiguous real-world scenes. Code, data, and models are available at .

</details>


### [16] [$\texttt{WEAVER}$, Better, Faster, Longer: An Effective World Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13672)
*Arnav Kumar Jain, Yilin Wu, Jesse Farebrother, Gokul Swamy, Andrea Bajcsy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WEAVER 是一个面向机器人操作的多视角世界模型，通过 flow-matching 损失预测未来潜变量和奖励，同时实现了高保真度、长时序一致性和高效率，在真实机器人上验证了策略评估（与真实成功率相关性 ρ=0.870）、策略改进（在 π0.5 基础上提升 38%）和测试时规划（提升 14%，速度提升 5-10 倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型在机器人领域有巨大潜力（策略评估、策略改进、测试时规划），但现有方法难以同时满足保真度、长时序一致性和效率三个要求。作者希望设计一个能同时满足这三项要求的架构，以解锁机器人操作任务中的实际应用。

Method: WEAVER 采用多视角世界模型架构，通过 flow-matching 损失训练来预测未来潜变量和奖励值。论文蒸馏了模型架构、记忆机制和预测目标方面的关键设计决策，以支持长时序动态操作任务。模型在真实机器人硬件上部署，用于策略评估、策略改进（基于 π0.5 基础模型）和测试时规划。

Result: 在真实机器人操作任务中：策略评估与真实成功率的相关系数 ρ=0.870；策略改进在 π0.5 基础上提升 38% 的真实成功率；测试时规划提升 14% 的真实成功率，且速度比先前世界模型快 5-10 倍。在分布外场景下也优于先前方法。

Conclusion: WEAVER 通过精心设计的架构和训练目标，首次在机器人操作任务中同时实现了高保真度、长时序一致性和高效率，验证了世界模型在策略评估、改进和规划中的实际价值，为具身智能中的世界模型应用提供了有力支撑。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 WEAVER，一个面向机器人操作任务的多视角世界模型，使用 flow-matching 损失预测未来潜变量和奖励值，并在真实机器人硬件上验证了策略评估、策略改进和测试时规划能力。这完全符合 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 的定义。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and flow matching priorities, with high practical robotics impact. Signals: world model for robotics, flow-matching loss, multi-view, policy evaluation/improvement/planning, long-horizon consistency

Abstract: The potential impacts of world models (WMs, i.e., learned simulators) on robotics are far-reaching -- policy evaluation, policy improvement, and test-time planning -- all with limited real-world interaction. To unlock these downstream capabilities, a WM needs to jointly satisfy three desiderata: $\textit{(i)}$ fidelity (i.e., producing simulated trajectories that correlate with reality), $\textit{(ii)}$ consistency (i.e., producing simulated trajectories that are coherent over long horizons), and $\textit{(iii)}$ efficiency (i.e., producing simulated trajectories quickly). We propose $\texttt{WEAVER}$ (World Estimation Across Views for Embodied Reasoning): a WM architecture that simultaneously achieves all three desiderata, providing state-of-the-art results on robotic manipulation tasks. $\texttt{WEAVER}$ is a multi-view WM trained to predict future latents and reward values via a flow-matching loss. We distill the key design decisions across model architecture, memory, and prediction objectives required to unlock the kinds of long-horizon dynamic manipulation tasks that have confounded prior world modeling approaches. We apply $\texttt{WEAVER}$ in robotic hardware, demonstrating its effectiveness at policy evaluation ($\rho$=0.870 correlation with real-world success rate), policy improvement (real-world success rate improvement of $38\%$ on top of the $\pi_{0.5}$ robot foundation model), and test-time planning (real-world success rate improvement of $14\%$ with a $5-10\times$ speedup over prior WMs). $\texttt{WEAVER}$ also demonstrates better performance than prior WMs when evaluated on out-of-distribution scenarios. Code, models, and videos at: .

</details>


### [17] [MaskWAM: Unifying Mask Prompting and Prediction for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13515)
*Hanyang Yu, Haitao Lin, Jingbo Zhang, Wenyao Zhang, Chenghao Gu, Heng Li, Ping Tan*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 值得关注 (72.52) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MaskWAM 提出了一种以物体为中心的 World Action Model，通过统一掩码提示与预测（Mixture of Transformers），在视频预测中引入物体级语义监督和空间锚点，显著提升了机器人在杂乱场景和语言模糊任务中的策略泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 World Action Models (WAMs) 存在两个根本性空间瓶颈：(1) 标准文本输入在杂乱场景中会产生指代歧义；(2) 无结构的 RGB 预测缺乏语义锚定，且容易受任务无关背景干扰。因此需要一种物体级、空间精确的建模方式。

Method: 提出 MaskWAM，核心设计包括：(1) 将掩码同时作为显式输入和预测目标，通过统一的 Mixture of Transformers (MoT) 架构进行联合建模；(2) 预测未来掩码提供物体级语义监督，抑制视觉噪声；(3) 首帧视觉提示（如目标物体掩码）建立精确空间锚点，减少语言歧义。模型本质上是视觉驱动的，直接掩码条件比文本条件提供更强的引导。

Result: 在 LIBERO、RoboTwin 和真实世界任务上，MaskWAM 在语言清晰和语言模糊任务中均显著优于基线方法，验证了掩码提示与预测联合建模的有效性。

Conclusion: MaskWAM 通过将掩码作为统一的输入和预测目标，有效解决了 WAMs 的空间歧义和背景干扰问题，为机器人操作提供了一种更鲁棒、更精确的物体级世界模型范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MaskWAM，一种面向机器人控制的物体级世界-动作模型（World Action Model），通过视频预测实现策略泛化。这属于具身智能/机器人领域的世界模型，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and multimodal reasoning priorities via object-centric mask prediction for robotic control. Signals: world model, object-centric mask prediction, mixture of transformers, video prediction for robotics, spatial anchor for language ambiguity

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) present a promising paradigm for robotic control via video prediction. However, current WAMs suffer from fundamental spatial bottlenecks: standard text inputs introduce referential ambiguity in cluttered scenes, while unstructured RGB predictions lack semantic grounding and remain biased by task-irrelevant backgrounds. To overcome these limitations, we introduce MaskWAM, an object-centric world-action model. By jointly integrating masks as both explicit inputs and predictions via a unified Mixture of Transformers (MoT), MaskWAM unlocks robust policy generalization. This design provides two key benefits: (1) predicting future masks yields object-centric semantic supervision that suppresses visual noise, significantly enhancing even standard text-conditioned WAMs; and (2) coupling this predictive supervision with first-frame visual prompts, such as target object masks, establishes a precise spatial anchor that substantially reduces language ambiguity. Crucially, as WAMs are inherently vision-driven architectures, direct mask conditioning yields substantially stronger guidance than text alone, establishing a precise and robust paradigm for manipulating unseen objects. Evaluations on LIBERO, RoboTwin, and real-world tasks demonstrate that MaskWAM significantly outperforms baselines in both language-clear and language-ambiguous tasks.

</details>


### [18] [ProPlay: Procedural World Models for Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12780)
*Yijun Ma, Zehong Wang, Yiyang Li, Ziming Li, Xiaoguang Guo, Weixiang Sun, Chuxu Zhang, Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.LG

TL;DR: ProPlay 提出一种程序化世界模型，通过过程图抽象成功轨迹并支持过程级预演，使 LLM Agent 能够在部分可观测环境中自我进化，持续改进对环境动态的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM Agent 方法通常依赖记忆或规划模块，但很少在两者之间形成闭环以持续精炼对环境动态的内部理解。在部分可观测环境中，Agent 需要主动探索、从有限反馈中学习并决定何时信任先验经验，现有方法难以满足这些需求。

Method: ProPlay 将成功轨迹抽象为过程（procedure），并组织成过程图（procedure graph），捕获任务阶段之间的因果转换关系。每个转换关联一个可靠性记录嵌入（reliability record embedding），用于从历史结果中估计其任务特定贡献。在每个 episode 之前，ProPlay 在已知图结构上模拟未来过程轨迹作为结构化软引导；执行后，利用环境反馈精炼图结构。

Result: 在公开基准上的实验表明，ProPlay 在环境理解和自我进化能力上持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: ProPlay 通过过程级预演和过程图的自适应精炼，有效提升了 LLM Agent 在部分可观测环境中的自我进化能力，为 Agent 世界模型提供了一种新的结构化抽象范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ProPlay，一种程序化世界模型（procedural world model），用于 LLM Agent 在部分可观测环境中进行自我进化。它通过过程图（procedure graph）抽象成功轨迹，支持过程级预演（preplay）和基于环境反馈的图结构优化。这属于世界模型方向中面向 Agent 规划与控制的具身世界模型（embodied_world_model）范畴，因此复用该 canonical subtopic。

Importance reason: ProPlay's procedural world model for self-evolving LLM agents directly advances world model and agent self-evolution, aligning with top-weighted direction and priority subtopics. Signals: world model, procedural abstraction, self-evolving agent, LLM agent, procedure graph

Abstract: Self-evolving agents are expected to improve through interaction without external supervision, but this remains difficult in partially observable environments where agents must explore actively, learn from limited feedback, and decide when to trust prior experience. Existing LLM-agent methods often rely on memory or planning modules, yet they rarely close the loop between them to continually refine an internal understanding of environment dynamics. We introduce ProPlay, a procedural world model that supports procedure-level preplay, where agents can rehearse future procedural paths using the learned world knowledge. Rather than representing experience as isolated rules or low-level action constraints, ProPlay abstracts successful trajectories into procedures and organizes them in a procedure graph that captures causal transitions among task stages. Each transition is associated with a reliability record embedding to estimate its task-specific contribution from past outcomes. Before each episode, ProPlay simulates future procedural trajectories over known graph structures as structured soft guidance; after execution, it refines the graph using environment feedback. Experiments on public benchmarks show that ProPlay consistently improves environment understanding and self-evolution capability over strong baselines. Our code has been released in .

</details>


### [19] [VISA: VLM-Guided Instance Semantic Auditing for 3D Occupancy World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13460)
*Ruiqi Xian, Yuehan Xian, Jing Liang, Xuewei Qi, Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

TL;DR: VISA提出一种训练时语义审计方法，利用离线VLM对3D占据世界模型中的物体实例进行结构化审计（类别假设、混淆、可靠性、属性、证据），通过可靠性加权分类、属性因子和场景级审计图损失蒸馏到语义logits中，在不改变推理流程的情况下提升nuScenes上OccWorld和GaussianWorld的mIoU，尤其改善了物体类和稀有类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D语义占据世界模型在物体类和稀有类上存在错误，影响自由空间解释、碰撞检测和时序状态传播。常见的VLM策略（将3D体素/物体特征与裁剪-标题嵌入对齐）虽能提升文本空间相似度，但无法可靠提升闭集占据mIoU。作者希望利用VLM的语义知识以更有效的方式辅助占据世界模型。

Method: VISA在训练时对每个物理物体实例的代表性裁剪区域查询离线VLM，获取结构化审计结果（类别假设、可能混淆、可靠性、属性、证据），并沿物体轨迹传播。审计结果通过可靠性加权分类损失、属性因子损失和场景级审计图损失蒸馏到匹配的3D物体体素语义logits中。推理时无需VLM参与，保持原有流程不变。

Result: 在nuScenes上，三次运行平均：OccWorld mIoU从19.06提升至20.05，GaussianWorld mIoU从21.36提升至21.91；GaussianWorld上物体类mIoU从18.18提升至19.16，稀有类mIoU从15.60提升至16.79。

Conclusion: VLM更适合作为可靠性感知的语义审计器来辅助闭集占据预测，而非作为通用的裁剪-标题嵌入对齐目标。VISA方法在不改变推理流程的前提下有效提升了3D占据世界模型的语义预测质量。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于自动驾驶场景下的3D占据世界模型（occupancy world model），通过VLM进行语义审计以提升语义占据预测质量，属于具身智能/自动驾驶中的世界模型方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL]具身世界模型与规划控制。同时涉及VLM用于语义审计，也匹配多模态理解与生成方向下的vlm_mllm_general。

Importance reason: Strong world model paper but focuses on autonomous driving occupancy, not the user's priority continuous multimodal or video generation topics. Signals: world model, 3D occupancy, VLM auditing, nuScenes, closed-set mIoU

Abstract: Semantic 3D occupancy provides a voxelized world state for autonomous driving and robot decision making, but object and rare-class errors can affect free-space interpretation, collision checking, and temporal state propagation. We show that a common VLM strategy, aligning 3D voxel or object features with crop-caption embeddings, improves text-space similarity without reliably improving closed-set occupancy mIoU. Motivated by this mismatch, we propose VISA, a training-time semantic auditing approach for existing occupancy world models. VISA queries an offline VLM on a representative crop of each physical object instance, obtains a structured audit with class hypotheses, plausible confusions, reliability, attributes, and evidence, and propagates it along the object track. The audit is grounded to matched 3D object voxels and distilled into semantic logits through reliability-weighted taxonomy, attribute-factor, and scene-level audit graph losses, while inference remains unchanged and requires no VLM. On nuScenes, averaged across three runs, VISA improves OccWorld from 19.06 to 20.05 mIoU and GaussianWorld from 21.36 to 21.91 mIoU; on GaussianWorld, object mIoU improves from 18.18 to 19.16 and rare-class mIoU from 15.60 to 16.79. These results suggest that VLMs are better suited to closed-set occupancy as reliability-aware semantic auditors than as generic caption-embedding targets.

</details>

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and continuous multimodal representation for robot control, aligning with top priority subtopics. Signals: world action model, representation visual-action tokenizer, continuous visual token, latent action, robot manipulation

Abstract: This work presents RepWAM, a representation-centric world action model (WAM) built on representation visual-action tokenizers. Existing WAMs typically inherit reconstruction-oriented video tokenizers from pretrained video generation models. Although these tokenizers preserve visual fidelity, pixel reconstruction alone provides limited guidance for learning instruction-following dynamics that connect future prediction with robot control. To address this, we explore a semantic visual-action latent space for representation-centric world action modeling. Specifically, we train a representation visual-action tokenizer that maps visual inputs into aligned visual and latent action tokens. We then pretrain our WAM to jointly model future visual states and the latent actions that connect them under language instructions, followed by adaptation to real robot trajectories for closed-loop manipulation. Experiments on real-world manipulation tasks and simulation benchmarks show that RepWAM delivers strong performance across diverse manipulation settings, while ablations highlight the value of semantic visual-action tokenization over reconstruction-oriented alternatives. These results establish representation visual-action tokenization as a promising foundation for world action models and a step toward generalist robot policies. Code and weights will be available at .

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反应式运动生成方法受限于计算复杂度，要么生成静态场景的全局轨迹，要么对环境做保守假设。主要瓶颈在于高保真环境下的规划运行时性能以及感知与规划模块的时间集成。

Method: 提出G-MAPP框架，利用GPU加速世界建模和基于向量场的规划，实现更快的并行状态探索用于准全局轨迹规划，并通过紧耦合感知-动作循环实现实时动态环境中的反应式运动生成。

Result: GPU版本相比CPU版本实现高达5倍加速，在真实7-DoF Franka Emika机器人上的简单和挑战性物理场景中均成功避障。

Conclusion: GPU加速的世界建模与向量场规划可以有效解决反应式运动生成中的计算瓶颈，实现动态杂乱环境中的实时、无碰撞运动生成。

Classification reason: 该论文提出GPU加速的机器人运动规划框架，核心在于加速世界建模（world modeling）与基于向量场的规划，实现实时感知-动作闭环，属于物理世界建模与交互式模拟器范畴，与[可交互与物理世界建模]（interactive_physical_world）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotics motion planning, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: GPU-accelerated planning, multi-agent, reactive motion, world modeling, robotics

Abstract: Reactive motion generation in unstructured environments remains an open challenge in robotics. Due to the computational complexity of collision-free motion generation, existing methods either generate global trajectories for static scenarios, or employ models that make conservative assumptions about the environment. This paper identifies the primary bottleneck as the runtime performance demand of planning on high-fidelity environments, and the temporal integration between the perception and planning modules. Therefore, we propose a framework that does not compromise on runtime performance and world representations for perception and planning by accelerating world modeling and vector-field based planning using the GPU. This allows us to achieve faster parallel state exploration for quasi-global trajectory planning, and tighter coupling of the perception-action loop in real-time for dynamic cluttered environments with off-the-shelf depth sensors. We quantitatively evaluate the computation-time and success rate differences for the CPU and GPU versions of our planner, and perform qualitative evaluations of our coupled framework using real-world experiments on a 7-DoF Franka Emika robot. Experimental results demonstrate that our GPU-based framework achieves up to a 5x speedup over the CPU version and successfully avoids collisions across both trivial and challenging physical world scenarios.

</details>


### [22] [Identifiability Without Gaussianity: Symbolic World Models and Near-Infinite Temporal Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12471)
*Seth Dobrin, Łukasz Chmiel*

Main category: stat.ML, cs.ET, cs.CL, cs.LG

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 可交互与物理世界建模

Importance: 高优先级 (94.87) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文证明 Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) 的线性可识别性受限于高斯假设，并引入 Physics-Grounded Symbolic Architecture (PGSA)，证明其在所有物理系统中（无论潜变量分布如何）都能实现精确线性可识别性，且误差仅受数值精度限制，从而获得近无限时间一致性。四个定理的核心代数部分已在 Lean 4 中形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Klindt 等人证明 JEPA 只有在世界潜变量服从高斯平稳过程时才能实现线性可识别性，这意味着对于任何非高斯物理系统，统计世界模型的表示误差会随时间单调增长。这一"高斯边界"对世界模型的时间一致性构成了根本限制，本文旨在突破这一限制。

Method: 提出 Physics-Grounded Symbolic Architecture (PGSA)，将世界模型的表示建立在世界动力学的因果生成器（causal generator）的符号化基础上，而非统计对齐机制。通过符号化方法，模型直接利用物理系统的因果结构进行表示学习，而非依赖数据分布的统计特性。

Result: (1) PGSA 在所有物理系统中（无论潜变量分布如何）都能实现精确线性可识别性；(2) 每步误差仅受数值精度限制；(3) 因此 PGSA 能在无限多步转换中保持时间一致性（近无限时间一致性）；(4) 统计世界模型在任何非高斯系统中都无法实现这一性质，无论模型容量或训练数据量如何。四个定理的核心代数部分已在 Lean 4 中形式化验证。

Conclusion: 符号化地扎根于世界动力学的因果生成器是实现近无限时间一致性的充分条件，并且在非高斯系统中是唯一条件。这揭示了统计对齐机制的根本局限，为构建具有长期时间一致性的世界模型提供了新的理论方向。

Classification reason: 论文提出 Physics-Grounded Symbolic Architecture (PGSA)，证明符号化世界模型在非高斯物理系统中也能实现精确线性可识别性和近无限时间一致性，核心关注物理世界建模与因果动力学，属于世界模型方向下的"可交互与物理世界建模"（interactive_physical_world）子主题。

Importance reason: Proves symbolic world models achieve near-infinite temporal consistency, directly advancing the user's high-priority world model and continuous representation subtopics. Signals: world model, symbolic grounding, temporal consistency, identifiability, Lean 4 formal verification

Abstract: Klindt, LeCun, and Balestriero ( ) proved that Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) achieve linear identifiability, the linear recovery of the world's true latent variables, if and only if the world's latent dynamics follow a Gaussian, stationary process. This Gaussian boundary implies a fundamental limit on temporal consistency: for any non-Gaussian physical system, the representation error of a statistical World Model grows monotonically with time. We prove that this limit is an artifact of the statistical alignment mechanism, not a property of World Models in general. We introduce the Physics-Grounded Symbolic Architecture (PGSA) and prove three results: (1) a PGSA achieves exact linear identifiability for all physical regimes, regardless of the latent distribution; (2) the per-step error of a PGSA is bounded by numerical precision alone; and (3) as a direct consequence, a PGSA maintains temporal consistency for an unbounded number of transitions, a property we term near-infinite temporal consistency. We further prove that statistical World Models cannot achieve this property for any non-Gaussian system, regardless of model capacity or the volume of training data. The algebraic cores of four of the theorems are formalized in Lean 4 with Mathlib4 v4.31.0 (zero sorry placeholders); the Klindt et al. converse is taken as an external premise. The contrast establishes that symbolic grounding in the causal generator of the world's dynamics is the sufficient condition and, in non-Gaussian regimes, the only condition for near-infinite temporal consistency.

</details>

### [23] [Diffusion Transformer World-Action Model for AV Scene Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12987)
*Ruslan Sharifullin, Benjamin Jiang, Kai Xi Chew*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.RO, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (94.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一个紧凑的潜空间世界模型，使用扩散Transformer（DiT）在冻结编码器的潜空间中预测自动驾驶的未来摄像头场景。通过系统诊断确定了四个关键成分（空间token、x0目标、残差锚定、匹配不确定性的采样），并揭示了失真指标（如SSIM）会掩盖扩散模型的真实优势——FID/KID指标显示扩散模型比回归方法好4.8倍。模型具有真实的动作可控性（转向与场景位移的Spearman ρ=0.81），并设计了1.7M参数的"跳跃"模型恢复完整运动幅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需要动作条件世界模型来预测未来场景，以支持规划与仿真，避免真实路测的高成本。然而现有紧凑模型预测的未来模糊不清，且标准失真指标（如余弦相似度、SSIM）会奖励模糊的回归均值，掩盖生成模型的真实质量。

Method: 采用编码-预测-解码流水线：冻结的编码器（如V-JEPA2）将当前前视摄像头帧编码为潜空间，然后使用潜空间Diffusion Transformer（DiT）根据当前潜变量和一系列自车动作预测未来潜变量，最后用冻结的解码器渲染为256×256图像。通过系统诊断确定了四个关键成分：空间token、x0目标、残差锚定、匹配目标不确定性的采样策略。还设计了1.7M参数的"跳跃"模型来恢复完整运动幅度。

Result: V-JEPA2编码器相比最佳单帧编码器将转向RMSE降低40%。扩散模型KID为0.078，回归方法为0.375（提升4.8倍）。动作可控性方面，扩散模型的转向与场景位移Spearman ρ=0.81，回归方法为-0.18。"跳跃"模型恢复的运动幅度为真实值的1.02倍，而单次模型不到一半。

Conclusion: 扩散Transformer在潜空间世界模型中显著优于回归方法，能生成更真实、动作可控的未来帧。失真指标会误导评估，应使用基于分布的指标（如FID/KID）。残差锚定和匹配不确定性的采样是生成高质量预测的关键。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and diffusion transformer priorities, with novel insights on distortion metrics and action controllability. Signals: World model for AV scene prediction, Diffusion Transformer (DiT) in latent space, Action-conditioned with high controllability (Spearman ρ=0.81), Reveals distortion metrics (SSIM) mislead, FID/KID 4.8x better, Compact 1.7M "jump" model recovers motion

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models let an autonomous vehicle predict future camera scenes from its own planned controls, enabling planning and simulation without real-world rollouts, but at compact, trainable scale the futures are ambiguous and the field's standard distortion metrics actively mislead: they reward a blurry regression mean over a realistic prediction. We confront this with a compact latent world model that, given the present front-camera latent and a sequence of ego-actions, predicts future scene latents a frozen decoder renders to $256 \times 256$ frames up to 8 seconds ahead, evaluated on 150 held-out nuScenes scenes. We first benchmark where to predict: across six frozen encoders spanning four representation families, V-JEPA2 with temporal context reduces steering RMSE by 40% over the best single-frame encoder. We then train a latent Diffusion Transformer (DiT) and, through a controlled diagnosis, identify the four ingredients it needs: spatial tokens, the $x_0$ objective, residual anchoring, and sampling matched to target uncertainty. In a Stable-Diffusion-VAE encode-predict-decode pipeline we expose the central tension: distortion metrics (cosine similarity, SSIM) favor the blurry mean, masking that the diffusion model is far closer to the real frame distribution. Inception-based FID and KID reveal a clean perception-distortion frontier: diffusion attains KID 0.078 versus 0.375 for regression ($4.8\times$ better), and a deployable train-derived calibration makes this practical without test-time ground truth. The model is genuinely action-controllable (steering drives scene displacement, Spearman $\rho = 0.81$, vs $-0.18$ for regression). We trace limited single-pass motion to a shared-present anchor and engineer a compact 1.7M-parameter "jump" model that recovers full ground-truth motion magnitude ($1.02\times$ GT), where single-pass models capture less than half.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 理论层面，证明等变潜世界模型的T步rollout误差在对称轨道上恒定（Theorem A），并由预测器的Lyapunov谱按通道分层界定可预测水平（T_j(ε)~log(1/ε)/λ_j），同时给出匹配下界。实验层面，在40维Lorenz-96系统上验证只有Z_N-等变网络能恢复完整Lyapunov谱（R²=0.98），并将该认证方法直接应用于TD-MPC2和V-JEPA等预训练世界模型进行零数据校准审计。

Result: 等变网络在Lorenz-96上恢复Lyapunov谱的R²=0.98，而密集和循环基线失败。TD-MPC2检查点落在认证的scope分类上——强扩张区域校准比0.94-1.02，弱扩张区域乐观，收缩区域正确弃权。在1M-317M多任务阶梯上，校准不随参数规模改善。V-JEPA 2-AC（1B参数，真实机器人数据）的交叉验证审计正确覆盖了过度乐观的切谱。

Conclusion: 规模买来插值，结构买来可认证的预测水平。等变性提供了非等变模型在任何规模下都无法拥有的可认证预测保证。该认证方法无需训练数据即可审计预训练世界模型，且模型规模扩大并不改善校准质量，强调了结构设计（等变性）对于可信世界模型的重要性。

Classification reason: 论文研究等变潜空间世界模型的可预测性认证（certified predictability horizon），属于世界模型的理论分析与认证方法。虽然不直接生成视频，但核心是潜空间世界模型的rollout误差分析与Lyapunov谱估计，与video_world_model中世界模型的预测能力、未来预测、长时序建模高度相关，且该[CANONICAL] subtopic是world_model方向下最匹配的现有主题。

Signals: world model certification, equivariant latent dynamics, Lyapunov spectrum, training-free audit, scale vs. structure

Abstract: Scale buys interpolation; structure buys a certified horizon. A world model's average error says nothing about whether a particular prediction can be trusted, or for how long. For equivariant latent world models we give a computable, multi-step certificate of the predictable horizon: $T$-step rollout error is provably constant over each symmetry orbit (Theorem A) and stratified channel-by-channel by the predictor's Lyapunov spectrum, $T_j(\epsilon)\sim\log(1/\epsilon)/\lambda_j$. The horizon is two-sided -- a matching lower bound makes approximate equivariance provably horizon-limited -- and the certificate is exclusive to structure: orbit-constant error characterizes equivariance, so no non-equivariant model has it at any scale. Empirically, on 40-D Lorenz-96 only a $\mathbb{Z}_N$-equivariant network recovers the full Lyapunov spectrum ($R^2{=}0.98$); dense and recurrent baselines fail. Because the spectrum is faithful, the certificate acts, a priori: under a fixed sensing budget a $c\times$-inflated certificate provably needs $c\times$ the budget, and the equivariant certificate meets a budget its inflated dense counterpart cannot -- with zero calibration data. The same read-out, unchanged, audits public pretrained world models training-free: TD-MPC2 checkpoints land on the certificate's own scope taxonomy -- calibrated where strongly expansive (ratio 0.94-1.02), optimistic where weakly expansive, correctly abstaining where contracting -- a map a deployed monitor replicates cell-by-cell, out-of-sample. Across the official 1M-317M multitask ladder, calibration does not improve with parameters. On V-JEPA 2-AC (1B, real robot data) the measured cross-check correctly overrides an over-promising tangent spectrum -- the cross-validated audit, not the raw number, is the deployable object. Scale buys interpolation, not a calibrated horizon.

</details>

Abstract: World modeling is emerging as a central principle for building intelligent systems capable of prediction, reasoning, and decision making. A central distinction can be drawn between explicit world models, which learn structured dynamics for rollout-based reasoning and planning, and implicit world models, which encode predictive structure within scalable learned representations. These complementary paradigms provide a foundation for physical AI in domains such as robotics and autonomous driving, enabling intelligence beyond reactive control under real-world constraints. Recent foundation models further suggest a pathway toward unified systems integrating perception, prediction, and action. Despite rapid progress, major challenges remain in hierarchical reasoning, long-horizon planning, and autonomous goal formation, which are critical for advancing toward artificial general intelligence. This tutorial presents a coherent framework in which diverse world modeling approaches are unified through shared predictive structure and differentiated by how such structure is represented and exploited.

</details>

Classification reason: 该论文通过分析80,814篇AI顶会论文，研究AI研究主题的相变现象，并定义了早期预警信号。论文在2025年数据中明确将"world models"列为需要监测的主题之一，与世界模型方向直接相关。由于论文本身是科学计量学/研究动态分析，而非提出新的世界模型方法，将其归入世界模型方向下的"视频世界模型"（video_world_model）作为最接近的现有规范子主题，因为该子主题涵盖世界模型相关的研究动态。

Abstract: Do research topics in artificial intelligence grow gradually, or do they advance through abrupt, detectable jumps? Analyzing 80,814 accepted main-track papers from five premier AI conferences (ACL, CVPR, ICLR, ICML, NeurIPS) spanning 2017 to 2025, we show major AI topics advance through topical phase transitions: remaining marginal for years, then surging across venues within one to three years. Large language models became the dominant cross-venue topic by 2025, diffusion models rose with comparable abruptness, and language-model methods crossed into computer vision via vision-language models, whereas reinforcement learning compounded smoothly, distinguishing genuine phase transitions from ordinary growth. This structure is our primary contribution: a large-scale, cross-venue characterization of how AI research reorganizes. We then ask whether a transition leaves a detectable footprint before it peaks. We define an early-warning signature, four publication-dynamics criteria frozen on 2017-2021 data, and evaluate it out of sample on 2023-2025 transitions, obtaining a precision of 27% and recall of 63% against a 13.5% base rate. Applied to 2025 data, the signature flags reasoning and test-time compute, agentic AI, multimodal LLMs, retrieval-augmented generation, and world models as topics to monitor over 2026-2028. The source code is also publicly available on GitHub at .

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: MoVerse 采用三阶段流水线：(1) 拓扑感知扩散将窄视场输入扩展为重力对齐的 360° 全景图；(2) 全景几何感知残差预测将全景图提升为持久 3D 高斯支架（Gaussian Scaffold），形成可直接渲染的空间记忆；(3) 高斯条件视频渲染器沿用户指定相机轨迹将支架渲染结果转换为逼真视频，其中双向扩散教师模型用于高质量条件渲染，再蒸馏为因果自回归学生模型以实现低延迟流式输出。

Conclusion: MoVerse 通过分离世界构建与观测渲染，结合显式 3D 表示的可控性与生成视频模型的感知质量，为单图像世界创建与交互式视频输出提供了一条实用路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MoVerse，从单张窄视场图像构建可交互漫游的 360° 全景视频世界模型，支持实时用户控制相机轨迹并生成连贯视频。这属于视频世界模型（video_world_model）的典型工作，同时涉及 3D 高斯场景表示与视频渲染，也匹配多模态生成方向。

Importance reason: Directly advances interactive video world models from single images with real-time 8 FPS, aligning with top-priority world model and video generation subtopics. Signals: world model, video generation, real-time 8 FPS, single-image to 360 scene, autoregressive video renderer

Abstract: We present MoVerse, a real-time video world model that creates an interactively navigable scene from a single narrow-field-of-view image. This setting is challenging because the input observes only a small fraction of the environment, while interactive roaming requires a complete surrounding world, persistent geometry, controllable camera motion, and temporally coherent high-fidelity observations. MoVerse addresses this problem by separating world construction from observation rendering. It first expands the input into a gravity-aligned 360$^\circ$ panorama with topology-aware diffusion, closing the missing field of view before 3D reasoning. It then lifts the panorama into a persistent 3D Gaussian scaffold using panoramic geometry-aware residual prediction, yielding a dense and directly renderable spatial memory. Finally, a Gaussian-conditioned video renderer translates scaffold renderings along user-specified camera trajectories into photorealistic video. To make this renderer practical for interaction, we train a bidirectional diffusion teacher for high-quality conditional rendering and distill it into a causal autoregressive student for bounded-latency streaming. This design combines the controllability and long-range consistency of explicit 3D representations with the perceptual quality of generative video models. MoVerse supports real-time scene roaming at 8~FPS on a single NVIDIA RTX~4090 GPU, demonstrating a practical path toward single-image world creation with interactive video output.

</details>


### [28] [EPM-JEPA: Operator-Side Experience Modulation in JEPA-Family World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12979)
*Vedant Pandya*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 值得关注 (79.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文比较了两种在 JEPA 世界模型中融入累积经验以应对测试时分布偏移的机制：操作数侧注入（EI-JEPA）和算子侧调制（EPM-JEPA，通过 LoRA 生成权重增量）。在 Moving MNIST 重力偏移设定下，EPM-JEPA 相比无记忆基线提升 1.90%，而 EI-JEPA 反而低于基线。主要贡献是机制分析：发现 D_shift 轨迹由缓冲循环、EMA 目标漂移和 LoRA 固有暂态三个独立动力学过程组成，而非收敛到平衡态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA 系列世界模型使用静态预测器，当测试时动态分布与训练分布不一致时，预测器权重无法自适应调整。现有方法缺乏在测试阶段利用累积经验来适应分布偏移的机制。

Method: 提出两种经验调制机制：(1) EI-JEPA：将压缩的经验表示作为残差注入到预测器的隐藏状态中（操作数侧注入）；(2) EPM-JEPA：通过 LoRA 将相同经验表示生成低秩权重增量，调制预测器权重（算子侧调制）。在 Moving MNIST 重力偏移设定下进行预注册比较实验。

Result: EPM-JEPA (D_shift^{n=50}=0.7848±0.0078) 与 EI-JEPA (0.8238) 差异为 4.74%，按预定标准为无效结果。作为非预注册的次要观察，EPM-JEPA 相比无记忆基线 (0.8000) 提升 1.90%，且跨种子一致，而 EI-JEPA 低于基线。机制分析发现 D_shift 轨迹反映三个独立动力学过程：缓冲循环、EMA 目标漂移和 LoRA 固有暂态（+0.021）。

Conclusion: 算子侧（权重级）调制比操作数侧（隐藏状态级）注入更有效，但 LoRA 引入的暂态峰值限制了性能。这些发现启发提出 PEM-JEPA，一种基于物理的改进方案以解决动力学峰值限制。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 JEPA 系列世界模型在测试时分布偏移下的经验调制机制，属于世界模型方向。虽然论文使用 Moving MNIST 视频数据，但其核心关注的是世界模型的预测器适应性问题（视频预测中的分布偏移），与 [CANONICAL] 视频世界模型（video_world_model）主题高度吻合。

Importance reason: Novel mechanism analysis of JEPA world models under distribution shift, but limited to synthetic Moving MNIST and lacks direct connection to priority subtopics. Signals: JEPA world model, operator-side modulation, LoRA weight modulation, distribution shift analysis, Moving MNIST gravity shift

Abstract: JEPA-family world models use a static predictor whose weights do not adapt when test-time dynamics diverge from training. We compare two mechanisms for incorporating accumulated experience into a JEPA predictor under distribution shift: operand-side injection, where a compressed experience representation is added as a residual to the predictor's hidden state (EI-JEPA), and operator-side modulation, where the same representation generates low-rank weight deltas via LoRA applied to the predictor's weights (EPM-JEPA). On a pre-registered comparison (Moving MNIST, gravity shift), EPM-JEPA (D_shift^{n=50} = 0.7848 +/- 0.0078, three seeds) differs from EI-JEPA (0.8238) by delta = 4.74% - Outcome C: a null result - by our stated criterion, a valid outcome. As a secondary, non-pre-registered observation, EPM-JEPA improves 1.90% over a no-memory baseline (0.8000), consistently across seeds, while EI-JEPA underperforms the baseline, indicating the benefit is specific to weight-level modulation. Our primary contribution is a mechanism analysis: the D_shift^{n=50} trajectory reflects three independent dynamical processes - buffer cycling, EMA target drift, and an intrinsic LoRA settling transient of +0.021 - rather than convergence to equilibrium. These findings motivate PEM-JEPA, a physics-grounded successor addressing this dynamical-peak limitation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧抓取生成器通常将接触力作为下游验证步骤，导致运动学上可行的抓取姿态仍可能违反物理稳定抓取条件。本文旨在将接触力直接纳入生成过程，确保生成的抓取在物理上稳定且满足摩擦约束。

Method: 提出 EquiDexFlow，一个 SE(3) 等变流匹配模型。模型以物体点云为输入，通过等变网络联合预测手腕姿态、关节角度、指尖接触点、表面法向量和接触力。架构通过将接触点投影到物体表面并将力约束在库仑摩擦锥内，确保放置和摩擦合规性。训练使用 81 个物体上的 8,100 个力闭合抓取数据，针对 16-DoF Allegro Hand。解码后的指尖接触通过每指逆运动学重定向到 16-DoF LEAP Hand。

Result: 模型在 200 次旋转上验证了端到端 SE(3) 等变性，手腕残差低于 0.04°，关节偏差为零。在所有消融变体中实现零摩擦违规、最佳综合分数和最低扳手残差。硬件可行性优化使每个关节至少在其执行器包络内 5%。物理机器人上，重定向的 EquiDexFlow 解码抓取在所有六个测试物体上成功完成开环抓取-保持试验，每个非对称物体在标准姿态和 120° 共旋转下均成功。

Conclusion: 本文证明了将接触力直接纳入生成过程可以显著提高灵巧抓取的物理稳定性和可迁移性。SE(3) 等变流匹配模型能够生成满足摩擦约束和力闭合条件的抓取，且对物体姿态变化具有鲁棒性，为灵巧操作提供了新的生成范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于SE(3)等变流匹配的灵巧抓取生成模型，从点云预测抓取姿态、接触点与力，属于3D物体交互与抓取生成，映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation），该子主题涵盖3D物体/场景生成与神经渲染，灵巧抓取生成可视为3D物体交互生成的一个子方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on dexterous grasping with equivariant flow matching, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords apply. Signals: SE(3)-equivariant flow matching, dexterous grasp generation, contact force prediction, Coulomb friction cone, physical robot validation

Abstract: Most learned dexterous grasp generators relegate contact forces to a downstream verification step, so a kinematically-plausible pose can still violate the conditions for a stable physical grasp. We address this with EquiDexFlow, an SE(3)-equivariant flow-matching model that jointly predicts wrist pose, joint angles, fingertip contacts, surface normals, and contact forces from an object point cloud. Our architecture projects contacts onto the object surface and forces into the Coulomb friction cone by construction, so placement and friction compliance hold without loss penalties. We prove end-to-end SE(3) equivariance and verify it empirically over 200 rotations, with wrist residuals below $0.04^\circ$ and exactly zero joint deviation. Trained on 8,100 force-closure grasps across 81 objects for the 16-DoF Allegro Hand, our model achieves zero friction violations, the best composite score, and the lowest wrench residual among all ablation variants. We retarget decoded fingertip contacts to a 16-DoF LEAP Hand via per-finger inverse kinematics, and our hardware-feasible refinement places every joint at least 5% inside its actuator envelope while preserving wrench balance. On the physical robot, retargeted EquiDexFlow-decoded grasps complete open-loop pick-and-hold trials on all six test objects, with every asymmetric object succeeding at both the canonical pose and a $120^\circ$ co-rotation. Videos, code, and checkpoints are available at .

</details>

*Gabriel Steele, Alzahra Altalib, Alessandro Perelli*

Main category: physics.med-ph, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成
