TL;DR: 提出 Dual-Domain Equivariant GAN (DDE-GAN)，在空间域和频域联合学习，并引入旋转等变性损失，用于 CT-PET 多模态医学图像合成，在 HECKTOR 2022 数据集上取得优于基线模型的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于 GAN 的 CT-PET 图像合成方法仅在空间域操作，忽略了几何一致性（如旋转不变性），导致结构保真度有限。本文旨在通过联合空间域和频域学习以及引入旋转等变性来提升多模态医学图像合成的质量和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出 DDE-GAN，包含生成器和判别器，在空间域和频域（傅里叶域）联合学习以捕获互补的解剖和频谱信息。将 CT 和 PET 物理测量中固有的旋转等变性嵌入到生成器和判别器的损失函数中，确保在旋转下输出一致。采用分层双域训练策略，通过多阶段损失函数强制域内和域间一致性。

Result: 在 HECKTOR 2022 CT-PET 数据集上，DDE-GAN 在 CT-PET 图像合成质量上优于基线模型，结合双域学习与几何等变性显著提升了多模态图像合成的准确性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 双域学习与几何等变性的结合能有效提升多模态医学图像合成质量，该方法在 PET 补全和数据增强等实际应用中具有潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CT-PET 多模态医学图像合成方法，属于多模态生成方向。虽然具体应用是医学图像，但核心任务是跨模态图像生成（CT→PET），与 3D 生成与重建中的多模态/跨模态图像合成任务高度相关。该论文使用 GAN 进行跨模态图像合成，可归入 3D 生成与重建（医学图像体积数据本质上是 3D 的）。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical CT-PET synthesis with equivariance, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative-keyword-listed. Signals: medical imaging, CT-PET synthesis, dual-domain GAN, rotational equivariance, HECKTOR dataset

Abstract: We present a Dual-Domain Equivariant Generative Adversarial Network (DDE-GAN) for multimodal CT-PET image synthesis. Traditional GAN-based approaches often operate solely in the spatial domain and ignore geometric consistency, resulting in limited structural fidelity. DDE-GAN addresses these challenges by jointly learning from both spatial and frequency (Fourier) domains, capturing complementary anatomical and spectral information. Furthermore, rotational equivariance embedded in the physics of the CT and PET measurements are integrated into the loss of both the generator and discriminator to ensure consistent responses under rotations, improving anatomical accuracy. A hierarchical dual-domain training strategy enforces intra- and inter-domain consistency through multi-stage loss functions. Evaluated on the HECKTOR 2022 CT-PET dataset, DDE-GAN achieves superior synthesis quality over baseline models for CT-PET image synthesis. The results demonstrate that combining dual-domain learning with geometric equivariance substantially enhances multimodal image synthesis accuracy and robustness, enabling practical applications in PET completion and data augmentation.

</details>


### [31] [Surflo: Consistent 3D Surface Flow Model with Global State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13644)
*Antoine Guédon, Shu Nakamura, Nicolas Dufour, Jiahui Lei, Ko Nishino, Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 高优先级 (92.35) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Surflo 提出一种前馈式 3D 表面重建模型，将任意数量未标定 RGB 视图压缩为 K 个全局潜变量 token，再通过 flow matching 将噪声点独立传输到 3D 表面，支持从数千到百万点的任意分辨率解码，并引入推理时梯度引导来抑制局部不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈 3D 重建方法存在局限：逐视角方法输出重叠且未对齐的点云，随输入视图数线性增长；全局潜变量方法则受限于固定低分辨率输出。本文希望实现一种既能利用全局状态又能支持任意分辨率解码的一致 3D 表面重建方法。

Method: Surflo 将可变数量的未标定 RGB 视图通过 Transformer 编码器压缩为 K 个全局潜变量 token（全局状态），然后通过 flow matching 将噪声点独立地从噪声分布传输到 3D 表面。解码过程不受固定网格或 token 预算限制，同一潜变量可在单次前向中生成从数千到百万个点。为抑制独立逐点解码带来的局部不一致性，在 ODE 积分过程中注入光度梯度作为推理时引导项，使相邻点相互关联。

Result: Surflo 在表面指标上匹配或超越前馈基线方法，运行速度比需要数百视图的优化方法快一个数量级，并且是唯一将全局潜变量与任意分辨率解码相结合的前馈方法。

Conclusion: Surflo 通过全局潜变量与 flow matching 解码的结合，实现了高效、一致且分辨率无关的 3D 表面重建，为前馈式 3D 重建提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Surflo，一种从多视图 RGB 图像通过 flow matching 解码 3D 表面点的前馈重建方法，属于 3D 场景/表面生成与重建范畴，与 canonical subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with continuous multimodal representation and flow matching for 3D surface decoding, a key priority subtopic. Signals: flow matching for 3D surface, global latent token compression, arbitrary-resolution decoding, inference-time gradient guidance, feed-forward 3D reconstruction

Abstract: Geometry is invariant to viewpoint, which makes any collection of images a redundant encoding of a single 3D state. Existing feed-forward reconstruction models fail to exploit this: per-view methods emit overlapping, unaligned pointmaps that grow linearly with input count, while global-latent methods commit to a fixed, low-resolution output. We introduce Surflo, which compresses a variable number of unposed RGB views into K latent tokens-one global state-and decodes oriented 3D surface points by independently transporting them from noise onto the surface via flow matching. This frees the output from any fixed grid or token budget: the same latent yields from a few thousand to a million points in a single forward pass. To suppress the local inconsistencies inherent to independent per-point decoding, an inference-time guidance term correlates nearby points by injecting a photometric gradient during ODE integration. Surflo matches or surpasses feed-forward baselines on surface metrics, runs an order of magnitude faster than optimization-based methods that require hundreds of views, and is the only feed-forward approach to combine a global latent with arbitrary-resolution decoding.

</details>


### [32] [World Tracing: Generative Pixel-Aligned Geometry Beyond the Visible](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13652)
*Hao Zhang, Mohamed El Banani, Jen-Hao Cheng, Paul Zhang, Yi Hua, Ben Mildenhall, Christoph Lassner, Narendra Ahuja, Gengshan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.GR

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 高优先级 (92.93) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: World Tracing 提出一种生成式像素对齐几何表示，为每个输入像素预测有序的 3D 点堆栈（可见表面+遮挡表面），并基于扩散 Transformer (WT-DiT) 和像素空间 flow matching 训练，在物体、场景和动态基准上同时实现了高保真可见表面重建和完整的遮挡几何生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像到 3D 方法在忠实度与完整性之间存在权衡：深度估计器锚定于输入像素但止步于可见表面，而图像到 3D 模型能生成完整形状但常与输入像素不对齐。作者希望同时实现像素对齐的可见表面重建和遮挡几何的完整生成。

Method: 提出 World Tracing 表示：为每个输入像素预测一个有序的相机空间 3D 点堆栈，第一层为可见表面，后续层为从前到后的遮挡表面交点。实例化为 WT-DiT（World-Tracing Diffusion Transformer），将多层几何视为独立的去噪 token，通过分解注意力和全局注意力耦合。采用像素空间 flow matching 训练，并设计混合噪声调度以平衡可见表面重建与遮挡几何生成。

Result: 在物体、场景和动态基准上，World Tracing 在可见表面重建和完整几何生成方面均优于深度估计器和图像到 3D 生成器。同时保持了 2D 到 3D 的对应关系，支持文本驱动的 3D 场景编辑、几何条件的新视角视频合成以及与纹理网格生成器的免训练集成。

Conclusion: World Tracing 通过生成式像素对齐几何表示，成功弥合了深度估计（像素对齐但仅可见表面）与图像到 3D 生成（完整但不对齐）之间的差距，为 3D 场景编辑、新视角合成等下游应用提供了统一的几何基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 World Tracing，一种生成式像素对齐几何表示，从单张图像预测可见表面及遮挡区域的 3D 点云，属于图像到 3D 生成与重建任务，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 3D 生成与重建 (three_d_generation) 子主题。

Importance reason: Novel pixel-aligned generative geometry with diffusion transformer and flow matching directly advances multimodal generation and continuous visual token representations. Signals: generative pixel-aligned geometry, diffusion transformer (WT-DiT), pixel-space flow matching, occluded geometry completion, text-driven 3D editing

Abstract: Image-to-3D methods often trade off faithfulness and completeness: depth estimators are anchored to input pixels but stop at the visible surface, while image-to-3D models generate complete shapes that are often misaligned with the input. We introduce World Tracing, a generative pixel-aligned geometry representation that predicts 3D points aligned with observed pixels while completing geometry beyond the visible surface. For each input pixel, World Tracing predicts an ordered stack of camera-space 3D points, where the first layer represents the visible surface and subsequent layers represent front-to-back intersections with occluded surfaces. We instantiate this representation with a world-tracing diffusion transformer, WT-DiT, which treats multiple geometry layers as separate denoising tokens coupled through factorized and global attention. WT-DiT is trained with pixel-space flow matching and a mixed noise schedule that balances visible-surface reconstruction with occluded-geometry generation. World Tracing achieves strong performance on visible-surface reconstruction and complete geometry generation across object, scene, and dynamic benchmarks, outperforming both depth predictors and image-to-3D generators. It also preserves 2D-to-3D correspondence, enabling text-driven 3D scene editing, geometry-conditioned novel-view video synthesis, and training-free integration with textured-mesh generators.

</details>


### [33] [IterCAD: An Iterative Multimodal Agent for Visually-Grounded CAD Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13368)
*Tao Hu, Jiaxin Ai, Licheng Wen, Xueheng Li, Shu Zou, Siqi Li, Nianchen Deng, Xinyu Cai, Hongbin Zhou, Pinlong Cai, Daocheng Fu, Yu Yang, Hairong Zhang, Botian Shi, Xuemeng Yang*

Importance: 值得关注 (71.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: IterCAD 是一个统一的多模态智能体框架，通过多轮交互实现闭环的 CAD 生成与编辑。它包含 Drawing-to-Code、Text-to-Code 和 Interactive Editing 三个任务，利用数据合成流水线生成标准工程图纸和编辑轨迹，并通过渐进式 SFT 和几何感知强化学习优化代码可执行性与几何精度。同时提出 IterCAD-Bench 评测套件和 CD-TR 曲线/AUC-TR 指标，在多个基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化 CAD 方法主要采用开环、一次性生成的方式，与真实世界中迭代式、交互式的 CAD 设计实践不匹配。缺乏能够进行闭环交互、支持多轮编辑和几何精确生成的统一框架。

Method: 1) 将 CAD 生成与编辑形式化为多模态智能体与可执行 CAD 沙箱之间的多轮交互任务；2) 开发数据合成流水线，生成符合工业标准的多视图工程图纸、复杂代码编辑任务和高保真交互轨迹；3) 通过渐进式 SFT（监督微调）结合几何感知强化学习（viable-prefix masking）优化智能体，提升代码可执行性和几何保真度；4) 提出 IterCAD-Bench 评测套件和 Chamfer Distance Tolerance-Recall (CD-TR) 曲线及 AUC-TR 指标。

Result: IterCAD 在多个基准上取得了极具竞争力的性能，在代码可执行性和几何精度方面显著优于现有方法，并展现出优越的闭环迭代精化能力。

Conclusion: IterCAD 证明了闭环交互式多模态智能体在 CAD 生成与编辑任务中的有效性，为自动化 CAD 设计提供了更贴近实际工程实践的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 IterCAD，一个用于 CAD（计算机辅助设计）生成与编辑的多模态智能体框架，属于 3D 生成与重建范畴。虽然 CAD 生成是 3D 生成的一个具体应用方向，但 [CANONICAL] 的 "3D 生成与重建" 主题足够宽泛，可以覆盖 CAD 生成与编辑工作。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D CAD generation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks keywords from the boost list. Signals: 3D generation, CAD, multimodal agent, iterative editing, reinforcement learning

Abstract: Computer-Aided Design is pivotal in modern manufacturing, yet existing automated methods predominantly rely on open-loop, one-shot generation, creating a mismatch with iterative real-world practices. In this paper, we present IterCAD, a unified multimodal agent framework for closed-loop, interactive CAD generation and editing. We formulate the task as a multi-turn interaction between a multimodal agent and an executable CAD sandbox, covering three tasks: Drawing-to-Code, Text-to-Code, and Interactive Editing. To support this, we develop a data synthesis pipeline incorporating advanced industrial manufacturing features to generate standard-compliant multi-view engineering drawings, complex code-editing tasks, and high-fidelity interaction trajectories. We optimize the agent via progressive SFT followed by geometry-aware reinforcement learning with viable-prefix masking to enhance code executability and geometric fidelity. Finally, we introduce the IterCAD-Bench evaluation suite and propose the Chamfer Distance Tolerance-Recall (CD-TR) curve alongside its AUC-TR metric, establishing a survivor-bias-free standard that unifies code validity and geometric precision. Extensive experiments demonstrate that IterCAD achieves highly competitive performance across multiple benchmarks, significantly outperforming existing approaches in both code executability and geometric precision, while exhibiting superior capabilities in closed-loop iterative refinement.

</details>


### [34] [DeepJEB++: Foundation Model-Driven Large-Scale 3D Engineering Dataset via 2D Latent Space Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12994)
*Soyoung Yoo, Leekyo Jeong, Jinsu Ra, Dongeon Lee, Sunwoong Yang, Hyogu Jeong, Namwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CE, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DeepJEB++ 是一个基于基础模型的数据增强框架，通过 2D 潜空间扩散模型合成多视角图像，再提升为 3D 网格，并自动进行有限元仿真标注，将少量种子 3D 设计扩展为大规模带仿真标签的 3D 数据集（40 倍扩展）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的工程设计中缺乏大规模带物理性能标签的 3D 数据集，现有 3D 数据增强方法难以保持细微几何变化，且自动化仿真标注困难。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) 在预训练 2D 潜扩散模型上微调多视角渲染图，通过潜空间插值合成新视图，并用 VLM 质量过滤器保留可制造设计；(2) 用领域自适应生成基础模型将验证后的图像提升为 3D 网格；(3) 自动流水线识别每个网格上的载荷和螺栓接口，分配有限元标签（质量、应力、位移）。

Result: 从不到 400 个种子设计出发，在单 GPU 每阶段条件下生成 15,360 个带仿真标签的 3D 支架（40 倍扩展），并在可制造性、标签保真度和分布一致性三个维度上验证了增强质量。

Conclusion: DeepJEB++ 证明了通过 2D 潜空间增强再提升到 3D 的范式可以有效扩展工程 3D 数据集，为数据驱动的工程 AI 研究提供了可复现的大规模资源。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DeepJEB++ 框架，利用 2D 潜空间扩散模型增强生成 3D 网格（mesh），属于 3D 物体生成范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Uses 2D latent diffusion for 3D data augmentation, but focuses on engineering design rather than core multimodal generation or world models. Signals: 2D latent space augmentation, 3D dataset generation, VLM quality filter, finite element labeling, engineering design focus

Abstract: Data-driven engineering design is constrained by the lack of large-scale 3D datasets that pair geometry with physics-based performance labels. In particular, existing 3D data augmentation techniques have limitations in preserving subtle and diverse geometric variations, and it remains difficult to automate the subsequent simulation-labeling process, where boundary conditions vary depending on the generated geometry. We present DeepJEB++, a foundation-model-driven data-augmentation framework that expands a small seed set of jet engine brackets into a large, simulation-labeled 3D dataset under constrained resources. Our key idea is to augment in the data-rich 2D latent space, then transfer to 3D. In Stage 1, we fine-tune a pretrained 2D latent diffusion model on multi-view renders and synthesize novel views by latent interpolation, retaining manufacturable designs through a vision-language-model (VLM) quality filter. In Stage 2, the validated images are lifted to 3D meshes by a domain-adapted generative foundation model. In Stage 3, an automated pipeline recognizes the load and bolt interfaces on each mesh and assigns finite-element labels -- mass, stress, and displacement -- without manual intervention. We assess augmentation quality along three intrinsic axes: manufacturability, label fidelity against the SimJEB ground truth, and distributional consistency. Starting from fewer than 400 seed designs, DeepJEB++ yields 15,360 simulation-labeled 3D brackets -- a 40x expansion -- using a single GPU per stage. The dataset will be made publicly available to support reproducible engineering-AI research.

</details>

*Jen-Hao Cheng, Yipeng Wang, Hao Zhang, Gengshan Yang, Jenq-Neng Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.GR

TL;DR: Flex4DHuman 是一个多视角视频扩散模型，利用相对相机位姿条件，将单目或稀疏多视角视频转换为密集同步多视角视频，结合4D高斯泼溅实现动态4D人体重建，无需显式几何先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单目视频进行动态4D人体重建具有广泛应用前景，但现有方法依赖骨架、深度图、法线等显式几何先验，限制了泛化性和可扩展性。作者希望提出一种无需几何先验、仅需相对相机位姿条件的方法，实现从日常单目视频到4D内容的可扩展重建。

Method: 基于 Wan 2.1 1.3B 文生视频模型，通过五轴位置编码（扩展时空RoPE加入视角索引和连续SE(3)相对相机几何）编码相机和视角信息。采用三阶段课程训练：位姿跟随、灵活参考到目标视角生成、时序展开。训练中使用干净的历史目标视角token支持时序展开，并加入多视角描述实现测试时文本控制。最后结合现成的4D高斯泼溅管线将单目静态相机视频提升为动态4D高斯泼溅。

Result: 在 DNA-Rendering 和 ActorsHQ 数据集上超越先前最先进方法，且混合人类-动物训练后可泛化到动物类别。

Conclusion: Flex4DHuman 为从日常单目视频进行可扩展4D内容创作提供了实用方案，适用于仿真、游戏、AR/VR和视频重拍等场景。

Classification reason: 该论文提出多视角视频扩散模型，从单目/稀疏多视角视频生成密集多视角视频，并用于4D高斯泼溅重建，属于动态4D场景生成与重建范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "4D 与动态场景生成"（dynamic_4d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong 4D human reconstruction from monocular video using diffusion and Gaussian splatting, but lacks direct connection to priority continuous language/multimodal models or autoregressive generation. Signals: 4D human reconstruction, multi-view video diffusion, relative camera-pose conditioning, 4D Gaussian splatting, no explicit geometry priors

Abstract: We present Flex4DHuman, a multi-view video diffusion model that transforms a monocular or sparse multi-view video of a dynamic subject into synchronized dense multi-view videos using only relative camera-pose conditioning. Unlike prior human-centric methods that rely on skeletons, depth maps, normals, or rendered target-view geometry, Flex4DHuman requires no explicit geometry priors and instead conditions generation through relative camera-pose positional encoding. The generated videos can be directly ingested by downstream reconstruction pipelines to create dynamic 4D Gaussian splats. Built on the Wan 2.1 1.3B text-to-video model, Flex4DHuman preserves the backbone architecture and encodes camera and view information through a five-axis positional encoding that extends spatio-temporal RoPE with view indices and continuous SE(3) relative camera geometry. A three-stage curriculum progressively trains the model for pose following, flexible reference-to-target view generation, and temporal rollout. To support temporal rollout, we train with clean historical target-view tokens. We also add multi-view captions to enable test-time text control. Combined with an off-the-shelf 4D Gaussian Splatting stage, our framework lifts monocular static-camera videos into dynamic 4D Gaussian splats. Experiments on DNA-Rendering and ActorsHQ show that Flex4DHuman surpasses prior state-of-the-art methods, while the same formulation generalizes to animal categories after mixed human-animal training. These capabilities make Flex4DHuman a practical step toward scalable 4D content creation from casual monocular videos for simulation, gaming, AR/VR, and video re-shooting.

</details>

*Xiangyu Lyu, Dan Lei*

Main category: cs.MM, cs.CV, cs.GR

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

TL;DR: SeamEdit 是一种无需训练、模型无关的流水线，将任意具备inpainting能力的VLM作为黑盒oracle，通过五阶段后处理（tile分解、黑盒inpainting、几何与颜色一致性校正、多候选排序、动态规划曲线拼缝融合）实现大图像的无缝语义编辑，有效消除语义变形、画面对齐漂移和可见拼缝伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大图像的语义区域编辑需要同时满足高生成质量和与周围内容的自然融合。现有方法依赖白盒模型，未能充分利用闭源模型的强大生成能力；而直接对闭源模型进行分块编辑会导致语义变形、画面对齐漂移和可见拼缝伪影等问题。

Method: SeamEdit 提出五阶段后处理流水线：(1) 基于覆盖的tile分解，将大图像划分为重叠tile；(2) 黑盒VLM inpainting，对每个tile独立调用VLM进行语义编辑；(3) 几何与颜色一致性校正，修正tile间的对齐和色彩差异；(4) 基于拼缝风险的多候选排序，选择最优编辑结果；(5) 动态规划曲线拼缝融合，沿非直线路径融合tile边界以消除可见拼缝。

Result: 实验表明SeamEdit在多种VLM（如SDXL、DALL-E等）上均能有效减少拼缝可见性，支持任意tile区域的语义修改，在生成质量和融合自然度上优于直接分块编辑的基线方法。

Conclusion: SeamEdit提供了一种训练无关、模型无关的大图像语义编辑方案，通过后处理流水线有效解决了黑盒VLM分块编辑中的关键问题，为利用闭源模型进行高质量大图像编辑提供了实用框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一种基于黑盒VLM的大图像语义区域编辑流水线，核心任务是图像编辑（inpainting），属于多模态生成方向下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）。同时该工作利用了VLM的inpainting能力，也与多模态理解与生成方向相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on black-box VLM inpainting for large-image editing, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous language models, autoregressive generation, or world models. Signals: training-free pipeline, black-box VLM inpainting, large-image semantic editing, seam artifact removal, model-agnostic

Abstract: Semantic region editing for large images must satisfy two requirements at the same time: high generative quality and natural integration with surrounding content. Some related methods rely on white-box models and leave the strong generation capability of closed-source models underexplored. Directly applying closed-source models to tiled editing, however, introduces several failure modes: semantic deformation, canvas-level alignment drift, and visible seam artifacts. This paper presents SeamEdit, a training-free and model-agnostic pipeline that treats any VLM with inpainting capability as a black-box oracle. SeamEdit mitigates these issues through a five-stage post-hoc pipeline: overlay-based tile decomposition, black-box VLM inpainting, geometric and color-consistency correction, seam-risk-based multi-candidate ranking, and dynamic-programming curved seam fusion. The pipeline reduces seam visibility and supports semantic modification of arbitrary tile regions.

</details>


### [37] [Detect, Remask, Repair: Diffusion Editing for Faithful Summarization of Evolving Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12807)
*Hao Zou, Zachary Horvitz, Chandhru Karthick, Zhou Yu, Kathleen McKeown*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 值得关注 (75.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 DETECT-REMASK-REPAIR 框架，利用掩码扩散语言模型检测、重掩码并修复过时的摘要片段，实现局部忠实性修复。同时引入 StreamSum 基准用于评估演化上下文摘要。实验表明该方法在忠实性、速度和保留内容之间提供了可控权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界事件的摘要会随着上下文演变和新信息到来而过时。传统做法是重新生成完整摘要，但这会丢弃已有草稿、掩盖变化内容，且当只有少数声明不再被支持时显得不必要。因此需要一种局部修复方法，仅更新过时片段而保留已支持的内容。

Method: 提出 DETECT-REMASK-REPAIR 三阶段框架：(1) Detect：利用自然语言推理（NLI）模型识别摘要中与更新后上下文不一致的过时片段；(2) Remask：将检测到的过时片段替换为掩码 token；(3) Repair：使用掩码扩散语言模型（如 MDLM）对掩码区域进行条件生成修复。该方法支持多种修复策略（忠实性导向、一步修复等），可在忠实性、速度和内容保留之间进行权衡。

Result: 在 DialogSum 和 StreamSum 基准上，局部扩散修复相比完整重写提供了可控的替代方案。忠实性导向修复能改善早期草稿，一步修复将修复成本降至半秒以下。框架还能作为后处理步骤提升自回归系统的忠实性。

Conclusion: 局部扩散修复是演化上下文摘要中一种有效且高效的方法，能够在忠实性、速度和内容保留之间灵活权衡。该方法还可作为自回归摘要系统的后处理校正步骤，提升其忠实性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于扩散模型的文本摘要局部忠实性修复方法（Detect-Remask-Repair），属于文本编辑/修复任务。虽然论文主要面向文本摘要而非图像视频，但"图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）是现有最接近的[CANONICAL]子主题，涵盖编辑与修复范式。该论文的"remask-repair"思想与扩散模型编辑框架高度对应。

Importance reason: Focuses on text summarization faithfulness repair, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: diffusion language model, faithfulness repair, evolving context summarization, not multimodal generation, no video/image generation

Abstract: Summaries of real-world events can become outdated as contexts evolve and new information arrives. A common response is to generate a new summary from the updated context, but full regeneration discards the previous draft, can obscure what changed, and may be unnecessary when only a few claims are unsupported. We study localized faithfulness repair: updating outdated spans in an existing summary while preserving supported content. We propose DETECT-REMASK-REPAIR, a diffusion-based framework that identifies, remasks, and repairs outdated regions with masked diffusion language models. To evaluate evolving-context summarization, we introduce StreamSum, a benchmark of synthetic event timelines. Experiments on DialogSum and StreamSum show that localized diffusion repair provides a controllable alternative to full rewriting: faithfulness-steered repair improves early drafts, one-step repair reduces repair cost to under half a second, with the framework enabling faithfulness-speed-preservation tradeoffs across datasets. We also find that the framework can provide a post-hoc correction step that improves faithfulness for autoregressive systems.

</details>


### [38] [DuET: Dual Expert Trajectories for Diffusion Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13303)
*Lidia Troeshestova, Alexander Ustyuzhanin, Sergey Kastryulin*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

TL;DR: DuET 是一种无需训练的扩散图像编辑推理方法，通过在去噪过程中临时切换到文生图阶段再切回编辑模式，放松源图像条件约束，使编辑结果更自然、指令相关性更高，同时保持图像条件编辑的结构优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于指令的扩散图像编辑器在每一步去噪中都强条件于源图像，当目标场景与输入差异较大时，编辑执行不充分且结果不自然。作者希望在不修改模型权重或增加采样成本的前提下，提升编辑的指令相关性和感知质量。

Method: DuET 是一种训练自由的推理方法。在去噪过程中，它暂时放松源图像条件约束，通过一个"文生图阶段"过渡，使去噪轨迹向目标分布移动，之后再切回编辑模式，从而兼顾编辑效果与源图像结构保留。

Result: 在多个模型和基准上，DuET 一致提升了指令相关性、语义忠实度和感知质量。部分情况下源图像保留略有下降，揭示了源保留与编辑忠实度之间的可预测权衡。

Conclusion: DuET 提供了一种简单有效的训练免费方法，通过动态调整条件强度来改善扩散图像编辑效果，揭示了源图像保留与编辑质量之间的权衡关系。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一种无需训练的扩散图像编辑方法 DuET，属于图像编辑方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "visual_editing (图像与视频编辑)" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on diffusion image editing, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's high-weight continuous language/multimodal or video generation areas. Signals: Training-free inference, diffusion image editing, source-image conditioning trade-off, improves edit fidelity, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Recent diffusion editors perform diverse instruction-based edits while conditioning on the source image at every denoising step. Yet persistent source-image conditioning can limit how fully an edit is executed and how natural the result appears, especially when the target scene diverges substantially from the input. We introduce DuET (Dual Expert Trajectories), a training-free inference method that temporarily relaxes source-image conditioning by transitioning through a text-to-image phase before returning to edit mode, allowing the denoising trajectory to move toward the target distribution while retaining the structural benefits of image-conditioned editing. Without modifying model weights or increasing sampling cost, DuET consistently improves instruction relevance, semantic fidelity, and perceptual quality across diverse models and benchmarks. In some cases, these gains come with a modest reduction in source-image preservation, revealing a predictable trade-off between source preservation and edit fidelity.

</details>

TL;DR: JointEdit3D 提出了一种前馈式3D场景编辑框架，在统一的RGB-几何重建生成潜空间中进行非对称潜空间修补，仅需单张编辑后的RGB参考图即可生成其余RGB视图和编辑后的几何潜变量，并引入SceneAnchor分支和编辑/背景感知损失来保持结构一致性。同时构建了SceneEdit3D-15K数据集和SceneEdit3D-Bench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景编辑方法通常依赖逐场景优化或级联的编辑-重建流程，导致测试时成本高、3D感知能力有限且结构不一致。缺乏统一的前馈式3D编辑框架以及标准化的3D场景编辑评测资源。

Method: 基于统一的RGB-几何重建生成潜空间，采用非对称潜空间修补策略：仅输入单张编辑后的RGB参考潜变量，生成其余RGB视图和编辑后的几何潜变量，并通过源场景锚定（source-scene anchoring）保持结构一致性。设计了SceneAnchor分支注入源场景结构信息，并采用编辑/背景感知损失平衡编辑区域保真度与未编辑内容保持。

Result: 在SceneEdit3D-Bench上，JointEdit3D在编辑区域质量和3D结构完整性上优于现有基线方法，同时保持了有竞争力的背景保持效果。

Conclusion: JointEdit3D证明了在统一潜空间中进行前馈式3D场景编辑的可行性，为3D编辑提供了一种高效且结构一致的新范式，同时贡献了标准化评测数据集和基准。

Classification reason: 该论文研究3D场景编辑（feed-forward 3D scene editing），属于图像与视频编辑（visual_editing）范畴下的3D编辑任务。虽然现有 canonical subtopic "visual_editing" 主要涵盖2D图像/视频编辑，但3D场景编辑是其自然延伸，且该 subtopic 已有50篇论文覆盖广泛编辑任务，是最佳匹配。

Importance reason: 3D场景编辑与用户核心方向（连续多模态模型、视频生成世界模型）关联较弱，且依赖单张编辑图而非连续潜空间推理。 Signals: 前馈式3D编辑, 统一潜空间, 非对称潜空间修补, 场景锚定分支, 编辑/背景感知损失

Abstract: Existing 3D scene editing methods typically rely on per-scene optimization over explicit 3D representations or cascaded edit-and-reconstruct pipelines, resulting in high test-time cost, limited 3D awareness, and structural inconsistencies. To couple appearance synthesis and geometry prediction during editing, we build on a unified RGB-geometry reconstruction-generation latent space and adapt it to feed-forward 3D scene editing. The resulting framework, \textbf{JointEdit3D}, performs asymmetric latent inpainting by observing only a single edited RGB reference latent and generating the remaining RGB views and edited geometry latent under source-scene anchoring. JointEdit3D introduces a dedicated SceneAnchor Branch to inject source-scene structure without forcing direct copying, and adopts edit/background-aware losses to balance edited-region fidelity with unedited-content preservation. To address the lack of paired resources for standardized 3D scene editing evaluation, we introduce SceneEdit3D-15K, a dataset with 15K paired editing samples and renderer-provided 3D annotations, together with SceneEdit3D-Bench, a curated 100-sample benchmark. Experiments show that JointEdit3D improves edited-region quality and 3D structural completeness over prior baselines while maintaining competitive background preservation.

</details>


### [40] [SmartFont: Dynamic Condition Allocation for Few-Shot Font Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13382)
*Zian Yang, Zixin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (44.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SmartFont 提出了一种基于扩散模型的少样本字体生成框架，通过全局内容-风格生成与弱监督局部校正专家相结合，并引入去噪状态条件分配模块自适应地融合全局和局部特征，实现了更好的全局-局部平衡和细粒度字体生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少样本字体生成方法要么依赖全局内容-风格建模（鲁棒但解耦不完美），要么强调局部/组件建模（能捕捉细节但依赖局部先验和参考覆盖）。作者认为关键挑战不在于学习更纯的条件，而在于通过多级分配来组织互补但有偏的全局和局部条件。

Method: SmartFont 包含三个核心组件：(1) 全局内容-风格生成分支，负责整体字形结构；(2) 弱监督局部校正专家（Local Corrective Experts），通过语义-空间分配学习专家级局部概念和语义空间图，实现细粒度校正；(3) 去噪状态条件分配模块（Denoising-State Condition Allocation），在不同时间步和注入块上自适应地加权全局内容、全局风格和局部校正特征。

Result: 大量实验表明 SmartFont 在全局-局部平衡、字形质量和局部细节保真度上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过多级条件分配机制有效融合全局和局部信息，是提升少样本字体生成质量的有效途径。

Classification reason: 该论文研究少样本字体生成（Few-Shot Font Generation），属于图像生成中的可控生成/风格化任务。字体生成本质上是一种风格化图像生成任务，与"图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）中的可控编辑、风格化方向高度吻合，因此复用该 canonical subtopic。

Importance reason: Focuses on font generation, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's key boost keywords. Signals: Few-shot font generation, diffusion-based, global-local condition allocation, weak supervision, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Few-shot font generation simultaneously requires global structural completeness and fine-grained local style fidelity. Existing methods usually either rely on global content-style modeling, which is robust but imperfectly disentangled, or emphasize component/local modeling, which captures fine details but relies heavily on local priors and reference coverage. We argue that the key challenge is not merely to learn purer conditions, but to organize complementary yet biased global and local conditions through multi-level allocation during generation. To this end, we propose SmartFont, a diffusion-based few-shot font generation framework that combines global content-style generation with weakly supervised local corrective experts. The local branch performs semantic-spatial allocation by learning expert-wise local concepts and semantically meaningful spatial maps under weak component supervision, enabling fine-grained correction without requiring explicit component-conditioned inference. On top of this, a denoising-state condition allocation module adaptively weights global content, global style, and local corrective feature across timesteps and injection blocks. Extensive experiments show that SmartFont achieves better global-local balance, improves glyph quality and local detail fidelity.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 VAR 图像编辑器主要在 token 流、特征或扁平 next-token logits 上操作，未能充分利用逐位残差 VAR 模型的两个原生结构：逐位伯努利预测头和加性多尺度残差码场。作者希望利用这些结构设计一种无需训练的编辑方法，实现更好的文本对齐和背景保持。

Method: BitResEdit 包含两个互补模块：(1) BitEdit：在共享编辑前缀上计算源-目标对比，倾斜 CFG 后的逐位 log-odds，并将每次更新投影到伯努利-KL 信任域内；(2) ResEdit：将采样得到的比特转换为每尺度连续码残差，用定位掩码门控，通过生成器原生的尺度求和机制重新注入。两者结合实现决策时比特引导与组合时代码组合的耦合。

Result: 在 PIE-Bench 上使用 Infinity-2B 模型，BitResEdit 在编辑区域 CLIP 分数上比最强同骨干 VAR 编辑器提升 +1.07，同时背景保持竞争力相当。消融实验表明 BitEdit 和 ResEdit 在目标对齐和背景保持方面发挥互补作用。

Conclusion: BitResEdit 有效利用了逐位残差 VAR 模型的原生结构（伯努利预测头和加性残差码场），实现了无需训练的文本引导图像编辑，在文本对齐和背景保持之间取得了良好平衡，为 VAR 模型的图像编辑提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种针对视觉自回归（VAR）模型的文本引导图像编辑方法 BitResEdit，属于图像编辑方向。现有 taxonomy 中 multimodal_generation 方向下的 [CANONICAL] subtopic "visual_editing"（图像与视频编辑）完全匹配该论文的核心主题。

Importance reason: Novel training-free editing for bitwise-residual VAR models, directly relevant to continuous visual token and autoregressive image generation priorities. Signals: autoregressive image generation, continuous visual token, training-free editing, bitwise residual VAR, text-guided editing

Abstract: Text-guided image editing with visual autoregressive (VAR) generators requires controlling both what the model samples and where the sampled change is written back into the image code. Existing VAR editors mainly operate on token streams, features, or flat next-token logits, leaving two native structures of bitwise-residual VAR models underused: the per-bit Bernoulli prediction head and the additive multi-scale residual code field from which the image is assembled. We propose BitResEdit, a training-free editor for bitwise-residual VAR generators such as Infinity. BitEdit performs source-negative guidance by tilting the post-CFG per-bit log-odds along a source--target contrast computed on a shared edited prefix, then projects each update into a closed-form Bernoulli-KL trust region around the clean CFG sampler. ResEdit converts the sampled bits into per-scale continuous-code residuals, gates them with a localization mask, and re-injects them through the generator's native sum-of-scales. Together they couple decision-time bit guidance with combination-time code composition, so masked-out latent features are preserved exactly by code arithmetic while localized, scale-aware edits are applied inside the target region. On PIE-Bench with Infinity-2B, BitResEdit attains the strongest text alignment among same-backbone VAR editors, improving CLIP on the edited region by +1.07 over the strongest prior editor while keeping background preservation competitive with it. Ablations show BitEdit and ResEdit play complementary roles in target alignment and background preservation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有发型迁移方法在源图像和目标图像之间存在较大姿态和尺度差异时表现不佳，因为缺失的发型内容需要合成而非简单迁移。作者希望解决这一挑战，实现大视角差异下的准确、姿态一致且保持身份的发型迁移。

Method: HairPort 包含三个核心组件：(1) Bald Converter——基于 FLUX.1 Kontext 的 LoRA 微调，将带发人脸转换为逼真的秃头版本，并构建了包含6000对秃头/原始图像的 Baldy 数据集用于训练；(2) 3D感知迁移管线——对参考发型进行3D重建并从目标视角重渲染，实现几何对齐；(3) 条件流匹配生成器——以秃头源图像和几何对齐的参考引导为条件，合成最终迁移结果。

Result: 实验表明 HairPort 在定性和定量上均优于现有方法，能够处理大姿态和尺度差异下的发型迁移，生成结果准确、姿态一致且保持身份特征。

Conclusion: HairPort 通过显式分离发型移除与迁移、引入3D几何一致性约束，有效解决了大姿态差异下的发型迁移难题，为虚拟试穿、AR 和娱乐应用提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究发型迁移（hairstyle transfer），属于肖像动画/面部重演范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "数字人与动作生成"（avatar_motion_generation）高度相关。

Signals: 3D-aware hairstyle transfer, Bald Converter dataset, conditional flow-matching generator, not in priority subtopics, no boost keywords matched

Abstract: Transferring hairstyles between images is an important but challenging task in computer graphics, computer vision, and visual effects. It enables users to explore new looks without physically altering their hair, with applications in virtual try-on systems, augmented reality, and entertainment. Most prior works operate best under small pose gaps, and they fall short under large viewpoint and scale differences, where missing hair content must be synthesized rather than transferred. We propose HairPort, a 3D-aware hairstyle transfer framework that attempts to solve these issues by explicitly separating hair removal from transfer and enforcing geometric consistency before synthesis. We introduce a Bald Converter, which produces realistic bald versions of faces through LoRA-based in-context adaptation of FLUX.1 Kontext. To train our Bald Converter, we introduce a new dataset, Baldy, containing 6,000 paired bald and original images across diverse identities and conditions. We also use a 3D-Aware Transfer Pipeline that reconstructs and re-renders the reference hairstyle from the target viewpoint before compositing it onto the source image. Being 3D aware, our method supports large pose and scale discrepancies between the source and target. Finally, a conditional flow-matching generator synthesizes the transferred result from the bald source and geometry-aligned reference guidance. Together, our method enables accurate, pose-consistent, and identity-preserving hairstyle transfer, outperforming existing methods both qualitatively and quantitatively.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音驱动说话头动画方法在精确唇音同步与自然表情/头部运动之间存在权衡：要么准确但僵硬，要么富有表现力但同步性差。需要一种能同时兼顾精细唇部同步和自然头部运动的方法。

Method: 1) 基于预训练视频生成模型构建 flow-matching 语音到肖像动画框架；2) 提出多层级语音表征，在局部和全局粒度上捕捉音素和韵律信息；3) 通过可学习的层级选择器将这些表征选择性注入 transformer 块；4) 引入无奖励强化学习（Reward-Free RL）方案，在 flow-matching 训练中抑制感知上不合理的运动，无需依赖手工设计的同步指标或奖励模型。

Result: 在定量唇音同步精度和定性人类评估（自然度与表现力）上均显著优于现有方法，达到 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: ReFree-S2V 通过多层级语音表征与无奖励强化学习的结合，有效解决了语音驱动说话头动画中唇音同步与自然表现力之间的权衡问题，为生成逼真的对话式肖像视频提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究语音驱动的说话头肖像动画生成（speech-driven talking head animation），属于数字人与动作生成（avatar_motion_generation）这一[CANONICAL]子主题的典型范畴。

Importance reason: Novel use of reward-free RL for flow-matching video generation, but the digital human subtopic is not a priority. Signals: flow-matching, reward-free RL, speech-driven animation, multi-level speech representation, video generation

Abstract: Speech-driven talking character animation seeks to generate life-like portrait videos that convey natural conversation behavior, aligning facial motion with spoken audio. Although recent advances in video generation have substantially improved realism in video-based animation, achieving both accurate lip articulation and expressive behavior remains challenging. Existing approaches typically trade off precise phoneme-to-lip synchronization against dynamic facial expressions and head motion, yielding animations that are either accurate yet rigid, or expressive but poorly synchronized. We address this challenge by proposing ReFree-S2V, a flow-matching speech-to-portrait animation framework that builds upon a pretrained video generation model to achieve fine-grained speech articulation and high-level expressive cues in speech-driven portrait animation. This model introduces a multi-level speech representation capturing phonetic and prosodic information at both local and global granularities. These representations are selectively injected into transformer blocks via learnable level selectors, enabling both accurate lip synchronization and natural expressive motion. To achieve natural head movements, we further introduce a novel reward-free reinforcement learning scheme into flow-matching training to discourage perceptually implausible motion without relying on handcrafted synchronization metrics or reward models, or the high cost of human preference annotation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ReFree-S2V achieves state-of-the-art performance, significantly outperforming existing methods in both quantitative lip-sync accuracy and qualitative human evaluations of naturalness and expressivity.

</details>

*Amir Mann, Gal Michael Harari, Merav Keidar, Or Litany*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: VideoMDM 提出一种扩散框架，仅从单目视频中提取的精确2D姿态监督训练3D人体动作先验，无需任何3D真值。通过预训练的2D转3D提升器提供近似3D序列作为噪声教师，模型在3D空间扩散去噪后重投影到2D进行监督，并证明了深度加权的2D重投影损失在期望上等价于直接3D监督。在HumanML3D上接近全3D监督的MDM性能，在真实视频数据集上生成的动作更受人类偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D人体动作生成模型严重依赖大规模3D动作捕捉数据，获取成本高昂且难以扩展。而单目视频数据丰富易得，但缺乏3D真值。本文旨在利用大量易获取的2D视频数据来学习3D人体动作先验，降低对3D标注的依赖。

Method: 提出VideoMDM框架：1) 使用预训练的2D-to-3D提升器从单目视频的2D关键点生成近似3D姿态序列作为"噪声教师"；2) 对这些近似3D序列添加噪声后，由扩散模型在3D空间去噪；3) 将去噪后的3D预测重投影到2D，与精确的2D关键点计算深度加权重投影损失；4) 理论上证明该损失在期望上等价于直接3D监督；5) 将速度一致性和过参数化表示对齐等3D运动正则化器适配到2D设置。

Result: 在HumanML3D数据集上，FID达到0.88（全3D监督的MDM为0.54），接近全监督性能；在真实视频数据集Fit3D和NBA上，生成的动作在人类偏好评估中一致优于基线方法，定量结果表现强劲。

Conclusion: VideoMDM证明了仅从2D视频监督即可学习有效的3D人体动作先验，大幅降低了对昂贵3D动作捕捉数据的依赖。该方法在标准基准上接近全3D监督的性能，并在真实视频数据上展现出实际应用潜力，为大规模3D动作生成开辟了新路径。

Classification reason: 该论文研究从单目视频的2D姿态监督生成3D人体动作，属于人体动作生成范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "数字人与动作生成"（avatar_motion_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel 2D-supervised 3D motion diffusion, but outside user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: 3D human motion generation, diffusion framework, 2D supervision, no 3D ground truth, depth-weighted reprojection loss

Abstract: We introduce VideoMDM, a diffusion-based framework that trains 3D human motion priors directly from accurate 2D poses extracted from monocular videos, without any 3D ground truth. A pretrained 2D-to-3D lifter provides approximate 3D pose sequences that serve as a noisy teacher: these are diffused, denoised by the model in 3D, and supervised in 2D by reprojecting the prediction and comparing against accurate keypoints. We show that, under mild assumptions, a depth-weighted 2D reprojection loss is equivalent in expectation to direct 3D supervision, and we adapt standard 3D motion regularizers - velocity consistency and over-parameterized representation alignment - to this 2D setting. Unlike methods that lift 2D to 3D only at inference, VideoMDM learns a coherent 3D motion manifold during training. On HumanML3D it nearly closes the gap to fully 3D-supervised MDM (FID 0.88 vs 0.54); On real video datasets Fit3D and NBA the method learns to generate motions consistently preferred by humans, with strong quantitative results.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生图模型蕴含丰富的空间先验知识，但现有方法将其用于深度预测时需要密集深度标注且训练流程复杂。作者希望提出一种简单、可扩展的方法，利用预训练 T2I 模型的空间先验实现高质量的联合图像-深度生成与深度预测。

Method: 提出 Modality Forcing 后训练方法：在预训练的 DiT 基础上，为图像和深度两个模态分别分配独立的噪声水平（noise level），使得模型可以灵活支持图像→深度、深度→图像、以及联合生成等多种条件生成模式。使用每个模态独立的解码器，允许在稀疏的真实世界深度数据上训练。从零开始训练不同规模（370M~3.3B参数）的 T2I 模型，验证可扩展性。

Result: 更大规模的模型和更多图像数据能产生更准确的深度预测。最强模型与最先进单目深度估计器性能相当，相比现有联合图像-深度生成模型，AbsRel 降低了 57%。

Conclusion: 图像生成可以作为空间感知的可扩展预训练目标。Modality Forcing 提供了一种简单有效的后训练方案，证明了 T2I 模型中的空间先验可以通过联合生成的方式迁移到深度预测任务中，且性能随模型规模和数据量提升。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Modality Forcing，基于预训练的 DiT（扩散 Transformer）进行联合图像-深度生成，属于文生图与图像扩散模型范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "文生图与图像扩散"。

Importance reason: Novel method for joint image-depth generation using DiT, but only tangentially touches priority subtopics. Signals: Modality Forcing, joint image-depth generation, DiT scalability, sparse depth training, competitive depth prediction

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models contain rich spatial priors. Synthesizing photorealistic, cluttered scenes requires an understanding of geometry, including perspective and relative scale. Prior works adapt T2I models to leverage this prior for depth prediction, but they require dense depth data and involve complex recipes. We propose Modality Forcing, a simple, scalable post-training recipe for joint image-depth generation using a single DiT trained on sparse depth data. Modality Forcing enables conditional and joint generation of image and depth in any permutation by assigning separate noise levels per modality. Per-modality decoders let us train on sparse, real-world depth and achieve strong, generalizable depth prediction. We further show that Modality Forcing inherits the scalability of T2I pre-training: by training a set of T2I models from scratch (370M to 3.3B parameters), we find that larger models trained on more image data produce more accurate depth. Our strongest model is competitive with state-of-the-art monocular depth estimators and reduces AbsRel by 57% relative to existing joint image-depth generative models. These results provide strong evidence that image generation is a scalable pre-training objective for spatial perception.

### [46] [TetherCache: Stabilizing Autoregressive Long-Form Video Generation with Gated Recall and Trusted Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13035)
*Yu Meng, Xiangyang Luo, Letian Li, Wenyuan Jiang, Chen Gao, Xinlei Chen, Yong Li, Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TetherCache 提出一种无需训练、即插即用的缓存管理策略，通过 GRAB（门控召回+注意力多样性平衡）和 TAME（可信对齐记忆编辑）两种机制，在固定 KV-cache 预算下稳定自回归长视频扩散模型的生成质量，有效缓解时序漂移和视觉退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型在生成长视频时面临两个关键问题：1) 有限的 KV-cache 预算无法保留完整历史帧；2) 反复以自生成帧为条件导致上下文分布偏移随时间累积，产生视觉伪影、质量退化和时序漂移。现有方法缺乏有效的缓存管理策略来解决这些问题。

Method: TetherCache 将缓存组织为 sink、memory 和 recent 三个区域，并引入两种互补机制：1) GRAB（Gated Recall with Attention-Diversity Balancing）—— 结合注意力相关性和时序多样性的门控分数来选择长程记忆帧，在固定缓存预算下保留信息丰富且多样化的历史上下文；2) TAME（Trusted Alignment via Memory Editing）—— 对新召回的记忆 token 进行轻量编辑，将其统计量对齐到可信上下文分布，减少漂移历史特征带来的污染。

Result: 基于 Self-Forcing 框架，TetherCache 在 VBench-Long 基准上持续提升 30s、60s 和 240s 设置下的长视频生成质量。在 240s 生成中，整体分数和语义分数显著提升，质量漂移从 7.84 降至 1.33。

Conclusion: TetherCache 是一种有效的训练无关缓存管理策略，能够显著稳定自回归长视频扩散模型的生成过程，缓解时序漂移问题，为分钟级长视频生成提供了实用解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究自回归视频扩散模型的长视频生成，属于视频扩散/视频生成范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 文生视频与视频扩散 子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses autoregressive video generation drift, a key challenge for long-form video world models, with a training-free cache method. Signals: autoregressive video generation, training-free cache, long-form video, temporal drift, KV-cache

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models provide a natural formulation for streaming and variable-length video generation by conditioning newly generated frames on previously generated content. However, extending these models to minute-level generation remains challenging: the limited KV-cache budget prevents the model from retaining the full history, while repeatedly conditioning on self-generated frames induces a context distribution shift that accumulates over time, leading to visual artifacts, quality degradation, and temporal drift. In this paper, we propose TetherCache, a training-free and plug-and-play cache management strategy for drift-resistant long video generation. TetherCache organizes the cache into sink, memory, and recent regions, and introduces two complementary mechanisms. First, GRAB (Gated Recall with Attention-Diversity Balancing) selects long-range memory frames using a gated score that combines attention-based relevance with temporal diversity, preserving informative yet diverse historical context under a fixed cache budget. Second, TAME (Trusted Alignment via Memory Editing) lightly edits newly recalled memory tokens by aligning their statistics to a trusted context distribution, reducing the pollution caused by drifted historical features. Built on Self-Forcing, TetherCache consistently improves long-video generation quality on VBench-Long across 30s, 60s, and 240s settings. In particular, for 240s generation, it substantially improves overall and semantic scores while reducing quality drift from 7.84 to 1.33, demonstrating its effectiveness for stable long-horizon autoregressive video diffusion.

</details>

Abstract: Cloning camera motion from reference videos is an important task in video generation, as videos provide intuitive and precise control. Existing methods either directly use parametric representations that fail to handle multi-shot generation or synthesize cross-paired data, which suffer from data scarcity, resulting in poor performance in complicated camera motion cloning. To address these issues, we introduce a general camera motion representation that encodes cameras as grid motion videos. This camera grid represents the camera parameters visually and supports the integration of diverse trajectories for multi-shot video generation. Building upon this, we propose OmniDirector, a unified framework trained on a million-scale camera grid-video pairs that coordinates characters, actions, and cameras to provide director-level control for multimodal diffusion transformers. Furthermore, we design a novel hierarchical prompt expansion agent that harmoniously integrates different control signals by systematically describing camera motion and visual content through understanding signal relationships. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superior performance and outstanding controllability of our framework. Project page:

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的少步扩散蒸馏方法在 4-8 步生成上已较为成熟，但进一步压缩到 2 步时面临任务难度增大和模型容量不足的核心瓶颈。作者希望探索专门针对两步生成场景的蒸馏策略，在保持高质量的同时实现极致的生成效率。

Method: 提出三个关键设计：(1) 分布对齐对抗学习——使用教师模型生成的图像而非外部真实图像作为 GAN 训练的真实样本，提供更可达且信息量更大的对抗目标；(2) 步骤解耦参数化——为两个去噪步骤分配独立的模型参数，以更好地匹配各自不同的容量需求；(3) 端到端迭代正则化训练——允许第一步接收来自最终图像质量的梯度，同时通过显式的步骤 1 损失保留有意义的中间生成结果。

Result: 在定性和定量评估中，两步生成与八步生成之间的质量差距被显著缩小，展示了精心设计的蒸馏策略在改善少步生成质量-效率权衡方面的潜力。

Conclusion: 通过针对两步生成场景专门设计的蒸馏策略，可以大幅缩小两步与多步生成之间的质量差距，证明了在极少数步数下实现高质量图像生成的可行性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出两步图像生成蒸馏方法，属于扩散模型少步采样与蒸馏加速方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化" (efficient_generation) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances efficient image generation via distillation, aligning with high-priority model efficiency and autoregressive image generation subtopics. Signals: two-step image generation, diffusion distillation, teacher-aligned adversarial learning, step-decoupled parameterization, end-to-end iterative regularization

Abstract: Few-step diffusion distillation has become increasingly mature for 4-8-step generation, yet pushing further to 2 steps remains challenging. In this work, we introduce Z-Image Turbo++, a high-quality 2-step image generation model distilled from the 8-step Z-Image Turbo teacher. Our method addresses the central bottlenecks of increased task difficulty and limited model capacity in 2-step generation through three simple but effective design choices tailored to this regime. First, we propose Distribution-Aligned Adversarial Learning, which uses teacher-generated images rather than external real images as real samples for GAN training, providing a more attainable and informative adversarial target. Second, we adopt Step-Decoupled Parameterization, assigning independent model parameters to the two denoising steps to better match their distinct capacity demands. Third, we perform End-to-End Training with Iterative Regularization, allowing the first step to receive gradients from final image quality while preserving a meaningful intermediate generation through an explicit step-1 loss. Together, these designs substantially narrow the quality gap between 2-step and 8-step generation in both qualitative and quantitative evaluations, highlighting the potential of carefully tailored distillation strategies for improving the quality-efficiency trade-off in few-step generation.

</details>

Importance reason: Focuses on safety-constrained flow matching for robotics, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: flow matching, constraint embedding, projection-free, safety-critical, planning and control

Abstract: While flow-based generative models have demonstrated strong performance across a wide range of domains, deploying them in safety-critical physical systems remains challenging due to strict constraint requirements. Existing approaches typically enforce safety through post-hoc corrections, which incur substantial computational overhead and may distort the learned distribution. We propose PolyFlow, a polytope-constrained flow matching framework that embeds constraints directly into the model and flow dynamics. PolyFlow introduces a discrete-time flow formulation and a projection-free architecture, which eliminate the discretization error and guarantee strict satisfaction of arbitrary polyhedral constraints, without the need for expensive iterative solvers. Experimental results show that PolyFlow achieves zero constraint violation while maintaining high distributional fidelity across a range of planning and control tasks. Compared to state-of-the-art constrained generation baselines, PolyFlow significantly reduces inference latency and demonstrates a favorable trade-off between safety, efficiency, and generative quality. Code is available on .

</details>

*Jianwei Fei, Yunshu Dai, Zhihua Xia, Xiaochun Cao, Jiantao Zhou, Alessandro Piva, Benedetta Tondi*

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向文生图扩散模型的高效、鲁棒且抗合谋攻击的指纹水印方法。通过将指纹编码到个性化归一化模块（PNM）的系数中，并引入基于无损函数不变参数变换的抗合谋机制，该方法在保持生成质量的同时实现了99.5%以上的指纹提取准确率，并能有效阻止合谋攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型指纹水印方法缺乏对合谋攻击（多个攻击者联合模型以移除或混淆指纹）的鲁棒性，这是一个此前未被系统探索的漏洞。作者旨在首次实现具有抗合谋能力的T2I模型指纹水印方法。

Method: 将比特串（指纹）编码到个性化归一化模块（PNM）的系数中，嵌入T2I模型，使得从任何生成图像中都能可靠恢复指纹。引入基于无损函数不变参数变换的抗合谋机制，使合谋后的模型生成质量显著下降（FID升高），从而无法使用。通过重参数化PNM可高效创建多个带指纹的模型副本，无需重新训练。还引入最坏情况优化策略以提升对模型级攻击的鲁棒性。

Result: 指纹提取准确率超过99.5%，在多种T2I图像生成和编辑任务上实现了高保真度和鲁棒性。与现有方法相比，首次在合谋攻击下展现出显著的主动鲁棒性，显著提高了合谋模型的FID值。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法有效填补了生成模型指纹水印在抗合谋攻击方面的空白，为保护T2I模型的知识产权提供了一种高效、鲁棒且可扩展的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究文生图扩散模型的指纹水印技术，属于生成模型的可控性、版权保护与安全对齐范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）中的对齐、可控性主题高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on IP protection for diffusion models, not on core generative modeling or world models. Signals: model fingerprinting, anti-collusion, diffusion model security, not core generation, no world model or continuous token focus

Abstract: Model fingerprinting, embedding user-specific identifiers (fingerprints) into generated outputs, has recently emerged as a popular solution to protect the intellectual property rights (IPR) of generative text-to-image (T2I) models and prevent unauthorized redistribution. In this work, we reveal a previously unexplored systematic vulnerability in existing generative model fingerprinting methods: they lack robustness against collusion attacks, where multiple attackers combine their models to remove or obscure the fingerprints. To address this issue, we take the first step towards a robust fingerprinting method for T2I models with anti-collusion capabilities. The proposed method encodes strings of bits, namely fingerprints, into the coefficients of a personalized normalization module (PNM) incorporated into T2I models, so that fingerprints can be reliably recovered from any generated image. To defend against collusion attacks and prevent unauthorized model redistribution, we introduce an anti-collusion mechanism based on lossless function-invariant parameter transformations. This mechanism significantly degrades the image generation quality of colluded models, making them effectively unusable. Moreover, our method allows developers to efficiently create multiple copies of fingerprinted T2I models by reparameterizing the PNM without the need for retraining. We also introduce a worst-case optimization strategy to improve robustness against model-level attacks. Our experiments demonstrate that the proposed method achieves high fidelity and robustness across multiple T2I image generation and editing tasks, with fingerprint extraction accuracy exceeding 99.5%. Compared with existing methods, our method demonstrates, for the first time, a notable proactive robustness to collusion attacks by significantly increasing the FID of colluded models.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出DPOP（Direct Preference Optimization with Penalization），在DPO损失基础上增加一个门控惩罚项，当当前策略对偏好回答的似然仍低于对拒绝回答的似然时，对参考模型的贪婪回答施加惩罚。在AlpacaEval 2.0上，DPOP在Llama-3-8b-it和Gemma-2-9b-it上分别比DPO、SimPO、AlphaDPO等基线提升5.3%和4.4%的长度控制胜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线偏好优化方法（如DPO）仅使用静态数据集中存储的偏好回答和拒绝回答，忽略了参考模型自身对同一提示生成的回答这一有用信号。作者希望利用这一被忽视的信号来进一步提升偏好对齐效果。

Method: DPOP在DPO损失基础上增加一个门控惩罚项。该惩罚仅在当前策略对偏好回答的似然仍低于对拒绝回答的似然时激活，对参考模型的贪婪回答（即参考模型对同一提示生成的回答）施加惩罚。惩罚采用SimNPO风格的长度归一化形式，比NPO和token级unlikelihood更有效。

Result: 在AlpacaEval 2.0上，DPOP在Llama-3-8b-it和Gemma-2-9b-it上分别比DPO、SimPO、AlphaDPO等基线提升5.3%和4.4%的长度控制胜率。消融实验表明SimNPO风格的长度归一化惩罚优于NPO和token级unlikelihood。

Conclusion: 利用参考模型自身生成的回答作为额外信号，通过门控惩罚机制可以有效提升DPO类方法的对齐效果，是一种简单且有效的扩展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出DPOP，是对DPO（直接偏好优化）的改进，属于生成模型对齐与偏好优化范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on DPO variant for LLM alignment, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: DPO extension, reference-greedy penalty, AlpacaEval gains, no multimodal or generation focus, no priority keywords matched

Abstract: Offline preference optimization has become a practical substitute for reinforcement learning from human feedback, but pairwise objectives such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and its variants use only the chosen and rejected responses stored in a static dataset. This leaves a useful signal unused: the response that the reference model itself would generate for the same prompt. We propose Direct Preference Optimization with Penalization (DPOP), a simple extension of DPO that augments the base preference loss with a gated penalty on reference-greedy responses. DPOP activates this penalty only when the current policy still assigns a lower likelihood to the preferred response than to the rejected response. On AlpacaEval 2.0, DPOP improves length-controlled win rate over DPO, SimPO, and AlphaDPO on both Llama-3-8b-it and Gemma-2-9b-it, achieving relative gains of 5.3\% and 4.4\% over baselines on the two models, respectively. Ablations further show that a SimNPO-style length-normalized penalty is stronger than NPO and token-level unlikelihood in this setting.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图扩散模型的提示策略较为通用，难以准确表达情感意图和细微的情感属性，缺乏对生成图像情感可控性的有效方法。

Method: EPIG 基于心理学 valence-arousal 情感表征，设计结构化、角色感知的提示丰富策略，在无需修改或重训练生成模型的前提下，增强提示中的情感相关成分，生成情感感知的提示以引导扩散模型生成更情感一致的视觉输出。

Conclusion: EPIG 是一种有效、轻量且无需训练的提示级情感增强方法，能显著提升文生图模型的情感可控性，特别适用于资源受限和个性化图像生成场景。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 EPIG 方法，通过情感感知的提示增强来引导文生图扩散模型生成更符合情感意图的图像，属于对生成模型的可控性与对齐优化。这与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）中 guidance 与可控性的方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on emotion-based prompt engineering for image generation, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and autoregressive video/image generation. Signals: Prompt enhancement, emotion control, training-free, limited to 10 prompts, no model architecture contribution

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models have achieved impressive results in synthesizing high-quality images from natural language prompts. However, commonly used prompting strategies remain relatively generic, limiting the model's ability to accurately express emotional intent and nuanced affective attributes. This work proposes EPIG, a method that enhances emotional expressiveness at the prompt level prior to image generation. Grounded in psychologically informed emotion representations (valence-arousal) and leveraging structured, role-aware prompt enrichment, EPIG enriches emotion-related components of prompts without modifying or retraining the image generation backbone. The resulting emotion-aware prompts guide the generative process toward more emotionally coherent visual outputs, with particular effectiveness in controlling arousal. EPIG is lightweight, training-free, and well suited for resource-constrained and personalized image generation scenarios. Experimental results on a benchmark of 10 diverse prompts show that EPIG reduces mean arousal error compared to strong baselines, including naive insertion and LLM-based prompt expansion, with reductions of 14% and 12%, respectively. These improvements are statistically significant. EPIG also preserves valence alignment and semantic consistency, as measured by CLIPScore and supported by ablation studies. The effect is more pronounced on prompts containing explicit subjects such as humans, children, or animals, where the reduction reaches 17%, highlighting the subject-sensitive behavior of the proposed method.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出联合信源信道生成编码（JSCGC），用生成模型替代传统解码器，将接收信号作为条件控制生成采样过程，将通信从确定性重建重构为受控生成，在潜空间图像传输中显著提升感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: JSCGC成功将生成模型引入通信系统，将通信重新定义为受控生成问题，在感知质量上显著优于传统方法，为无线生成式通信提供了新范式。

Importance reason: Proposes a generative communication paradigm that replaces traditional decoding with controlled generation, aligning with multimodal generation but not directly with priority subtopics. Signals: generative communication, joint source-channel-generation coding, controlled generation, perceptual quality, latent-space image transmission

Abstract: Conventional communication systems, including both separation-based coding and learning-based joint source-channel coding (JSCC), are typically designed under Shannon's rate-distortion theory. However, relying on generic distortion metrics fails to capture complex human visual perception, often resulting in blurred or unrealistic reconstructions. In this paper, we propose Joint Source-Channel-Generation Coding (JSCGC), a generative communication paradigm that replaces the conventional decoder with a generative model at the receiver. The received signal is treated as a condition that controls the sampling process into the learned conditional distribution, reformulating communication from deterministic reconstruction for distortion minimization to controlled generation for mutual information maximization under perceptual constraints. Based on this formulation, we develop a unified joint training and efficient stochastic sampling framework, and provide theoretical analysis of its effectiveness in both learning and inference stages. Extensive experiments on latent-space image transmission demonstrate that the JSCGC consistently improves feature-based, semantic-level, and distributional quality across diverse channel conditions, while exhibiting a distinct error behavior characterized by semantic inconsistency rather than distortion.

</details>

Method: 提出Jeffrey guidance，基于Jeffrey条件化规则（Jeffrey's rule of conditioning）更新边缘分布以逼近指定目标分布，同时保持条件结构并最小化对联合分布的扰动。具体地，在扩散采样过程中，通过调整每个时间步的分布使其满足目标约束，从而实现对生成结果的控制。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和FFHQ上以Inception嵌入分布为目标，FID显著降低；在CelebA-HQ上成功实现公平性控制，使生成图像的属性之间满足独立性约束。

Conclusion: Jeffrey guidance为扩散模型提供了一种更通用的控制框架，能够处理标准guidance无法表达的应用场景（如目标嵌入分布、公平性约束），具有理论严谨性和实际有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出Jeffrey guidance，一种在扩散模型采样时通过条件化实现可控生成的通用框架，属于生成模型的对齐与可控性（guidance）范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a principled framework for controlling diffusion models that could enhance multimodal generation and alignment tasks. Signals: Novel theoretical framework, Improves FID on CIFAR-10 and FFHQ, Enables fairness constraints, Relevant to multimodal generation, Not directly in priority subtopics

Abstract: A key strength of diffusion models lies in their flexibility, since their outputs can be controlled at sampling time through guidance. However, beyond simple cases such as conditional sampling, the target distribution is often left implicit, defined only through a sampling rule or a heuristic energy function. To address this, we propose Jeffrey guidance, a principled framework that extends diffusion-model control to applications beyond what standard guidance can express. It leverages Jeffrey's rule of conditioning to update marginal distributions towards a prescribed target, preserving the conditional structure and minimally perturbing the joint distribution. We first demonstrate Jeffrey guidance by targeting a prescribed embedding distribution. With Inception embeddings as the target, this leads to substantial reductions in FID on both CIFAR-10 and FFHQ. We further apply Jeffrey guidance to fairness on CelebA-HQ, updating an unconditional diffusion model to enforce independence between attributes.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学图表（如流程图、架构图）包含丰富信息，但静态图表难以传达动态的逐步解释过程。现有视频生成系统缺乏从论文中提取叙述并定位到图表特定区域的能力，也没有相应的评测基准。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出 MINARD 流水线：1) 利用 LLM 从论文正文中提取与图表相关的逐步叙述（paper-grounded narration）；2) 将叙述中的每一步通过区域分解（region decomposition）定位到图表的对应区域；3) 基于定位信息生成带区域高亮的讲解视频。同时构建 FigTalk 基准，包含序列级和组件级定位评测指标。

Result: 在 FigTalk 基准上，MINARD 生成的叙述在忠实性和自然度上优于基线方法；在空间定位任务中，MINARD 的叙述条件定位准确率显著高于现有方法。自动评测和人工评测均验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 论文-图表到视频生成是一个有价值的新任务，MINARD 流水线展示了利用论文文本引导图表区域定位和视频生成的有效性。FigTalk 基准为后续研究提供了评测基础。该工作为科学图表理解与多模态生成结合开辟了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了图到视频生成任务（figure-to-video generation）并发布了 FigTalk 基准数据集和新评测指标，属于生成模型评测与数据集（generation_benchmark）这一规范主题。同时论文也涉及多模态理解与生成（视频叙述生成+空间定位），因此也匹配 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向。

Importance reason: Novel task and benchmark for scientific figure video generation, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or autoregressive generation. Signals: scientific figure video generation, paper-grounded narration, region decomposition, FigTalk benchmark, multimodal generation evaluation

Abstract: Scientific figures compress complex pipelines into a single canvas, yet understanding them requires paper-grounded, step-by-step narration aligned with visual highlights a capability missing from current video generation systems and benchmarks. To address this, we introduce paper-grounded figure-to-video generation: generating narrated, region-grounded walkthrough videos from a figure and its paper. We propose MINARD (Multimodal Interpretation of Narrated Architecture via Region Decomposition), a pipeline that generates paper-grounded narrations and sequentially grounds them to figure regions. We also release FigTalk, a benchmark with new sequential and component-level grounding metrics derived. On FigTalk, MINARD generates humanlike, paper-faithful narrations and outperforms narration-conditioned figure spatial grounding compared to existing approaches in both automatic and human evaluation

*Zeyue Tian, Lei Ke, Zhaoyang Liu, Ruibin Yuan, Liumeng Xue, Yujiu Yang, Weijia Chen, Xu Tan, Qifeng Chen, Wei Xue, Yike Guo*

TL;DR: AudioX-Turbo 是一个统一高效的 Anything-to-Audio 生成框架，基于教师-学生范式，教师模型 AudioX-Base 采用多模态扩散 Transformer 与自适应融合模块，学生模型通过分布匹配蒸馏（适配 flow matching）实现仅 4 步采样，同时构建了约 920 万样本的大规模数据集 IF-caps-Pro，在文本到音频/音乐生成等任务上达到 SOTA，推理速度提升约 25 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频生成方法缺乏统一的多模态条件建模框架，且多步扩散采样推理成本过高，亟需一个既能灵活处理文本、视频、音频等多种控制信号，又能高效推理的统一框架。

Method: 采用教师-学生蒸馏范式：教师模型 AudioX-Base 基于多模态扩散 Transformer（MM-DiT），引入多模态自适应融合模块对齐不同模态输入；学生模型 AudioX-Turbo 通过分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）适配 flow matching 进行蒸馏，并辅以扩散判别器实现高质量少步生成。同时构建了大规模高质量数据集 IF-caps-Pro（约 920 万样本），采用两阶段数据收集与标注流程。

Result: 在文本到音频、文本到音乐等多项任务上达到 SOTA 性能，仅需 4 步采样，函数评估次数（NFE）相比多步基线减少约 25 倍，展示了高效且强大的指令跟随能力。

Conclusion: AudioX-Turbo 证明了统一多模态条件音频生成框架的可行性，通过蒸馏大幅降低推理成本，为高效音频生成提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出统一框架实现文本/视频/音频到音频的生成，属于多模态条件音频生成，与 [CANONICAL] 音频与多模态联合生成 (audio_av_generation) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong on multimodal generation and efficient inference, but audio focus is secondary to user's top visual/language priorities. Signals: multimodal diffusion transformer, flow matching distillation, 4-step sampling, 25x speedup, text-to-audio/music SOTA

Abstract: Audio and music generation based on flexible multimodal control signals is a widely applicable topic, with the following key challenges: 1) a unified multimodal modeling framework, 2) large-scale, high-quality training data, and 3) the prohibitive inference cost of multi-step diffusion sampling. As such, we propose AudioX-Turbo, a unified and efficient framework for anything-to-audio generation that integrates varied multimodal conditions (i.e., text, video, and audio signals) in this work. AudioX-Turbo follows a teacher-student paradigm. The teacher AudioX-Base is built on a Multimodal Diffusion Transformer with a Multimodal Adaptive Fusion module that aligns diverse multimodal inputs for high-fidelity synthesis, and is then distilled into the few-step student AudioX-Turbo via Distribution Matching Distillation adapted to flow matching, complemented by a diffusion-based discriminator for high-quality few-step generation. To support the training of AudioX-Turbo, we construct a large-scale, high-quality dataset, IF-caps-Pro, comprising approximately 9.2M samples curated through a two-stage data collection and annotation pipeline. We benchmark AudioX-Turbo across a wide range of tasks, finding that our model achieves superior performance, especially on text-to-audio and text-to-music generation, while operating at only 4 sampling steps and requiring approximately 25x fewer function evaluations (NFE) than multi-step baselines. These results demonstrate that our method is capable of audio generation under flexible multimodal control, showing efficient and powerful instruction-following capabilities. The code and datasets will be available at .

</details>

### [57] [Trajectory-Level Redirection Attacks on Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12978)
*Gokul Puthumanaillam, Vardhan Dongre, Pranay Thangeda, Hooshang Nayyeri, Dilek Hakkani-Tür, Melkior Ornik*

Main category: eess.SY, cs.RO, cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的轨迹级重定向攻击方法。攻击者可以在保持提示词表面语义不变（不包含目标词和纠正性语言）的前提下，通过微小的提示扰动，使机器人执行的实际物理轨迹被重定向到攻击者指定的目标。作者形式化了"命令保持的轨迹重定向"威胁模型，并提出了一种基于on-policy rollout的提示搜索方法。实验表明，看似无害的提示扰动可以完全改变机器人的最终物理输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA攻击主要研究对抗性提示如何引发特定的低级动作或使这些动作在变化的图像中持续存在，但忽略了更危险的轨迹级故障模式：提示词看起来仍然指定了预期任务，但最终物理结果被重定向。作者希望揭示这种更隐蔽、更危险的攻击面。

Method: 作者形式化了"命令保持的轨迹重定向"威胁模型：攻击者在episode开始前选择一个提示词，所有策略和环境组件保持不变，提示词必须接近良性指令且不包含目标词和纠正性语言。提出了一种on-policy提示搜索方法，利用rollout来发现满足命令保持约束的扰动，使得闭环行为追踪目标任务轨迹。

Result: 在仿真和硬件实验中，近良性的提示扰动能够成功将VLA rollout重定向到攻击者指定的目标，验证了轨迹级漏洞的存在。

Conclusion: VLA模型在指令接地方面存在轨迹级漏洞：看似保留了原始命令意图的文本，实际上可以让攻击者控制机器人的最终物理输出。这暴露了当前VLA策略在安全方面的重要缺陷。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型的对抗性提示攻击，属于多模态安全与攻防范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态安全与攻防" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel security analysis for VLA models but outside user's priority subtopics and negative keywords apply. Signals: VLA security, trajectory-level attack, prompt perturbation, robot control vulnerability, on-policy search

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies bring natural language into closed-loop robot control, enabling robots to execute manipulation tasks directly from text instructions. The same interface gives text a recurring role in control because the prompt is reused at every replanning step, and each prompt-conditioned action changes the future observations on which the policy acts. Existing VLA attacks study adversarial prompts that elicit targeted low-level actions or make such actions persist across changing images. We identify a stronger trajectory-level failure mode: a prompt that still $\textit{appears}$ to specify the intended task but redirects the final physical outcome. We mathematically formalize this setting as $\textit{command-preserving trajectory redirection}$, a prompt-only threat model in which the attacker chooses one prompt before the episode, all policy and environment components remain fixed, and the prompt must stay close to the benign instruction while omitting target words and correction language. To find such prompts, we introduce an on-policy prompt search method that uses rollouts to discover perturbations whose closed-loop behavior tracks a target task while satisfying the command-preserving constraints. Experiments in simulation and on hardware show that near-benign prompt perturbations can redirect VLA rollouts to attacker-specified targets. These results expose a trajectory-level vulnerability in VLA instruction grounding: text that appears to preserve the intended command can still give an adversary control over the robot's final physical outcome. Project website:

</details>


### [58] [Order Is Not Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12923)
*Gareth Seneque, Lap-Hang Ho, Nafise Erfanian Saeedi, Jeffrey Molendijk, Tim Elson*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (47.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文论证"有序不等于可控"，提出一种接收器门控响应定律（receiver-gated response law），将控制定义为在有限努力下移动目标/输出类的能力，并在生物神经（小鼠ALM、线虫、斑马鱼）、LLM、适配器和随机算子面板上提供实验证据。LLM面板显示响应向量在72.8-84.8%的符号预测准确率，支持局部可接受控制与可测量随机响应算子，但将部署前生成控制、因果充分性等问题留作开放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐、可解释性和神经扰动研究识别出许多"有序诱导对象"（order-inducing objects），但作者指出有序并不等同于可控。缺乏一个严格的形式化框架来区分"系统表现出有序模式"和"系统真正可被控制"之间的本质区别，这可能导致对齐研究中的概念混淆和错误结论。

Method: 提出receiver-gated response law的形式化框架：一个以分母索引的算子，将材料状态、动作/驱动力、浴（bath）和接收器状态映射到响应位移、汇（sinks）、努力和盆地投影。该定律是局部的，干预可被接纳、饱和、变号、泄漏或过驱动。控制被定义为在相同分母下有限努力能移动目标/输出类，同时损伤、无效/规避、无效格式、过驱动和不必要努力保持有界。在生物神经面板（小鼠ALM、线虫、斑马鱼）、LLM面板（四种材料条件下响应向量预测）、适配器面板（宪法条件适配器改变易感性）和随机算子面板上验证该框架。

Result: LLM面板：四种材料条件下响应向量在72.8-73.7%的组件符号预测准确率，非零组件上提升至84.3-84.8%；留出观察者预测系统效应和目标/预言家族分别达93.6%和91.7%准确率。生物面板提供了物理响应算子证据，同时排除了坐标同一性和控制器结论。适配器面板显示宪法条件适配器作为制备介质重塑易感性。随机算子面板区分了测量机会与可部署行动策略。

Conclusion: 证据支持局部可接受控制（local admitted control）和可测量随机响应算子，但部署前生成控制（deployable pre-generation control）、隐藏/logit因果充分性、生物到LLM坐标同一性以及字面热力学量不在本文范围内。该工作为AI对齐提供了更精确的控制理论框架，有助于区分真正的可控性与表面有序性。

Classification reason: 论文核心讨论AI对齐（alignment）、可解释性（interpretability）、steering与神经扰动中的控制概念，提出"order is not control"的论点，并定义receiver-gated response law。这属于多模态安全与对齐的范畴，与现有[多模态安全与攻防]（multimodal_safety_attack）方向高度相关，因为对齐和安全是同一枚硬币的两面——对齐研究直接服务于安全控制。

Importance reason: The paper's focus on control theory and biological/LLM response laws is tangential to the user's priority on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: control theory, receiver-gated response, LLM steering, biological neural evidence, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: AI alignment, interpretability, steering, and neural perturbation studies identify order-inducing objects. We argue that order is not control. Control requires a receiver-gated response law: a denominator-indexed operator mapping material state, action/drive, bath, and receiver state to response displacement, sinks, effort, and basin projection. We identify it across biological, LLM, adapter, and stochastic-operator panels. The laws are local: an intervention can be admitted, saturated, sign-changing, leaky, or overdriven depending on medium, bath, receiver state, action port, and comparator. Control is assigned when finite effort moves a target or outcome-readout class under the same denominator while damage, null/evasive, invalid format, overdrive, and unnecessary effort stay bounded. Mouse ALM, C. elegans, and zebrafish panels provide physical response-operator evidence while excluding coordinate identity and controller conclusions. LLM panels show generated-output response laws: across four material conditions, response vectors are predictable at 72.8-73.7% component-sign accuracy, rising to 84.3-84.8% on nonzero components; held-out observers predict system-effect and target/oracle families at 93.6% and 91.7% accuracy. Constitution-conditioned adapters reshape susceptibility as prepared media, and stochastic-operator panels separate measured opportunity from deployable action policies. This gives a driven-dissipative response-system account at the mesoscopic control level: drives act through prepared media, baths, and receivers, producing admitted movement, impedance, sinks, or overdrive. The evidence supports local admitted control and measurable stochastic response operators, while leaving deployable pre-generation control, hidden/logit causal sufficiency, biological-to-LLM coordinate identity, and literal thermodynamic quantities outside scope.

</details>

Conclusion: 分类指标掩盖了延迟结构，序贯分析使延迟可测量。当前检测器与理论最优之间存在数量级差距，主要源于特征散度利用不足，而非时间累积策略。

Abstract: Token-level hallucination detectors are evaluated as classifiers, by AUC over all tokens, yet a streaming monitor is judged by its reaction time: the number of tokens that pass between the onset of a hallucination and the alarm. We formulate hallucination onset detection as a quickest change detection problem. A first-order Markov model of the latent faithful/hallucinated state, validated on RAGTruth, places the task inside classical change-point theory and yields Lorden's lower bound on detection delay: about 1.3 tokens at a false-alarm rate of 0.01. We then show that a causal recurrent labeler acts as a CUSUM with a learned increment; at a matched false-alarm rate it detects in 11-13 tokens, against 31 for a linear per-token baseline, and a controlled decomposition attributes most of this advantage to a better per-token score rather than to temporal accumulation. An information-rate optimality theorem of Donsker-Varadhan type explains the remaining order-of-magnitude gap: the learned score realizes only 1/4.5 of the divergence the features carry, a deficit that recalibration cannot remove, with the remainder a finite-horizon effect. Classification metrics conceal this delay structure; sequential analysis makes it measurable

Conclusion: 幻觉管理应被视为FDA全生命周期监管义务而非部署前检查清单，需要结合多种缓解策略并保持人类专家监督。

Classification reason: 论文系统性地研究医学影像AI中的幻觉问题，包括分类体系、检测与缓解策略，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的"多模态幻觉与可靠性"（multimodal_hallucination）这一规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical hallucination, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and has negative keyword overlap. Signals: Medical imaging hallucination, FDA regulatory framework, cross-modality analysis, general-purpose vs. specialized models, human-in-the-loop safeguards

Abstract: AI systems are being deployed across medical imaging faster than their failure modes are understood. At this point in time, the failure of greatest clinical concern is hallucination: clinically plausible but factually incorrect outputs, including fabricated anatomical structures, missed findings, incorrect laterality, and invented measurements in generated reports, with direct consequences, for example, for biopsy decisions, staging, and treatment planning. This structured narrative synthesizes peer-reviewed studies, benchmark datasets, and FDA regulatory guidance across five imaging modalities to produce a cross-modality analysis of hallucination taxonomy, etiology, detection, and mitigation. Specifically, we address three questions in this study: (1) how can existing taxonomies be unified across modalities?, (2) how do medical-specialized foundation models hallucinate less than general-purpose ones?, and (3) which mitigation strategies are effective and compatible with FDA lifecycle oversight? We note that three taxonomic frameworks together cover the imaging pipeline in a way no single framework does alone. We also highlight that general-purpose foundation models outperform medical-specialized models on hallucination-specific benchmarks, indicating that narrow domain fine-tuning can introduce overfitting-induced confabulation. At the same time, the oversight of radiologists remains essential; for instance, a very high percentage of of AI-generated flags required expert correction before clinical use. Physics-informed architectural constraints, Chain-of-Thought prompting, and human-in-the-loop safeguards each address different failure modes and is effective when combined. All findings are mapped to the FDA's Total Product Lifecycle and Predetermined Change Control Plan frameworks, which treat hallucination management as a lifecycle obligation rather than a pre-deployment checklist.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DPO等后训练对齐方法在医学领域存在三个关键局限：(1) 序列级奖励信号将临床关键token与通用填充词同等对待；(2) 依赖静态SFT参考作为偏好响应，导致离策略分布偏移，优化偏向风格伪影而非临床正确性；(3) 对齐目标缺乏显式的视觉定位约束，模型对细微但诊断关键的病理特征不敏感。

Method: 提出一个细粒度、在策略的对齐框架：(1) 双向token级KL正则化，对每个token施加细粒度约束；(2) 视觉对比定位目标，通过配对干净图像与病灶损坏图像，惩罚缺乏充分视觉证据的响应；(3) 通过最小编辑模型生成输出来构建偏好对，仅修正临床错误片段，保留原始语言风格，实现on-policy对齐。

Result: 在医学影像任务和临床文本生成基准上的大量实验验证了该方法的有效性，在细粒度对齐和临床准确性方面优于现有DPO及其变体方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的细粒度、在策略偏好优化框架有效解决了医学LVLM对齐中的关键挑战，通过token级KL正则化和视觉对比约束提升了模型在临床场景中的事实一致性和视觉定位能力。

Classification reason: 该论文研究医学LVLM的后训练对齐方法，提出细粒度偏好优化框架，属于多模态RLHF/DPO/偏好学习范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical LVLM alignment, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords include medical diagnosis. Signals: Medical domain, fine-grained preference optimization, token-level KL regularization, visual-contrastive grounding, on-policy alignment

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have achieved strong performance across medical imaging tasks, yet they remain prone to factual inconsistencies, poor visual grounding, and misalignment with clinically meaningful feedback. Existing post-training alignment approaches, including Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and its variants, face three critical limitations in the medical domain: (1) sequence-level reward signals treat clinically critical tokens identically to generic filler text; (2) reliance on static supervised fine-tuning references as preferred responses introduces an off-policy distribution shift, steering optimization toward stylistic artifacts over clinical correctness; and (3) alignment objectives lack explicit visual grounding constraints, leaving models insensitive to subtle yet diagnostically decisive pathological features. Our method leverages a bidirectional token-wise KL regularizer alongside a visual-contrastive grounding objective that pairs clean and lesion-corrupted images to penalize responses generated without adequate visual evidence. Together, these components form a fine-grained, on-policy alignment framework that constructs preference pairs by minimally editing model-generated outputs, correcting only clinically erroneous spans while preserving the original linguistic style. Extensive experiments across medical imaging tasks and clinical text generation benchmarks validate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>
