TL;DR: 本文提出ECA（Efficient Continual Alignment），一种无样本的增量学习方法，用于开放域图像到文本生成任务中的持续对齐。通过MoQ（混合查询）、FeDEx（Fisher动态扩展）和嵌入字典回放三个机制，在避免灾难性遗忘的同时高效学习新任务特征，并在四个新构建的基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有增量学习研究多关注分类任务，而开放域图像到文本生成（OpenITG）中的持续学习问题尚未被充分探索。实际场景中视觉数据的主导类别会随时间漂移，模型需要在不访问旧数据的情况下持续适应新分布，同时保持已有知识。

Method: ECA包含三个核心机制：(1) Mixture of Query (MoQ)模块，为每个任务分配可学习的查询token，实现任务特定特征提取；(2) Fisher Dynamic Expansion (FeDEx)，基于Fisher信息矩阵的度量动态扩展模型结构，在关键参数上分配新容量；(3) 嵌入字典与字典回放（DR），存储旧任务的嵌入表示用于回放，避免直接存储原始图像数据。

Result: 在四个新构建的增量学习OpenITG基准上，ECA相比基线方法显著缓解了灾难性遗忘，在多个评估指标上取得了更优的增量学习性能。

Conclusion: ECA通过持续对齐的方式有效解决了开放域图像到文本生成中的增量学习问题，为预训练VLM在动态环境下的持续适应提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究持续对齐（continual alignment）问题，即在预训练VLM上增量式适配对齐模块以应对视觉数据分布漂移，属于多模态对齐范畴。虽然侧重增量学习场景，但核心是跨模态对齐的持续适应，与[多模态强化学习与对齐]（multimodal_rl_alignment）主题高度相关。

Importance reason: Addresses continual alignment for image-to-text generation, but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: continual alignment, exemplar-free incremental learning, open-ended image-to-text generation, catastrophic forgetting mitigation, new benchmarks

Abstract: Incremental Learning (IL) for Open-ended Image-to-Text Generation (OpenITG) enables models to continuously generate accurate, contextually relevant text for new images while preserving previously acquired knowledge. Unlike prior studies, this paper addresses a more practical scenario in which the predominant category of visual data shifts over time as environments evolve. In this context, we introduce a new notion of continual alignment, which incrementally adapts the alignment module within pre-trained VLMs to preserve high-quality cross-modal representations. Based on this idea, we propose Efficient Continual Alignment (ECA), a novel exemplar-free IL approach for OpenITG. The key challenge is enabling the model to acquire new, task-specific features while minimizing interference with the established alignment without accessing raw data from previous tasks. To address this, ECA employs three core mechanisms: a Mixture of Query (MoQ) module that adapts task-specific query tokens, a Fisher Dynamic Expansion (FeDEx) that dynamically expands model structure based on a Fisher Information Matrix (FIM)-based metric, and an embedding dictionary with Dictionary Replay (DR) to retain past knowledge. To evaluate ECA's performance, we construct four new IL OpenITG benchmarks that better reflect real-world scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate that ECA significantly mitigates catastrophic forgetting and improves IL performance compared to baseline methods. Code and benchmarks are available at .

</details>

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出贝叶斯条件先验估计（BCP），一种无梯度的测试时自适应方法，通过注入标签依赖关系来改善冻结VLM在多标签识别中分布偏移下的性能，无需微调骨干网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的视觉语言模型（VLM）在多标签零样本推理中独立评分每个标签，忽略了标签间的共现结构，导致在分布偏移下产生不连贯的标签集（主导概念抑制了兼容的弱标签）。现有测试时自适应（TTA）方法主要针对单标签分类，不适用于多标签场景。

Method: 将零样本logits视为固定图像-文本似然下的边际后验代理，将分布偏移误差归因于不匹配的标签先验。对每个测试图像，选择高置信度锚定标签，应用锚定条件贝叶斯更新（闭式解），等价于点互信息（PMI）调整——促进兼容标签、抑制不兼容标签。通过从无标签测试流中在线估计二阶共现统计量来学习锚定条件先验，仅需一次前向传播的额外开销。

Result: 在多个多标签基准和CLIP骨干网络上，BCP一致优于强TTA基线：RN50平均mAP从57.31提升至69.22，ViT-B/16从62.61提升至71.79。

Conclusion: BCP是一种轻量级、无梯度的多标签TTA方法，通过贝叶斯条件先验有效注入标签依赖关系，显著提升VLM在分布偏移下的多标签识别鲁棒性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多标签测试时自适应（TTA）方法，通过贝叶斯条件先验估计来改进冻结VLM在分布偏移下的多标签识别性能。这属于多模态模型的对齐与适应问题，与[多模态强化学习与对齐]（multimodal_rl_alignment）中涉及的多模态对齐、偏好学习方向高度相关，因此复用该规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on multi-label test-time adaptation for VLMs, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks alignment with key boost keywords. Signals: multi-label recognition, test-time adaptation, VLM distribution shift, Bayesian priors, gradient-free

Abstract: Multi-label recognition with frozen Vision-Language Models (VLMs) is brittle under distribution shift: standard zero-shot inference scores labels independently, ignoring co-occurrence structure and producing incoherent label sets where dominant concepts suppress weaker but compatible labels. We introduce Bayesian Conditional Priors (BCP) Estimation, a gradient-free test-time adaptation method that injects label dependency without tuning the backbone. BCP views zero-shot logits as a proxy for marginal posteriors under a fixed image-text likelihood and attributes shift-induced errors mainly to a mismatched label prior. For each test image, it selects a high-confidence anchor label and applies an anchor-conditioned Bayesian refinement. This update is closed-form in logit space and admits a pointwise mutual information (PMI) interpretation, explicitly promoting compatible labels and suppressing incompatible ones. BCP operates without target annotations by estimating anchor-conditioned priors online from the unlabeled test stream via lightweight second-order co-occurrence statistics, adding negligible overhead beyond a single forward pass. Across standard multi-label benchmarks and multiple CLIP backbones, BCP consistently outperforms strong TTA baselines, e.g., improving RN50 average mAP from 57.31 to 69.22 and ViT-B/16 from 62.61 to 71.79.

</details>


### [64] [PolyAlign: Conditional Human-Distribution Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13227)
*L. D. M. S. Sai Teja, Ufaq Khan, Sathira Silva, Xiao Wu, Muhammad Haris Khan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PolyAlign 提出了一种条件化人类分布对齐框架，通过将双语交互数据按语言、交互轨道、回复族和长度组织成桶特定的人类参考分布，结合 Bucket-Aware SFT 和 Human-Distribution Preference Optimization (HDPO)，使语言模型在保持任务性能的同时更好地匹配不同交互上下文下的人类回复分布，超越传统的全局对齐目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 SFT 和偏好优化方法将语言模型对齐到单一的全局助手行为，这虽然提升了平均有用性，但抑制了人类回复在不同语言、任务和对话设置中的自然变化。作者希望模型能够根据当前交互上下文匹配相应的人类回复分布，而非统一的回复风格。

Method: PolyAlign 框架包含两个核心组件：(1) Bucket-Aware SFT，将双语交互数据按语言、交互轨道、回复族和长度划分为多个桶，并在不同桶之间平衡优化；(2) Human-Distribution Preference Optimization (HDPO)，使用批评模型估计到桶特定人类支持分布的距离，以此正则化偏好学习过程。

Result: 在覆盖英语和中文单轮/多轮对话的双语评测套件上，PolyAlign 在保持竞争性任务性能的同时，显著提升了条件自然度和分布忠实性。

Conclusion: 后训练对齐应超越全局对齐目标，转向交互感知的对齐方法，使模型能够匹配不同交互上下文下的人类回复分布。

Classification reason: 该论文研究语言模型的后训练对齐方法（SFT、偏好优化），提出条件化人类分布对齐框架 PolyAlign，属于多模态/语言模型对齐范畴。虽然论文主要针对语言模型（非多模态），但其对齐方法论与多模态对齐高度相关，且现有 taxonomy 中 multimodal_rl_alignment 是 [CANONICAL] 且最匹配的对齐相关子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on conditional human-distribution alignment for LLMs, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and video/image generation. Signals: LLM alignment, conditional distribution, bilingual evaluation, not multimodal, no video/image generation

Abstract: Post-training methods such as supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and preference optimization typically align language models toward a single global assistant behavior. While effective for improving average helpfulness, this can suppress the natural variation of human responses across languages, tasks, and dialogue settings. We study this problem as conditional human-distribution alignment: models should match the human response distribution appropriate to the current interaction context, rather than a universal response style. We introduce PolyAlign, a distribution-aware alignment framework that organizes bilingual interaction data into bucket-specific human reference distributions defined by language, interaction track, response family, and length. PolyAlign combines Bucket-Aware SFT, which balances optimization across heterogeneous buckets, with Human-Distribution Preference Optimization (HDPO), which regularizes preference learning using critic-estimated distance to bucket-specific human support. Across a bilingual evaluation suite covering English and Chinese single- and multi-turn settings, PolyAlign improves conditional naturalness and distributional faithfulness while preserving competitive task utility. The results suggest that post-training should move beyond global alignment objectives toward interaction-aware alignment with human response distributions.

</details>

*MohammadHossein Rezaei, Anas Mahmoud, Zihao Wang, Utkarsh Tyagi, Advait Gosai, Razvan-Gabriel Dumitru, Aakash Sabharwal, Bing Liu, Yunzhong He*

TL;DR: 提出 RGSD，一种无需 LLM 验证器的 rubric 引导自蒸馏方法，通过将 rubric 条件化教师分布逐 token 蒸馏到无条件学生模型，替代传统 GRPO 中的稀疏轨迹奖励和验证器开销，在医疗和科学领域达到可比性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 rubric 的训练方法依赖 LLM 验证器对每个 rollout 打分，带来大量训练开销、验证器偏差和稀疏的端轨迹奖励信号。作者希望消除验证器依赖，同时保留 rubric 的指导能力。

Method: RGSD 将基础策略模型分为两个副本：一个以 rubric 为条件的教师模型和一个无条件的 student 模型。教师模型在 rubric 条件下生成分布，student 模型通过逐 token KL 散度蒸馏学习该分布，从而将稀疏的轨迹级奖励替换为密集的逐 token 学习信号。训练中完全移除 LLM 验证器，每个 prompt 仅需一次 on-policy rollout。

Result: 在 Qwen-2.5 (3B, 7B) 和 Qwen3-Thinking (4B, 8B) 模型上，在医疗和科学领域，RGSD 的 rubric 满意度与基于 judge 的 GRPO 相当。消融实验表明原始 rubric 比自生成参考响应提供更强的教师增强信号，而更强的 GRPO judge 在某些设置下可超越 RGSD。

Conclusion: RGSD 是一种有效的无验证器替代方案，适用于验证器成本或可靠性成为瓶颈的场景，可作为 GRPO 等 judge-based 方法的补充。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Rubric-Guided Self-Distillation (RGSD)，一种无需验证器的后训练对齐方法，使用 rubric 条件化教师蒸馏来替代 RLVR/GRPO 中的奖励信号。这属于多模态/语言模型的对齐与偏好优化范畴，与 canonical subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel verifier-free distillation method for rubric-based alignment, but focuses on medical/science domains and RL training, not on the user's priority subtopics. Signals: rubric-guided self-distillation, verifier-free training, per-token distillation, medical and science domains, post-training alignment

Abstract: Rubrics have emerged as an alternative to RLVR in open-ended domains where a single ground-truth final answer is not available. Existing rubric-based training methods rely on an LLM verifier that scores each rollout against rubrics. This introduces substantial training-time overhead, exposes optimization to verifier-specific biases, and reduces rubric feedback to a sparse end-of-trajectory signal. We propose Rubric-Guided Self-Distillation (RGSD), a verifier-free training method in which the base policy, conditioned on the rubric, serves as the teacher for the unconditioned student. RGSD distills the rubric-conditioned teacher distribution into the student token-by-token, replacing sparse trajectory-level rewards with dense per-token learning signals and removing the LLM judge from the training loop entirely. Across Qwen-2.5 (3B, 7B) and Qwen3-Thinking (4B, 8B) models on medical and science domains, RGSD achieves rubric satisfaction comparable to judge-based GRPO while using one on-policy rollout per prompt and no training-time verifier calls. Ablations show that raw rubrics provide a stronger teacher enrichment signal than self-generated reference responses, while a stronger GRPO judge can outperform RGSD in some settings, positioning RGSD as a complementary verifier-free alternative when verifier cost or reliability is the bottleneck.

</details>


### [66] [Dense Supervision, Sparse Updates: On the Sparsity and Geometry of On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13657)
*Guo Yu, Wenlin Liu, Yulan Hu, Hao-Xuan Ma, Jun-Peng Jiang, Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了 on-policy distillation（OPD）在语言和视觉语言模型上的参数更新特性，发现 OPD 更新具有坐标稀疏性、FFN 偏重、数值满秩但谱集中、且主要落在源权重接近零的坐标上，表明密集教师监督并未导致密集的参数重写，而是保留了 on-policy 后训练的几何特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy distillation（OPD）结合了 on-policy 学生轨迹和密集教师监督，已成为重要的后训练方法，但其对模型参数的具体影响尚不清楚。理解 OPD 如何改变参数有助于揭示其工作机制并指导更高效的训练策略。

Method: 在多个语言模型和视觉语言模型对上，从稀疏性和几何两个维度分析 OPD 的参数更新模式：1）稀疏性分析：测量更新幅度、坐标稀疏度、层分布和 FFN 偏重；2）子网络恢复实验：仅训练发现的关键子网络能否恢复完整 OPD 性能；3）优化器消融：比较 SGD 与 AdamW；4）几何分析：计算更新的数值秩、谱分布、与源权重主奇异子空间的关系、以及更新在源权重接近零坐标上的分布。

Result: 1）OPD 更新幅度小且坐标稀疏，分布在所有层中且偏重 FFN；2）仅训练发现的子网络可恢复接近完整 OPD 的性能；3）SGD 在 OPD 中表现不如 AdamW，因为密集教师监督保留了异构的坐标梯度尺度；4）更新数值满秩但谱集中，主要位于源权重主奇异子空间之外，且集中在源权重接近零的坐标上。

Conclusion: 密集教师监督并未使 OPD 变成普通的密集参数重写，OPD 保留了 on-policy 后训练的重要几何特征。这些发现为理解蒸馏后训练机制提供了理论洞见，并提示了更高效训练策略的设计方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 on-policy distillation（OPD）在语言模型和视觉语言模型上的参数更新稀疏性与几何特性，属于后训练对齐/蒸馏方法分析。虽然不直接涉及 RLHF/DPO，但 OPD 是 post-training 中对齐/蒸馏的一种重要范式，与多模态对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）方向紧密相关，且该 canonical 子主题涵盖多模态后训练对齐方法。

Importance reason: Focuses on on-policy distillation sparsity/geometry, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: on-policy distillation, parameter sparsity, FFN-heavy updates, spectral geometry, not aligned with user's priority subtopics

Abstract: On-policy distillation (\textsc{OPD}) has recently become a prominent post-training recipe as it combines two desirable ingredients: on-policy student trajectories and dense teacher supervision, yet how this hybrid changes a model's parameters remains unclear. Across several language and vision-language model pairs and use cases, our analysis yields two main findings. On sparsity, \textsc{OPD}-style updates are small and coordinate-sparse. They are distributed across layers and are usually FFN-heavy. This sparse structure is operationally useful: training only the discovered subnetwork recovers nearly the same performance as full \textsc{OPD}. However, the sparsity-inducing SGD optimizer underperforms AdamW in our optimizer ablation, likely because dense teacher supervision preserves heterogeneous coordinate-wise gradient scales where AdamW's adaptive scaling remains useful. On geometry, the updates are numerically full-rank but spectrally concentrated; they lie mostly away from the principal singular subspaces of the source weights and fall disproportionately on coordinates where the source weights are close to zero. These findings suggest that dense teacher supervision does not turn \textsc{OPD} into ordinary dense parameter rewriting; instead, \textsc{OPD} retains important geometric signatures of on-policy post-training.

</details>

*Changye Li, Meng Lu, Yi Wu, Ligeng Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 PERIA，一个工具增强的视觉智能体，通过视觉感知工具和视觉交互工具来增强 VLM 的空间推理能力。采用监督工具轨迹合成、复合奖励和 OR-GIGPO 策略优化进行训练，在 13 个基准上相比 Qwen3-8B 提升 10.0%（分布内）和 4.4%（分布外），性能媲美 Qwen3-VL-235B 和 GPT-5 等大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在空间推理任务中表现有限，因为仅依赖视觉编码器的隐式表征不足以恢复细粒度的空间证据。空间推理需要主动获取证据和多步视觉交互，而现有模型缺乏这种能力。

Method: 提出 PERIA 框架，包含两类轻量工具族：视觉感知工具（提取文本、符号、空间证据）和视觉交互工具（操作视觉上下文、追踪路径、验证空间关系）。训练采用三阶段方案：监督工具轨迹合成、复合奖励设计、以及 Observation-Relaxed Group-in-Group Policy Optimization (OR-GIGPO) 进行多工具行为优化。

Result: 在 8 个数据集的 13 个基准上，PERIA-8B 相比 Qwen3-8B 骨干在分布内基准提升 10.0%，在分布外基准提升 4.4%。超越同尺寸 SOTA 基线 7.0%-14.8%，性能与 Qwen3-VL-235B-A22B-Thinking 和 GPT-5 等大模型相当。

Conclusion: 工具增强的视觉智能体方法能有效提升 VLM 的空间推理能力，通过显式的感知与交互工具弥补隐式视觉表征的不足，为构建更强的多模态推理系统提供了有效路径。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于空间推理（spatial reasoning），通过工具增强的视觉智能体进行多步视觉交互与推理，属于多模态推理范畴。现有 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "多模态推理与思维链"（multimodal_reasoning_cot）涵盖视觉推理、空间推理等内容，与该论文高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong spatial reasoning benchmark but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Tool-augmented VLM, spatial reasoning, OR-GIGPO policy optimization, 13 benchmarks, Qwen3-8B backbone

Abstract: While recent vision-language models (VLMs) demonstrate strong multimodal understanding, they remain limited in spatial reasoning tasks that require active evidence acquisition and multi-step visual interaction. This limitation suggests that relying solely on implicit visual representations from vision encoders is insufficient for recovering fine-grained spatial evidence. We introduce PERception-Interaction-reason Agent (PERIA), a tool-augmented visual agent for spatial reasoning tasks across map reasoning, visual probing, and vision reconstruction. PERIA uses two lightweight tool families: vision perception tools for exposing textual, symbolic, and spatial evidence, and vision interaction tools for manipulating visual context, tracing paths, and verifying spatial relations. To train PERIA, we develop a unified recipe that combines supervised tool-use trajectory synthesis, composite rewards, and Observation-Relaxed Group-in-Group Policy Optimization (OR-GIGPO) for effective multi-tool behavior. Experiments on 13 benchmarks from 8 datasets show that PERIA-8B improves over the Qwen3-8B backbone by 10.0% on in-distribution benchmarks and 4.4% on out-of-distribution benchmarks, while outperforming previous state-of-the-art baselines of similar size by 7.0%-14.8%. It also achieves performance comparable to much larger models such as Qwen3-VL-235B-A22B-Thinking and GPT-5, demonstrating the effectiveness of PERIA in enhancing spatial reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [68] [Language-Guided Abstraction for Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12847)
*Xu-Jing Ye, Yuan-Gen Wang, Ruping Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 L-VARC 框架，利用语言引导的 Privileged Information（LUPI）分支增强视觉抽象推理，通过 DeepSeek-V3 语义压缩模块和跨注意力投影器对齐视觉与语义特征，推理时仅需 18M 参数，在 ARC 任务上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ARC 方法要么依赖纯语言 LLM（参数庞大），要么纯视觉方法难以捕捉高层语义、容易过拟合像素级模式。如何用语言先验引导视觉推理同时保持轻量级推理模型是一个关键挑战。

Method: 提出 L-VARC 框架：(1) 语义压缩模块——用统一任务无关提示输入 DeepSeek-V3 对 LARC 语言描述进行精炼和结构化，使其适配 CLIP 文本编码器长度；(2) 跨注意力投影器——对齐视觉特征与语义嵌入，在训练阶段通过 LUPI 分支引导视觉模型学习，推理时丢弃该分支，仅保留 18M 参数的轻量模型。

Result: 在 ARC 任务上超越现有 SOTA 方法，消融实验验证了语义压缩模块和跨注意力投影器的各自贡献。

Conclusion: 语言引导的 Privileged Information 能有效提升视觉抽象推理能力，且推理时无需额外语言模型开销，实现了轻量高效的视觉推理。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 ARC 抽象推理任务，通过语言引导增强视觉推理，属于多模态推理范畴，与 canonical 子主题"多模态推理与思维链"（multimodal_reasoning_cot）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on ARC visual reasoning with language guidance, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: ARC reasoning, language-guided abstraction, LUPI branch, lightweight inference, DeepSeek-V3

Abstract: The Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus (ARC) is viewed as a critical avenue to Artificial General Intelligence (AGI), as it enables models to learn abstract transformation rules from few-shot examples and then generalize to new tasks. However, prevalent ARC methodology is either pure language or vision-only (i.e., VARC). The former depends heavily on LLMs, consuming billions of parameters. The latter often struggles to capture high-level semantics, leading to overfitting on pixel-level patterns. To bridge this gap, we propose L-VARC, a novel framework that enhances visual reasoning via a language-guided Learning Using Privileged Information (LUPI) branch. Specifically, we design a Semantic Compression Module by feeding a unified, task-agnostic prompt into DeepSeek-V3. In this way, the raw LARC (a crowd-sourced language description dataset) can be substantially refined and structured, fitting with the context length constraint of standard text encoders (e.g., CLIP). Moreover, we design a Cross-Attention Projector to align visual features with semantic embeddings, aiming to guide the training of the ARC model. Notably, the LUPI branch is taken in the training process and will be discarded during inference, thereby yielding a lightweight model with a mere 18 million parameters. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our L-VARC effectively leverages linguistic priors to boost visual reasoning and outperforms state-of-the-art. Ablation studies further confirm the contribution of the two new designs towards the L-VARC framework. The code is available at .

</details>


### [69] [Bridging Modal Isolation in Interleaved Thinking: Supervising Modality Transitions via Stepwise Reinforcement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12886)
*Tingyu Li, Le Zhou, Siyuan Li, Yujun Wu, Xinglong Xu, Jingxuan Wei, Conghui He, Cheng Tan*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MoTiF 框架，通过两阶段训练（Reflective SFT + Flow-GRPO）直接优化交错推理中文本与图像模态之间的转换保真度，解决模态隔离问题，在视觉谜题基准上显著提升跨模态一致性与任务准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂长链交错推理中，统一多模态模型生成的图像与文本上下文脱节，后续文本又忽略视觉证据，导致两种模态交替但无法真正相互促进。作者将这种现象定义为"模态隔离"，并归因于模态边界处的累积信息损失。

Method: 将每个推理循环分解为原子操作，定义模态转换损失（modality transition loss），量化文本到图像的跨模态幻觉和图像到文本的视觉利用不足。提出 MoTiF 两阶段训练框架：Reflective SFT 训练模型检测并恢复错误视觉输出；Flow-GRPO 通过强化学习提升图像生成保真度。所有训练信号来自转换级保真度而非最终任务准确率。

Result: 在四个视觉谜题基准上，转换级监督方法显著提升了跨模态一致性和最终任务准确率，验证了显式结构监督在模态边界处的有效性。

Conclusion: 有效的交错推理需要在模态边界处进行显式结构监督，而不仅仅是扩大模型规模或优化最终任务目标。

Classification reason: 该论文研究交错思维（interleaved thinking）中文本推理与视觉生成的交替过程，提出模态隔离问题并通过逐步强化学习优化模态转换。核心是多模态推理（交替推理链），属于多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Directly addresses multimodal interleaved reasoning and modality transition fidelity, aligning with your priority on multimodal understanding and generation. Signals: multimodal interleaved reasoning, modality transition supervision, reinforcement learning for generation, visual puzzle benchmarks, cross-modal coherence

Abstract: Interleaved thinking, where a unified multimodal model alternates between textual reasoning and visual generation, has shown promise on spatial and physical tasks. However, in complex long-chain scenarios, we identify a fundamental failure mode: generated images diverge from the textual context while subsequent text ignores the visual evidence, causing the two modalities to alternate without genuinely informing each other. We term this Modal Isolation and attribute it to compounding information loss at modality boundaries. We decompose each reasoning cycle into atomic operations and define modality transition loss, quantifying cross-modal hallucination (text-to-image) and visual utilization deficit (image-to-text) at each boundary. We propose MoTiF (Modality Tiransition Fidelity), a two-stage training framework that directly optimizes these transitions: Reflective SFT trains the model to detect and recover from erroneous visual outputs; Flow-GRPO improves image generation fidelity via reinforcement learning. All training signals in MoTiF derive from transition-level fidelity rather than end-task accuracy. Across four visual puzzle benchmarks, this transition-level supervision substantially improves both cross-modal coherence and final task accuracy. The results demonstrate that effective interleaved reasoning requires explicit structural supervision at modality boundaries, not merely scaling or end-task optimization.

</details>


### [70] [Iterative Visual Thinking: Teaching Vision-Language Models Spatial Self-Correction through Visual Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13156)
*Animesh Tripathy, Aswanth Krishnan*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (89.43) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Iterative Visual Thinking (IVT)，一种闭环框架，让 VLM 通过观察自身预测的渲染结果进行迭代视觉反馈修正。通过两阶段训练（SFT 教师修正数据 + GRPO 强化学习），在 RefCOCOg 等空间定位基准上，Acc@0.5 从 79.6% 提升至 82.0%，且多步修正稳定性显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 在单次空间定位上表现良好，但缺乏观察和修正自身预测的机制。直接让 VLM 迭代观察自身预测的渲染结果会导致性能崩溃（Acc@0.5 从 79.6% 骤降至 48.7%），说明存在 grounding 能力与自我修正能力之间的根本差距。

Method: 提出 Iterative Visual Thinking (IVT) 闭环框架：模型预测边界框 → 将预测渲染到图像上 → 模型观察渲染结果并迭代修正。两阶段训练：(1) SFT 阶段：利用基础模型自身的预测作为真实错误，用教师 VLM 生成修正推理轨迹，无需人工标注；(2) GRPO 阶段：使用 IoU 奖励进行组相对策略优化，稳定多步修正过程。

Result: 在 RefCOCOg、Ref-Adv、Ref-L4 混合基准（505 样本）上，SFT + IVT 全面超越单次基线：Acc@0.5 达 82.0%（+2.4pp），Acc@0.7 达 74.1%（+3.2pp），Acc@0.9 达 48.3%（+2.8pp）。GRPO 进一步将每步 IoU 退化降低 5 倍。全部训练仅用 2400 样本和单张 GPU。

Conclusion: 空间自我修正是可学习的能力，通过视觉反馈闭环和两阶段训练可以在较小规模下有效注入 VLM，弥合 grounding 与 self-correction 之间的差距。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Iterative Visual Thinking (IVT)，通过视觉反馈让 VLM 迭代修正空间定位预测，属于多模态视觉推理与自我修正能力，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链 (multimodal_reasoning_cot) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Introduces a novel closed-loop self-correction framework for VLM spatial grounding with modest training cost, directly relevant to multimodal understanding and reasoning. Signals: iterative visual feedback, spatial self-correction, GRPO for refinement, low-resource training, VLM grounding improvement

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong singleshot spatial grounding, yet lack any mechanism to observe and correct their own predictions. We find that naively prompting a VLM to iterate over rendered visualizations of its predictions causes catastrophic failure: Acc@0.5 on referring expression comprehension collapses from 79.6% to 48.7% (a 31 percentage point drop), revealing a fundamental gap between grounding capability and self-correction ability. We propose Iterative Visual Thinking (IVT), a closed-loop framework in which the model predicts a bounding box, observes the prediction rendered on the image, and iteratively refines through visual feedback. A two-phase training recipe closes the self-correction gap: first, we exploit the base model's own predictions as realistic errors and prompt a teacher VLM to generate corrective reasoning traces, yielding supervised data without human annotation; second, we apply Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with a simple IoU reward to stabilize multi-step refinement. On a mixed benchmark spanning RefCOCOg, Ref-Adv, and Ref-L4 (505 test samples), SFT warm-up with IVT surpasses the single-shot base model on every metric: Acc@0.5 rises to 82.0% (+2.4pp), Acc@0.7 to 74.1% (+3.2pp), and Acc@0.9 to 48.3% (+2.8pp). GRPO further reduces per-step IoU degradation by 5x, stabilizing the refinement trajectory. All training uses only 2,400 samples on a single GPU, demonstrating that spatial self-correction is a learnable capability that can be instilled at modest scale.

</details>


### [71] [Cross-Modal Masked Compositional Concept Modeling for Enhancing Visio-Linguistic Compositionality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13288)
*Wei Li, Zhen Huang, Xinmei Tian*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MACCO 框架，通过跨模态掩码组合概念建模来增强视觉语言模型的组合性理解能力。该方法在一个模态中掩码组合概念，并从另一模态的完整上下文信息中重建它们，同时引入两个辅助目标进行跨模态和模态内的特征对齐与正则化。在五个组合性基准上显著提升了 CLIP 等 VLM 的组合性理解，并改善了文本到图像生成和多模态大语言模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP 等对比训练的视觉语言模型在组合性理解上存在"词袋"行为，难以捕捉物体关系、属性-物体绑定和词序依赖。现有方法未能充分利用图文数据中固有的丰富组合信息。

Method: 提出 MACCO 框架，核心思想是跨模态掩码组合概念建模：在一个模态中掩码组合概念（如属性、关系），然后利用另一模态的完整上下文信息进行重建。同时引入两个辅助目标：跨模态对齐（inter-modal）和模态内正则化（intra-modal），以联合对齐和正则化掩码特征。

Result: 在五个组合性基准上取得显著提升，增强了 VLM 对句法结构和语言信息的捕捉能力。改进的组合性还迁移到了文本到图像生成和多模态大语言模型任务中。

Conclusion: 跨模态掩码组合概念建模是一种有效增强视觉语言模型组合性理解的方法，且改进具有跨任务迁移性。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于提升视觉语言模型的组合性理解能力（compositional understanding），属于多模态推理范畴。组合性理解（如属性-物体绑定、关系推理）是多模态推理的核心子问题，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态推理与思维链" (multimodal_reasoning_cot)。

Importance reason: Focuses on visio-linguistic compositionality, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous models or video generation. Signals: Compositional understanding, CLIP enhancement, Cross-modal masking, Text-to-image generation improvement, MLLM improvement

Abstract: Contrastively trained vision-language models like CLIP, have made remarkable progress in learning joint image-text representations, but still face challenges in compositional understanding. They often exhibit a "bag-of-words" behavior--struggling to capture the object relations, attribute-object bindings, and word order dependencies. This limitation arises not only from the reliance on global, single-vector representations for optimization, but also from the insufficient exploitation and modeling of the rich compositional information inherently present in paired image text data. In this work, we propose MACCO (MAsked Compositional Concept MOdeling), a framework that masks compositional concepts in one modality and reconstructs them conditioned on the full contextual information from the other, enabling the model to capture and align cross-modal compositional structures more effectively. To facilitate this process, we introduce two auxiliary objectives that jointly align and regularize masked features both inter-modally and intra-modally. Extensive experiments on five compositional benchmarks, along with in-depth analyses, demonstrate that our approach not only significantly enhances compositionality in VLMs but also improves their ability to capture syntactic structure and linguistic information. Additionally, the improved compositionality also benefits text-to-image generation and multimodal large language model. Code is available at .

</details>

### [72] [GRIP: Feedback-Guided Prompt Retrieval for Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12744)
*Garvita Allabadi, Matteo Sodano, Roberto Estevão, Yuxiong Wang, Vikram Adve, Emre Kiciman, Ranveer Chandra*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GRIP 提出一种可学习的视觉检索框架，利用多模态大模型的反馈信号通过对比训练来区分有益/有害的上下文示例，替代传统基于特征相似度的检索方法，在分类、描述和 VQA 任务上持续优于相似度检索，且可跨模型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 M-ICL 方法基于特征空间相似度选择上下文示例，但作者系统分析发现视觉相似的示例并不一定最有助于提升上下文学习性能，因此需要一种能利用模型反馈来识别真正有益示例的检索方法。

Method: 提出 GRIP（Guided Retrieval of In-context Prompts），一个纯视觉的可学习检索框架。通过对比训练，利用 LMM 的反馈信号（如预测正确性）来学习区分有益和有害的上下文示例，从而优化检索过程，超越纯相似度匹配。

Result: 在分类、描述和 VQA 三个多模态任务上，GRIP 在 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 上持续优于基于相似度的检索方法，在 Idefics2-8B 上的分类任务中提升最为显著。此外，从一个开源 LMM 训练的检索器可迁移到其他模型（包括 GPT-4o 和 Gemini）而无需重新训练。

Conclusion: GRIP 证明了利用 LMM 反馈信号进行检索训练可以有效提升 M-ICL 性能，且具有良好的跨模型迁移性，为多模态上下文学习提供了一种可扩展且经济的部署方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 GRIP，一种基于 LMM 反馈的示例检索框架，用于多模态上下文学习（M-ICL）中的 prompt 选择。核心是跨模态/多模态检索（从示例库中检索最有助于 LMM 预测的图像-文本对），属于多模态检索范畴，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 "多模态检索"。

Importance reason: Focuses on M-ICL retrieval for LMMs, not on core priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Multimodal in-context learning, Retrieval augmentation, Contrastive training, Cross-model transfer, Not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: In-Context Learning (ICL) has become a powerful mechanism for adapting Large Language Models (LLMs) to new tasks without fine-tuning. Extending this concept to Large Multimodal Models (LMMs), Multimodal In-Context Learning (M-ICL) relies on retrieving relevant examples, such as images, captions, or question-answer pairs, to guide predictions across tasks like classification, captioning, and visual question answering (VQA). Most existing approaches select in-context examples based on feature-space similarity, assuming that semantically similar samples provide the most useful context. However, our systematic analysis reveals that this assumption does not always hold: visually similar examples are not necessarily those that most effectively enhance in-context learning performance. To address this, we propose the Guided Retrieval of In-context Prompts (GRIP), a learnable vision-only retrieval framework that leverages feedback from LMMs to identify examples that truly improve model predictions. GRIP learns to distinguish beneficial from detrimental in-context examples through contrastive training, refining retrieval beyond pure similarity. Across three multimodal tasks, namely classification, captioning, and VQA, GRIP improves consistently over similarity-based retrieval on Qwen2.5-VL-7B, with its strongest gains in classification on Idefics2-8B. Moreover, we demonstrate that retrievers trained with feedback from one open LMM can be transferred to other models without retraining, including closed-source GPT-4o and Gemini, enabling scalable and cost-efficient deployment of M-ICL. Code will be published upon acceptance.

</details>


### [73] [VietFashion: Benchmarking Sketch-Text Composed Image Retrieval for Cultural Outfits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13427)
*Hoang-Nguyen Cao, Le-Hoang Bui, Dinh-Khoi Vo, Minh-Triet Tran, Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VietFashion 是一个面向越南传统服饰 Ao Dai 的草图-文本组合图像检索基准，包含 650 张手绘草图和生成式模型扩增的 21,000+ 张逼真图像，采用多目标检索设定，揭示了现有方法在细粒度文化语义建模上的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化服饰的视觉检索面临独特挑战，其身份往往依赖于微妙的结构和象征性细节，而标准 AI 模型难以捕捉这些信息。现有组合检索基准缺乏对文化服饰的细粒度建模。

Method: 构建 VietFashion 数据集：收集 650 张手绘草图（表达服装结构），从时尚杂志提取文本描述（编码文化语义），利用生成模型扩增为 21,000+ 张逼真图像并配对齐描述。采用多目标检索设定（一个查询对应多个有效结果），建立标准化评估协议，对现有组合图像检索方法进行基准测试。

Result: 实验表明现有最先进的组合图像检索方法在 VietFashion 上表现不佳，在建模细粒度文化语义和多模态组合方面存在显著性能差距，验证了该基准的挑战性。

Conclusion: VietFashion 为细粒度文化服饰检索提供了一个具有挑战性的新基准，揭示了当前多模态检索方法在文化语义理解方面的不足，有望推动该方向的研究。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 sketch-text composed image retrieval（草图-文本组合图像检索），属于跨模态组合检索任务，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on a niche cultural fashion retrieval benchmark, not aligned with user's priority on generative models or continuous representations. Signals: Cultural garment retrieval, Sketch-text composed retrieval, Multi-target retrieval, Fine-grained fashion, Not generative or world model

Abstract: Cultural garments pose a unique challenge for visual retrieval systems, as their identity often depends on subtle structural and symbolic details that are poorly captured by standard AI models. We introduce VietFashion, a new benchmark for sketch-text composed image retrieval centered on the Ao Dai, a traditional Vietnamese garment. VietFashion enables designers and researchers to retrieve culturally meaningful outfits using a combination of hand-drawn sketches, which convey garment structure, and textual descriptions, which encode cultural semantics. The dataset is initialized with 650 sketches and expanded using generative models to produce over 21,000 photorealistic images with aligned captions. Textual prompts that describe detailed outfit attributes, which are extracted from fashion magazines to ensure authenticity and diversity. To better reflect the inherent ambiguity of design intent, VietFashion adopts a multi-target retrieval setting, where a single query may correspond to multiple valid results. We establish standardized evaluation protocols and benchmark state-of-the-art composed image retrieval methods. Experimental results reveal significant performance gaps in modeling fine-grained cultural semantics and multi-modal composition, positioning VietFashion as a challenging benchmark for fine-grained fashion retrieval. The dataset is publicly available at: .

</details>

*Seongjin Kim, Sungil Kim*

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多字段混合检索增强生成（RAG）框架，利用13,329份韩国海事安全法庭报告（1971-2025）构建结构化"事故卡片"知识库，通过稀疏与稠密检索的融合（RRF）实现海事事故根因分析的自动化检索与报告生成，显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海事事故裁决报告包含关键的法庭调查结果，但人工从数十年记录中检索相关先例并撰写一致报告非常耗时费力，亟需自动化方法提升效率与一致性。

Method: 将原始裁决报告转化为结构化"事故卡片"知识库，索引三个字段（摘要、原因、处置）及层次化L1/L2原因分类；采用字段感知的混合检索策略，通过RRF融合稀疏检索（BM25）与稠密检索（嵌入模型）的排序结果；基于元数据导出的代理相关性分数评估检索性能；使用检索到的先例作为上下文输入LLM生成根因分析报告。

Result: 所提检索方法显著优于基线，NormRecall@100从0.18提升至0.55；基于检索增强的生成相比纯LLM基线，LLM-as-a-judge评分从3.34提升至3.72。

Conclusion: 字段感知的RAG框架能够有效简化海事安全调查流程，实现更快的先例搜索和更一致、基于证据的根因分析报告生成。

Classification reason: 该论文提出多字段混合检索增强生成（RAG）框架用于海事事故根因分析，核心是结构化知识库构建与多字段混合检索（稀疏+稠密融合），属于多模态/文档检索方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval）。

Importance reason: Specialized maritime RAG application with no alignment to user's priority subtopics or boost keywords. Signals: domain-specific RAG, maritime safety, no continuous language model, no video/image generation, no world model

Abstract: Maritime accident adjudication reports contain critical tribunal findings for root cause analysis (RCA), yet retrieving relevant precedents and drafting consistent reports from decades of records remains labor-intensive. This paper proposes a multi-field hybrid retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) framework for automated maritime RCA, utilizing a comprehensive dataset of 13,329 Korea Maritime Safety Tribunal (KMST) reports (1971-2025). We transform raw adjudications into a structured knowledge base of "incident cards", indexing three distinct fields-Summary, Causes, and Disposition-alongside a hierarchical L1/L2 cause taxonomy. Our retrieval strategy employs a field-aware hybrid approach, fusing sparse and dense rankings via Reciprocal Rank Fusion (RRF). Given the lack of large-scale expert relevance labels, we evaluate retrieval performance using ceiling-normalized recall and nDCG based on a metadata-derived proxy relevance score. Experimental results demonstrate that our proposed retrieval significantly outperforms baseline methods, improving NormRecall@100 from 0.18 to 0.55. Furthermore, grounding the generator on the retrieved precedents enhances RCA generation quality over an LLM-only baseline, increasing the LLM-as-a-judge score from 3.34 to 3.72. These findings suggest that field-aware RAG can substantially streamline maritime safety investigation workflows by enabling faster precedent search and more consistent, evidence-based RCA drafting.

</details>

### [75] [RoboProcessBench: Benchmarking Process-Aware Understanding in Vision-Language Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13040)
*Dayu Xia, Yue Shi, Yao Mu, Huiting Ji, Chaofan Ma, Yingjie Zhou, Hua Chen, Yang Liu, Jiezhang Cao, Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: RoboProcessBench 是一个面向视觉语言模型在机器人操作中过程感知理解能力的评测基准，包含静态监控和动态推理两个维度、12 类诊断问题、约 58k QA 对。评测发现现有 VLM 在过程感知方面存在广泛局限，而基于该数据集的后训练能带来一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在机器人操作中常被用作视觉评判器、奖励生成器或故障检测器，这些角色要求模型不仅判断最终任务成功与否，还要理解操作执行的物理和时间进程。然而现有评测未能测试 VLM 是否具备细粒度的过程理解能力。

Method: 将过程感知能力分解为静态监控和动态推理两个维度，实例化为 12 类诊断问题（涵盖阶段、接触、运动、协调、局部进度、时序顺序、结果、原始级转换等）。基于物理可执行的轨迹构建 ProcessData 语料库，包含约 58k QA 对、260 个操作任务，并拆分为 ProcessData-SFT（后训练）和 ProcessData-Eval（评测）两部分。

Result: 在 ProcessData-Eval 上对多种 VLM 的广泛评测显示，模型在 12 类诊断任务上普遍存在局限。但使用 ProcessData-SFT 后训练的 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 和 InternVL-3-8B 在局部状态、运动、进度和原始级线索上表现出一致提升。

Conclusion: RoboProcessBench 既可作为评测基准，也可作为可学习的监督来源，用于开发能够监控和评估机器人操作过程的 VLM。当前模型在过程感知理解方面仍缺乏鲁棒性，但通过针对性后训练可以改善。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 RoboProcessBench，一个用于评估 VLM 在机器人操作中过程感知能力的基准，包含 12 类诊断问题和 ~58k QA 对。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题，因为其核心贡献是构建了一个新的多模态评测基准。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotic manipulation process understanding, which is outside the user's core priority subtopics and lacks alignment with boost keywords. Signals: robotics benchmark, VLM process understanding, static monitoring, dynamic reasoning, post-training gains

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly explored as visual critics, reward generators, and failure detectors in robotic manipulation. These roles implicitly require models to judge not only final task success, but also how a manipulation execution is physically and temporally progressing. However, existing evaluations fail to test whether VLMs possess fine-grained process understanding. To address this gap, we present RoboProcessBench, a benchmark for process-aware understanding in vision-language robotic manipulation. RoboProcessBench decomposes such capability into two complementary dimensions, \emph{static monitoring} and \emph{dynamic reasoning}, instantiated as 12 diagnostic question families covering phase, contact, motion, coordination, primitive-local progress, temporal order, outcome, and primitive-level transitions. Built from physically grounded execution traces, the curated benchmark corpus ProcessData contains \textasciitilde 58k question-answer pairs across 260 manipulation tasks, which is further split into ProcessData-SFT and ProcessData-Eval for post-training and evaluation purposes. Extensive evaluation of various VLMs on ProcessData-Eval reveals broad limitations across 12 diagnostic task families, suggesting current models still lack robust process-aware understanding of manipulation executions. But with ProcessData-SFT, the post-trained \textit{Qwen2.5-VL-7B} and \textit{InternVL-3-8B} exhibit consistent gains on local state, motion, progress, and primitive-aware cues. These results demonstrate that RoboProcessBench serves as both an evaluation benchmark and a learnable supervision source for developing VLMs capable of monitoring and evaluating robotic manipulation processes. Project webpage: \href{ }{ }.

</details>


### [76] [SalArt-VQA: Diagnosing Whether VLMs Understand Salient Artifacts in Generated Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12671)
*Xiaoxiao Sun, Ruotian Zhang, Junzhe Huang, James Burgess, Serena Yeung-Levy*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SalArt-VQA 是一个诊断基准，用于细粒度评估 VLM 对 AI 生成图像中显著伪影的理解能力，包含 950 张图像和 3681 道多选题，覆盖存在检测、语义定位、空间定位和证据支持的缺陷识别四个维度。实验发现，即使检测召回率高达 99.37%，模型在所有伪影问题上同时正确的比例仅为 53.26%，揭示了高检测准确率掩盖的深层失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在检测 AI 生成图像伪影时，仅关注图像级别的正确/错误判断，但一个正确的图像级决策可能隐藏重要失败：模型可能依赖错误的视觉线索、选择错误区域或描述图像中不存在的缺陷。因此需要细粒度的诊断基准来评估 VLM 对伪影的真实理解能力。

Method: 构建 SalArt-VQA 基准，包含 950 张 AI 生成图像（含伪影）、匹配的真实参考图像和配对生成参考图像，以及 3681 道人工编写的多选题。设计四类对齐问题：存在检测、语义定位、空间定位和证据支持的缺陷识别。通过参考图像分集测试模型在伪影缺失时的校准与弃权能力。在 20 个 VLM 上进行系统评估。

Result: 最强模型在伪影图像上的检测召回率达 99.37%，但在所有四个伪影问题上同时正确的比例仅为 53.26%。对比伪影图像与无伪影参考图像揭示了敏感度-校准权衡：敏感模型常做出无依据的伪影断言，而保守模型主要通过遗漏真实伪影来避免误报。

Conclusion: 高伪影检测准确率并不等同于对伪影的扎实理解。SalArt-VQA 揭示了被图像级检测指标隐藏的失败模式，为评估 VLM 伪影主张是否得到局部视觉证据支持提供了细粒度评测工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 SalArt-VQA，一个用于细粒度评估 VLM 对 AI 生成图像中显著伪影理解能力的诊断基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM artifact understanding evaluation, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: VLM evaluation benchmark, AI-generated image artifacts, fine-grained diagnostic, detection accuracy vs. understanding gap, sensitivity-calibration tradeoff

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used to detect whether AI-generated images contain visible artifacts, yet their ability to analyze such artifacts remains poorly understood. A correct image-level decision can still hide important failures: a model may correctly flag an artifact while relying on the wrong visual cue, selecting the wrong region, or describing a defect that the image does not support. To evaluate these behaviors directly, we introduce SalArt-VQA, a diagnostic benchmark for fine-grained SALient ARTifact understanding in AI-generated images. SalArt-VQA contains 950 images and 3,681 human-authored multiple-choice questions spanning artifact images, matched real reference images, and paired generated reference images. Four aligned question types evaluate presence detection, semantic localization, spatial grounding, and evidence-grounded defect identification, while the reference splits test calibration and abstention when the annotated defect is absent. Across 20 VLMs, SalArt-VQA reveals failures that image-level detection accuracy hides: the strongest model reaches 99.37% detection recall on artifact images but answers all four artifact-side questions correctly on only 53.26% of images. Comparing artifact images with artifact-free references reveals a sensitivity-calibration tradeoff: sensitive models often make unsupported artifact claims, while conservative models avoid false alarms largely by missing real artifacts. These results show that high artifact detection accuracy alone does not imply grounded artifact understanding. SalArt-VQA exposes these hidden failure modes and provides a fine-grained evaluation of whether VLM artifact claims are supported by local visual evidence.

</details>


### [77] [VLADriveBench: Evaluating CoT-Action Relationship in VLA for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12706)
*Thach Nguyen, Danhua Guo, Tom Lampo, Fei Wu, Burhan Yaman*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (49.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VLADriveBench 是一个评估 VLA 模型中思维链（CoT）与驾驶动作之间关系的基准框架，结合观测指标（提及、幻觉、矛盾、动作对齐）和 CoT 干预协议，发现不同模型的 CoT 与动作的因果关联程度差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型基准仅评估轨迹质量，不评估 CoT 推理是否与驾驶动作相关、一致或具有因果关系，因此需要专门的评测框架来揭示 CoT 与动作之间的关系。

Method: 提出 VLADriveBench 框架，包含两类互补分析：(1) 观测指标——提及率、幻觉率、矛盾率、动作对齐度；(2) CoT 干预协议——通过修改/移除 CoT 观察对最终动作的影响，判断 CoT 是否具有因果作用。在 ORION 和 Alpamayo v1.5 等模型上进行评测。

Result: ORION 在观测指标上得分最高，但其 CoT 是附带现象（epiphenomenal），即移除 CoT 不影响动作；Alpamayo v1.5 观测得分较低，但其 CoT 具有强因果性，视觉显著性调节了 CoT 的影响程度。

Conclusion: VLA 模型的 CoT 质量不能仅靠观测指标衡量，需要结合因果干预分析才能全面评估 CoT 与动作的真实关系；视觉显著性在 CoT 因果性中起关键作用。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 VLADriveBench，一个专门评估 VLA 模型中 CoT 推理与驾驶动作之间关系的新基准框架，属于多模态评测与基准方向。虽然涉及自动驾驶，但其核心贡献是评测协议和基准，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLA CoT evaluation for autonomous driving, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: VLA benchmark, CoT-action relationship, autonomous driving, observational metrics, intervention protocol

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models generate chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning alongside driving trajectories, but existing benchmarks evaluate only trajectory quality and do not assess whether the CoT is relevant, consistent, or causally connected to the driving action. We introduce VLADriveBench, a framework that combines observational metrics (mentioning, hallucination, contradiction, action alignment) with a CoT intervention protocol to provide complementary views of the CoT-action relationship. Applying VLADriveBench to three models across two architectures, we find that the two analyses can diverge sharply: ORION scores highest on observational alignment yet its CoT is epiphenomenal, while Alpamayo v1.5 scores lower yet its CoT is strongly causal, with visual salience gating the extent of CoT influence.

</details>

*Charles Moslonka, Amaury de Vitry, Arthur Garnier, Hicham Randrianarivo, Emmanuel Malherbe*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (49.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LEDGER 是一个包含 4,999 份数字化企业年报的长上下文评测基准，涵盖 31 个财务 KPI 的检索与提取任务，包含 118,048 个自然语言查询、对话式"大海捞针"任务和完整 KPI 提取任务，并提供人工 OCR 质量标注和完整工具链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融 NLP 评测大多局限于 SEC 10-K 纯文本和少量问答对，缺乏对长上下文、数值密集、包含图表表格的真实年报文档的系统评测，无法充分评估大模型在金融领域的实际能力。

Method: 收集 4,999 份数字化企业年报（含图表、表格和叙述文本），标注 31 个财务 KPI，构建三个评测任务：(1) 页面级 KPI 检索（118,048 个 TREC 风格查询）；(2) 对话式"大海捞针"单值查找；(3) 完整 KPI 提取。提供人工 OCR 质量标注、标注者间一致性评估及完整工具链。

Result: 构建了大规模长上下文金融评测基准，展示了 CEO 信函修辞与市场影响之间的关联案例研究，验证了数据集的研究实用性。

Conclusion: LEDGER 填补了金融领域长上下文评测的空白，为评估大模型在真实年报文档上的检索与提取能力提供了标准化基准，有助于推动金融 NLP 研究。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向金融年报的长上下文检索与提取评测基准（LEDGER），属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。虽然论文聚焦于金融文本（非典型多模态），但评测基准涉及表格、图表、数字等混合内容，且评测任务（KPI检索、提取）属于多模态文档理解范畴，与现有 canonical 子主题合理匹配。

Importance reason: Strong financial NLP benchmark but low alignment with user's focus on continuous multimodal models and generative world models. Signals: Long-context financial benchmark, 4999 annual reports, 31 KPI extraction, OCR quality annotations, CEO-letter market impact case study

Abstract: Finance reporting is a natural proving ground for large language models, and the very-long-context capabilities of recent models across all sizes make rigorous evaluation in this domain an increasingly pressing need. Yet most public financial resources reduce the task to plain-text SEC 10-K filings paired with a handful of question-answer items. We release LEDGER (Long-context Evaluation of Documents for Grounded Extraction and Retrieval), a corpus of 4,999 digitized corporate annual reports - full documents with figures, tables, and narrative, not just regulatory filings. Each report is labeled with 31 consolidated financial KPIs to be extracted and linked to the market's reaction at the earnings date. From this data we derive three evaluation benchmarks spanning the difficulty spectrum: a pure page-level KPI retrieval task with TREC-style relevance judgments over 118,048 questions in natural language, a conversational "needle-in-a-haystack" single-value lookup, and a full KPI extraction task, both from long, numerically dense reports. We additionally provide human OCR-quality annotations with inter-annotator agreement and the complete extraction, validation, and scoring toolchain. We further demonstrate the dataset's research utility with a case study linking CEO-letter rhetoric to post-publication market impact.

</details>


### [79] [MLUBench: A Benchmark for Lifelong Unlearning Evaluation in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12809)
*He Li, Haoang Chi, Qizhou Wang, Yunxin Mao, Zhiheng Zhang, Jie Tan, Tongliang Liu, Wenjing Yang, Bo Han*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (73.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MLUBench是一个大规模、全面的MLLM终身遗忘学习评测基准，包含127个实体、9个类别。实验发现现有遗忘方法存在严重的累积退化问题，且MLLM终身遗忘面临独特挑战：持续遗忘一个模态会破坏多模态对齐。作者提出LUMoE方法有效缓解了退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型（MLLM）训练于海量多模态数据，数据所有者可能随时间顺序请求删除特定内容（终身遗忘学习）。然而现有基准规模小、范围有限，无法捕捉MLLM终身遗忘学习的复杂性，亟需一个大规模、全面的评测基准来推动该领域研究。

Method: 构建MLUBench基准（127个实体、9个类别），系统评测多种现有遗忘方法在MLLM终身遗忘场景下的表现。分析发现持续遗忘一个模态会导致多模态对齐退化。提出LUMoE方法，通过专家混合架构缓解遗忘过程中的累积退化问题。

Result: 现有遗忘方法在MLUBench上表现出严重的累积退化；LUMoE显著缓解了基线方法的退化问题，在保持遗忘效果的同时更好地维持了模型的多模态对齐能力。

Conclusion: MLLM终身遗忘学习是一个具有独特挑战的问题（多模态对齐约束），现有方法难以应对。MLUBench为这一方向提供了标准化评测平台，LUMoE展示了有效的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了MLUBench，一个面向多模态大模型（MLLM）的终身遗忘学习评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向。该论文与[多模态评测与基准]（multimodal_benchmark）这一CANONICAL subtopic高度匹配，因此直接复用该分类。

Importance reason: Focuses on MLLM unlearning, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks boost keywords. Signals: MLLM lifelong unlearning benchmark, cumulative degradation problem, LUMoE method, multimodal alignment challenge, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are trained on massive multimodal data, making data unlearning increasingly important as data owners may request the removal of specific content. In practice, these requests often arrive sequentially over time, giving rise to the challenging problem of MLLM Lifelong Unlearning. However, most existing benchmarks are limited in scale and scope, failing to capture the complexities of MLLM lifelong unlearning. To fill this gap, we introduce the MLUBench, a large-scale and comprehensive benchmark featuring 127 entities across 9 classes under lifelong unlearning requests. We perform extensive experiments using MLUBench and reveal that existing unlearning methods suffer from severe, cumulative degradation. More critically, we further identify the unique challenge of this problem: unlike in unimodal models, MLLM lifelong unlearning is constrained by the need to preserve multimodal alignment. Continually unlearning from one modality could degrade the entire model. To alleviate this challenge, we propose LUMoE, an effective method. Experiments demonstrate that LUMoE significantly mitigates the degradation problem faced by baselines. The source code and the MLUBench dataset are open-sourced in .

</details>


### [80] [GeoNatureAgent Benchmark: Benchmarking LLM Agents for Environmental Geospatial Analysis Across Frontier and Open-Weight Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12821)
*Gabriel Diaz-Ireland, Diego Prieto-Herráez, Mario García Peces, Javier Velázquez, Devika Jain*

Main category: cs.ET, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (75.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GeoNatureAgent Benchmark是首个评估LLM智能体通过结构化工具调用真实地理空间API进行环境分析的基准，包含93个任务、18个类别，评测了7个LLM。Claude Sonnet 4以60.8%领先，DeepSeek V3.2以11倍低成本达到93%的性能，比较类任务普遍无法解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境科学家在数据整理上花费了大量精力而非分析本身，但能够自动化地理空间工作流的AI智能体尚未得到有效验证——目前没有基准能够评估通过结构化工具调用真实API的智能体。

Method: 构建了包含93个任务、18个类别的GeoNatureAgent Benchmark，覆盖市镇分析、多轮对话、空间推理、跨指标综合、错误处理与恢复、排序、比较、多语言理解、栖息地分析和任务拒绝等。通过一个自托管的API提供西班牙和葡萄牙的三种环境指标及16个工具。在7个LLM上以温度1.0的三个种子进行评测，报告能力和单例成本。

Result: (1) Claude Sonnet 4以60.8%±0.8%领先，DeepSeek V3.2以56.3%±3.1%紧随其后，其余模型均低于51%；(2) 成本-准确率Pareto前沿主要由开源模型占据，DeepSeek V3.2以11倍低成本（$0.011/例）达到Claude 93%的能力；(3) 比较类任务普遍无法解决（接近值比较准确率为0%）；(4) 结构化工具调用比通用GIS基准更具区分度，准确率低25-35个百分点。

Conclusion: GeoNatureAgent Benchmark填补了环境地理空间分析智能体评测的空白，揭示了当前LLM在结构化工具调用和比较推理方面的系统性局限，并展示了开源模型在成本效益上的优势。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了GeoNatureAgent Benchmark，是首个面向环境地理空间分析LLM智能体的评测基准，属于多模态/智能体评测基准范畴，与[多模态评测与基准]（multimodal_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on geospatial environmental analysis agents, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and contains negative keywords. Signals: environmental geospatial analysis, LLM agent benchmark, structured tool calling, comparison tasks unsolved, open-weight models cost-efficient

Abstract: Environmental scientists spend disproportionate effort on data wrangling rather than analysis, and AI agents that automate geospatial workflows remain unvalidated: no benchmark evaluates agents operating through structured tool calling against real APIs. We introduce the GeoNatureAgent Benchmark, the first benchmark for environmental analysis agents that operate via structured tool calls to a production-style geospatial API. It comprises 93 tasks across 18 categories, covering municipality analysis, multi-turn conversation, spatial reasoning, cross-indicator synthesis, error handling and recovery, ranking, comparison, multilingual understanding, habitat analysis, and task rejection. Tasks are evaluated against an open, self-hostable API serving three environmental indicators across Spain and Portugal via sixteen tools. We evaluate seven LLMs (Claude Sonnet 4, DeepSeek V3.2, GLM-5, Gemini 2.5 Pro, Qwen3-235B, GPT-OSS-120B, Llama 4 Scout) under three temperature-1.0 seeds, reporting capability and per-case cost as orthogonal axes. We find: (1) Claude Sonnet 4 leads at 60.8% +/- 0.8%, followed by DeepSeek V3.2 at 56.3% +/- 3.1%, with no other model above 51%; (2) the cost-accuracy Pareto frontier is occupied mostly by open-weight models, with DeepSeek V3.2 offering 93% of Claude's capability at 11x lower cost ($0.011/case); (3) comparison tasks remain universally unsolved (0% on close-value comparisons), exposing systematic reasoning limits; and (4) structured tool calling against a real API is more discriminative than general-purpose GIS benchmarks, with accuracies 25-35 points lower. We further show extensibility by integrating BigEarthNet V2 land cover for Portugal alongside Spanish CO2 and erosion indicators. The benchmark, harness, and self-hostable API are publicly available.

</details>


### [81] [ArogyaSutra: A Multi-Agent Framework for Multimodal Medical Reasoning in Indic Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13572)
*Tanmoy Kanti Halder, Akash Ghosh, Subhadip Baidya, Arijit Roy, Sriparna Saha*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ArogyaSutra，一个基于 actor-critic 的多智能体框架，结合工具调用与双记忆机制，用于多语言（含7种印度语言）多模态医学推理。同时构建了大规模多语言多模态医学问答数据集 ArogyaBodha，覆盖31个身体系统、6种成像模态和21个临床领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在通用领域表现良好，但在医疗等专业场景中能力有限，尤其缺乏对多语言和低资源语言的支持。印度农村地区患者常使用本地语言表达复杂医疗问题并依赖医学影像，而现有以英语为中心的 MLLM 难以满足这些需求。

Method: 构建 ArogyaBodha 数据集（从8个异构来源收集，覆盖英语和7种印度语言）；提出 ArogyaSutra 多智能体框架，包含 actor-critic 架构、工具调用（tool grounding）、双记忆机制（dual-memory）用于逐步推理决策，并利用存储的 actor-critic 模拟轨迹进行蒸馏训练。

Result: 实验表明，该数据集和框架在所有印度语言上均提升了多语言医学推理准确率，消融实验验证了各组件的贡献。

Conclusion: ArogyaSutra 框架和 ArogyaBodha 数据集有效提升了多语言多模态医学推理能力，为低资源语言场景下的 AI 医疗辅助提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了多语言多模态医学问答数据集 ArogyaBodha，并提出了多智能体推理框架 ArogyaSutra。核心贡献之一是数据集与评测基准，属于 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准 范畴。

Importance reason: Focuses on multilingual medical reasoning, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: Multilingual medical MLLM, multi-agent framework, low-resource language focus, medical QA dataset, not aligned with user's core interests

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown promising reasoning capabilities in general domains, yet their performance remains limited in specialized settings such as healthcare, especially in multilingual and low-resource scenarios. This gap is critical in regions like rural India, where patients often express complex medical queries in native Indic languages and rely on multimodal inputs such as medical images. Existing English-centric MLLMs struggle to support such use cases, limiting equitable access to AI-driven healthcare assistance. To address this challenge, we introduce ArogyaBodha, a large-scale multilingual multimodal medical question-answer dataset constructed from eight heterogeneous sources, covering 31 body systems, six imaging modalities, and 21 clinical domains across English and seven major Indian languages. We further propose ArogyaSutra, an actor-critic-based multi-agent framework that integrates tool grounding with dual-memory mechanisms for step-wise, reasoning-aware decision making, and uses stored actor-critic simulation trajectories for distillation. Experiments show that our dataset and framework improve multilingual medical reasoning accuracy across all Indic languages, with ablations validating the contribution of each component. The source code and dataset are available at: ArogyaSutra/

</details>


### [82] [DailyReport: An Open-ended Benchmark for Evaluating Search Agents on Daily Search Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12871)
*Jingxuan Han, Wei Liu, Mingyang Zhu, Youpeng Wang, Ziwen Wang, Lin Qiu, Xuezhi Cao, Xunliang Cai, Zheren Fu, Licheng Zhang, Zhendong Mao*

TL;DR: DailyReport 是一个面向日常搜索任务的开放式评测基准，包含150个开放任务和3546个关联评分标准，通过级联性能归因和用户中心聚合提供可解释的维度评分和用户偏好评分。在17个智能体系统上的评估表明当前系统尚未达到用户期望。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索智能体评测基准主要关注专业任务，难以反映真实用户场景，且依赖粗粒度的任务级评分标准，限制了评估的可解释性。作者希望构建一个更贴近真实用户日常搜索需求、且能提供细粒度可解释评估的基准。

Method: 构建了包含150个开放式任务的 DailyReport 基准，每个任务分解为子任务，并设计了3546个级联评分标准（cascade rubrics），从多个解耦维度进行评估。通过级联性能归因（cascade performance attribution）和用户中心聚合（user-centric aggregation）方法，为每个维度计算可解释的分数以及用户偏好分数。

Result: 在17个智能体系统上的评估结果显示，当前搜索智能体系统在真实日常搜索任务上的表现仍远未达到用户期望。

Conclusion: DailyReport 提供了一个更贴近真实用户需求的搜索智能体评测基准，揭示了现有系统的不足，并为未来搜索智能体的研究提供了公开的数据集和代码。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向搜索智能体（Search Agents）的开放式评测基准 DailyReport，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。虽然论文聚焦于搜索任务而非传统多模态理解，但评测基准的设计范式（开放任务、级联评估、可解释评分）与多模态评测高度相关，且该子主题是 [CANONICAL] 条目，优先复用。

Importance reason: Benchmark for search agents is not aligned with user's priority on generative models and continuous representations. Signals: search agents evaluation, daily search tasks, open-ended benchmark, not generative model, not continuous language model

Abstract: Search Agents (SAs) typically leverage large language models (LLMs) to support complex information-seeking tasks by autonomously exploring web sources and synthesizing information into comprehensive responses. For SAs evaluation, prior benchmarks mainly focus on specialized tasks that are unlikely to arise in real-world user scenarios. Moreover, their reliance on coarse task-level rubrics often limits evaluation interpretability. To bridge this gap, we introduce DailyReport, an open-ended benchmark to evaluate SA capabilities on daily search tasks. It contains 150 open-ended tasks with 3,546 associated rubrics, capturing widely discussed and timely information demands of real-world users. Each task is decomposed into subtasks and evaluated with cascade rubrics across disentangled dimensions. Through cascade performance attribution and user-centric aggregation, we derive highly interpretable scores for each dimension, along with a user preference score. Our results on 17 agentic systems show that current systems still fall short of users' expectations. To facilitate future research, our dataset and code are made publicly available at .

</details>


### [83] [PRISMR: Overcoming Parse Collapse in Multimodal Listwise Ranking via Parameterized Representation Internalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12942)
*Hao Jiang, Xin Li, Annan Wang, Zhi Yang, Haoxiang Zhang, Yichi Zhang, Weisi Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (60.21) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PRISMR 提出了一种参数化表示内化框架，通过轻量级超网络并行编码多模态候选并生成实例特定的 LoRA 权重，解决了多模态列表排序中自回归解码器因上下文利用不足导致的"解析崩溃"问题，并引入大规模多模态评论排序基准验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式列表排序（listwise ranking）在大语言模型/多模态大模型中通过单次前向传播捕获全局列表上下文，但在长上下文多模态场景下性能严重下降。作者发现了一种称为"解析崩溃"（parse collapse）的失败模式——自回归解码器生成流畅但不完整的排序，静默省略候选并提前终止。现有提示工程和约束解码方法不足以解决该问题。

Method: PRISMR 框架用参数化结构条件替代瞬态上下文列表处理。具体地，使用轻量级超网络并行编码多模态候选，生成每个候选特定的 LoRA 权重，然后将这些权重合成为实例特定的适配器注入 LMM。该范式使模型能够更鲁棒地内化列表结构，同时保留基础模型能力。

Result: 实验表明 PRISMR 显著减少了解析崩溃，提升了列表排序性能，并且能够有效跨领域和跨指令微调骨干网络迁移。

Conclusion: PRISMR 通过参数化表示内化有效解决了多模态列表排序中的解析崩溃问题，为长上下文多模态排序提供了一种新的范式，且具有良好的跨领域迁移能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个多模态列表排序（listwise ranking）框架 PRISMR，并引入了一个大规模多模态评论排序评测基准。论文核心贡献之一是评测基准，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）。

Importance reason: Addresses a specific failure mode in multimodal ranking but is not aligned with the user's priority subtopics on continuous models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: Multimodal listwise ranking, Parse collapse, Parameterized representation internalization, LoRA adapter, New benchmark

Abstract: Generative listwise ranking with Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) aims to capture global list context in a single forward pass, but its effectiveness degrades in long-context multimodal scenarios. We identify a recurring failure mode, parse collapse, where the autoregressive decoder produces fluent yet incomplete rankings by silently omitting candidates and terminating early. This failure stems from limited context utilization rather than simple formatting mistakes, making prompt engineering and constrained decoding insufficient. We propose PRISMR (Parameterized Representation Internalization for Semantic Multimodal Ranking), a framework that replaces transient in-context list processing with parametric structural conditioning. PRISMR uses a lightweight hypernetwork to encode multimodal candidates in parallel and generate item-specific LoRA weights, which are synthesized into an instance-specific adapter for a LMM. This paradigm enables more robust internalization of list structure while preserving the base model. We further introduce a large-scale multimodal review-ranking benchmark for evaluation. Experiments demonstrate that PRISMR substantially reduces parse collapse, improves listwise ranking performance, and transfers effectively across domains and instruction-tuned backbones.

</details>


### [84] [Multi-Modal Agents for Power Distribution Defect Detection: An Evaluation of Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12969)
*Quan Quan*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (50.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多模态智能体框架用于电力配电缺陷检测，系统评估了多模态基础模型在感知、推理和工具使用三个关键能力上的表现，并构建了领域特定的评测数据集和基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电力配电网络巡检方法在语义理解、泛化能力和闭环自动化方面存在局限性，亟需引入多模态基础模型作为统一认知引擎来提升缺陷检测的智能化水平。

Method: 提出Multi-Modal Agent框架，将多模态基础模型作为核心认知引擎，在三个维度进行评估：(1) 感知——识别设备并生成专家级缺陷描述；(2) 推理——基于视觉发现诊断原因、评估严重性并规划维护策略；(3) 工具使用——自主执行查询知识库、生成工单等操作实现闭环维护。同时构建了领域特定的评测数据集和基准。

Result: 实验展示了当前多模态基础模型在感知、推理和工具使用三个维度上的优势与局限性，为在高风险工业环境中部署自主智能体提供了实证依据。

Conclusion: 多模态基础模型在电力配电缺陷检测中展现出潜力，但在感知精度、领域推理深度和工具使用自主性方面仍有提升空间，未来需要进一步优化模型在工业场景中的综合能力。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了面向电力配电缺陷检测的多模态智能体框架，并系统评估了多模态基础模型在感知、推理和工具使用三个维度的能力，同时开发了领域特定的评测数据集和基准。核心贡献在于评测与基准构建，因此映射到[多模态评测与基准]（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on power distribution defect detection, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and contains negative keywords. Signals: domain-specific application, power distribution, defect detection, multimodal agent evaluation, benchmark

Abstract: The power distribution network is critical to reliable electricity delivery, yet traditional inspection methods face limitations in semantic understanding, generalization, and closed-loop automation. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a Multi-Modal Agent framework specifically for power distribution defect detection. Central to this study is the systematic evaluation of multimodal foundation models as unified cognitive engines. We rigorously assess their integrated performance across three critical capabilities: (1) Perception, where the model must accurately identify equipment and generate expert-level descriptions of defects; (2) Reasoning, where the model interprets visual findings to diagnose causes, assess severity, and plan maintenance strategies based on domain knowledge; and (3) Tool Usage, where the model acts as an autonomous operator to execute actions -- such as querying knowledge bases or generating work orders -- to achieve closed-loop maintenance. To support this evaluation, a domain-specific evaluation dataset and a comprehensive benchmark are developed. Experimental results demonstrate the strengths and limitations of current foundation models in these three dimensions, providing empirical evidence for deploying autonomous agents in high-stakes industrial environments.

</details>


### [85] [SciR: A Controllable Benchmark for Scientific Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13020)
*Pierre Beckmann, Marco Valentino, Andre Freitas*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (79.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SciR 是一个面向 LLM 科学推理的可控基准，覆盖演绎、归纳和因果溯因三种推理范式，通过从形式化对象生成任务并渲染为多文档科学语篇，实现对信息提取难度和推理难度两个维度的独立控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学推理基准要么依赖人工标注（成本高、缺乏机制性真值），要么是合成逻辑推理基准（不类似真实科学文献），缺乏同时具备可控性和真实性的多范式科学推理评测。

Method: 从形式化对象（演绎树、归纳规则假设、因果图）生成可验证答案的任务，再通过每个轨道的领域调优体裁渲染为多文档科学语篇，独立控制提取难度和推理难度两个轴。

Result: 测试了六个模型，两个难度轴均对模型性能产生负面影响且效应叠加；渲染甚至损害了神经符号流水线；推理模型（如 deepseek-r1）在推理轴上优于非推理指令模型。

Conclusion: SciR 是首个具有提取和推理难度参数化控制的多范式科学推理基准，揭示了当前 LLM 在科学推理中提取与推理能力之间的权衡。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 SciR，一个面向 LLM 科学推理的可控评测基准，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。虽然论文主要关注文本推理而非多模态，但 benchmark 类工作映射到该 canonical 子主题最为合适。

Importance reason: Focuses on scientific reasoning benchmarks, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: scientific reasoning benchmark, controllable difficulty axes, multi-paradigm reasoning, not aligned with user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Three paradigmatic forms of inference recur across scientific reasoning: deduction, induction, and causal abduction. Reliably evaluating LLMs on these in scientific settings is currently out of reach: scientific benchmarks built on human annotations are costly and lack mechanistic ground truth, while synthetic logical-reasoning benchmarks do not resemble real scientific documents. We introduce SciR, a benchmark that combines multi-paradigm reasoning with controllable scientific rendering, anchored on three paradigmatic scientific problems. Tasks are generated from formal objects (deduction tree, inductive rule hypothesis, causal graph) to guarantee verifiable answers, then rendered into multi-document scientific discourse via per-track domain-tuned genres. The construction lets us independently vary two difficulty axes: how hard it is to extract the key information needed for inference, and how hard the principled inference itself is. We test six models. Both axes hurt every model, and their effects compound. The rendering even hurts neurosymbolic pipelines, which hand inference to a verified solver. The two axes yield a per-model extraction-vs-inference profile: for instance, reasoning models like deepseek-r1 mostly surpass non-reasoning instruct models on the inference axis. To our knowledge, SciR is the first multi-paradigm scientific-reasoning benchmark with parametric control on both extraction and inference difficulty.

</details>


### [86] [Rethinking RAG in Long Videos: What to Retrieve and How to Use It?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13141)
*Yuho Lee, Jisu Shin, Nicole Hee-Yeon Kim, Jihwan Bang, Juntae Lee, Kyuwoong Hwang, Fatih Porikli, Hwanjun Song*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文针对长视频中的检索增强生成（VideoRAG）提出两个贡献：一是 V-RAGBench 基准，通过 ⟨query, evidence chunk, answer⟩ 三元组实现检索与生成的解耦评估；二是 CARVE 方法，通过并行多配置检索器与块自适应重排序，为每个证据块选择最优配置，生成交错式多配置证据，在 VideoRAG 任务上超越八种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VideoRAG 研究存在两个关键局限：1）现有基准中查询可以在不依赖视频的情况下被回答，掩盖了检索错误；2）现有方法对每个查询使用单一的模态-粒度配置，忽略了不同证据块之间的差异性。因此需要更可靠的评测基准和更灵活的检索策略。

Method: 1）构建 V-RAGBench 基准，包含 ⟨query, evidence chunk, answer⟩ 三元组，确保查询必须依赖视频证据才能回答，实现检索与生成的解耦评估。2）提出 CARVE 方法，并行运行多个不同配置（不同模态、不同时间粒度）的检索器，然后通过块自适应重排序（chunk-adaptive reranking）为每个证据块选择最优配置，最终生成交错式多配置证据供生成器使用。

Result: CARVE 在 V-RAGBench 上超越八种近期 VideoRAG 基线方法。分析表明，CARVE 为生成器提供的证据块来自多种配置的交错组合，而非单一配置，这种块级决策行为是查询级方法无法实现的。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了 VideoRAG 中检索配置选择的重要性，证明了块级自适应配置选择优于查询级统一配置。V-RAGBench 为 VideoRAG 提供了更可靠的评测平台，CARVE 为长视频 RAG 提供了一种简单有效的多配置融合方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 V-RAGBench，一个面向长视频检索增强生成（VideoRAG）的新评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark and method for VideoRAG, but the subtopic (multimodal evaluation/benchmark) is not a priority and lacks direct connection to user's focus on continuous models or generation. Signals: VideoRAG benchmark, retrieval-generation decoupling, chunk-adaptive reranking, long video understanding, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation is moving beyond text into long, egocentric video, where systems must select query-relevant chunks across multiple modalities and temporal granularities. Yet progress in VideoRAG is limited by two gaps: existing benchmarks allow queries to be answered without the video, obscuring retrieval errors, and prior methods apply a single modality-granularity configuration per query, ignoring chunk-level variability. We address both by introducing V-RAGBench, a benchmark of $\langle$query, evidence chunk, answer$\rangle$ triplets that enables faithful, decoupled evaluation of retrieval and generation, and CARVE, a simple method that runs parallel retrievers across configurations and employs chunk-adaptive reranking to identify the winning configuration for each chunk. Each chunk then enters the generator under its winning configuration selected during retrieval, yielding an interleaved evidence form where the chunk-level decision propagates across both stages. CARVE outperforms eight recent VideoRAG baselines, with the chunks supplied to the generator interleaving multiple configurations rather than sharing a single one, a behavior unattainable by query-level methods.

</details>


### [87] [Reasoning for Mobile User Experience with Multimodal LLMs: Task, Benchmark, and Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13192)
*Ruichao Mao, Zhou Fang, Teng Guo, Hao Yang, Yaping Li, Shaohua Peng, Maji Huang, Xiaoyu Lin, Shuoyang Liu, Xuepeng Li, Yuyu Zhang, Hai Rao*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 UXBench，一个包含 2000 个 VQA 样本的多模态基准，用于评估 MLLM 在 UI 用户体验推理方面的能力。同时提出 UI-UX 模型，基于 Qwen3-VL-4B-Thinking 并通过强化学习增强，引入奖励路由机制和非对称过渡奖励，在 UXBench 上达到 SOTA（0.7963），超越 Claude-4.5-Sonnet（0.6550）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在 UI 领域的应用（如 GUI 代理、设计转代码）发展迅速，但基于 UI 截图评估用户体验（UX）的研究仍不成熟，缺乏专门的评测基准和方法。

Method: 构建 UXBench 基准（2000 个 VQA 样本，8 个任务，覆盖布局关系、视觉层次、内容一致性）；提出 UI-UX 模型，基于 Qwen3-VL-4B-Thinking，通过强化学习训练，包含奖励路由机制（动态平衡感知理解与逻辑推理）和非对称过渡奖励（抑制冗余或不足推理步骤）。

Result: UI-UX 在 UXBench 上达到 0.7963 准确率，超越 Claude-4.5-Sonnet（0.6550），并在多种 UI 任务上展现出强泛化能力和低推理延迟。

Conclusion: 主流 MLLM 在 UI 用户体验推理方面仍存在根本性局限，本文提出的基准和方法为该方向提供了有效的评测工具和性能提升方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 UXBench，一个针对 UI 用户体验推理的多模态评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Focuses on UI UX reasoning, which is a niche application of MLLMs, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Multimodal benchmark for UI reasoning, RL-enhanced MLLM with reward routing, SOTA on UXBench but narrow domain, no overlap with priority subtopics or boost keywords

Abstract: User experience (UX) centered on usability, perceived consistency, and functional clarity is fundamental to real-world user interfaces (UI). The application of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) in the field of user interfaces is evolving rapidly, such as visual element grounding, graphical user interface (GUI) agents, and design-to-code generation. However, research efforts on evaluating UX based on UI screenshots are still immature. To address this, we propose UXBench, a novel multimodal benchmark consisting of 2,000 VQA data samples designed to assess MLLMs' ability to perform UI-based reasoning. UXBench includes 8 tasks based on real-world UI screenshots that require fine-grained diagnosis of UX issues across layout relationships, visual hierarchy, and content consistency. Our extensive evaluation of mainstream MLLMs shows that they remain fundamentally limited in their capacity for UI-based reasoning. The results underscore the need for further advancements in this area. To bridge this gap, we propose UI-UX, an MLLM based on Qwen3-VL-4B-Thinking foundation model and enhanced via reinforcement learning with two key innovations: a reward routing mechanism that dynamically balances perceptual understanding and logical reasoning during inference, and an asymmetric transition reward that suppresses redundant or insufficient reasoning steps. Experiments demonstrate that UI-UX achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance on UXBench, attaining an accuracy of 0.7963 -- surpassing Claude-4.5-Sonnet's 0.6550 -- while exhibiting strong generalization across diverse UI tasks and maintaining low inference latency.

</details>


### [88] [GeoDial: A Multimodal Conversational Tutoring Dataset for Geometry Problem-Solving with Visual Tutor Turns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12419)
*Sankalan Pal Chowdhury, Junling Wang, Donya Rooein, April Yi Wang, Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CY

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GeoDial 是一个包含1300+教师-学生对话的多模态几何辅导数据集，对话中的教学轮次显式地基于图表高亮进行。论文提出了可扩展的标注协议，整合了对话行为、视觉高亮和反馈。微调VLM的结果表明，模型在生成对话文本方面有显著提升，但在生成准确的图表高亮方面仍存在困难，揭示了当前方法在视觉推理与教学交互整合方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有辅导数据集大多局限于纯文本交互，而几何等学科严重依赖图表和视觉线索。为了开发能够像人类教师一样进行视觉化教学的AI辅导系统，需要一个包含视觉高亮的多模态对话数据集。

Method: 从经验丰富的数学教师处收集了1300+几何辅导对话，设计了可扩展的标注协议，整合对话行为（dialog acts）、视觉高亮（diagram highlights）和反馈标注。在此基础上微调了多个视觉语言模型（VLM），评估其在生成辅导话语和图表高亮方面的能力。

Result: 监督微调显著提升了生成对话的质量，但模型在生成准确的图表高亮方面表现不佳，揭示了当前VLM在视觉推理与教学交互整合方面的关键局限性。

Conclusion: GeoDial 为多模态AI辅导系统提供了有价值的基准，但现有VLM在生成准确的视觉高亮方面仍有不足，需要更有效的方法来整合视觉推理与教学交互。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个几何问题求解的多模态对话辅导数据集 GeoDial，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）范畴。该数据集包含教师-学生对话、图表高亮标注和反馈，用于训练和评估VLM在视觉辅导场景中的表现，符合该子主题的定义。

Importance reason: Focuses on geometry tutoring dataset, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: multimodal tutoring dataset, geometry problem-solving, visual tutor turns, VLM fine-tuning, diagram highlighting

Abstract: Several educational domains rely heavily on diagrams and visual cues, yet most existing tutoring datasets are limited to text-only interactions. This limits the development of AI tutors that can teach in visually grounded ways used by human instructors. Thus, we introduce GeoDial, a multimodal tutoring dataset of over 1.3K teacher-student dialogs in the domain of geometry collected from experienced math teachers, where instructional turns are explicitly grounded in diagram highlights. We propose a scalable annotation protocol that integrates dialog acts, visual highlighting, and feedback, enabling fine-grained supervision of both language and visual tutoring behavior. To illustrate the challenges posed by this setting, we fine-tune several vision-language models on GeoDial and evaluate their ability to generate tutoring utterances and diagram highlights. While supervised fine-tuning substantially improves the quality of generated dialog, it struggles to produce accurate diagram highlights, revealing a key limitation of current methods and highlighting the need for approaches that more effectively integrate visual reasoning with pedagogical interaction.

</details>

## 多模态高效化与轻量化 [[Back]](#toc)

高效 MLLM、PEFT、蒸馏、量化、token 压缩、稀疏推理。

### [89] [MiniMax Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13392)
*Xunhao Lai, Weiqi Xu, Yufeng Yang, Qiaorui Chen, Yang Xu, Lunbin Zeng, Xiaolong Li, Haohai Sun, Haichao Zhu, Vito Zhang, Pengyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (66.28) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MiniMax 提出 MSA（MiniMax Sparse Attention），一种基于 GQA 的块级稀疏注意力机制，通过轻量级 Index Branch 为每个 GQA 组独立选择 Top-k KV 块，配合 co-designed GPU kernel 实现高效块稀疏注意力。在 109B 原生多模态模型上，MSA 在 1M 上下文长度下将注意力计算量减少 28.4 倍，实现 14.2 倍 prefill 和 7.6 倍 decoding 加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超长上下文能力对前沿 LLM 至关重要（如 agent 工作流、仓库级代码推理、持久记忆），但 softmax attention 的二次复杂度使得在部署规模上难以承受。现有稀疏注意力方法在训练效率、部署友好性和实际加速方面仍有不足，亟需一种简单、可扩展且能有效利用 GPU 的稀疏注意力方案。

Method: MSA 基于 GQA 架构，引入一个轻量级 Index Branch 对 KV 块进行评分，为每个 GQA 组独立选择 Top-k 子集，实现组特定的稀疏检索；Main Branch 仅对选中的块执行精确的块稀疏注意力。为将稀疏性转化为实际加速，设计了 co-designed GPU 执行路径，使用无指数 Top-k 选择和 KV-outer 稀疏注意力来提升块粒度访问下的 tensor core 利用率。

Result: 在 109B 参数的原生多模态训练模型上，MSA 在 1M 上下文长度下将每 token 注意力计算量减少 28.4 倍，性能与 GQA 相当。配合 co-designed kernel，在 H800 GPU 上实现 14.2 倍 prefill 和 7.6 倍 decoding 墙钟加速。

Conclusion: MSA 通过简单、可扩展的块级稀疏注意力设计，在保持模型质量的同时显著降低了超长上下文下的计算开销，并已在生产级原生多模态模型中部署验证，证明了其在实际场景中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MiniMax Sparse Attention (MSA)，一种基于 GQA 的块级稀疏注意力机制，旨在降低超长上下文 LLM 的注意力计算成本。论文在 109B 原生多模态训练模型上验证，属于多模态理解与生成方向中的高效化方法，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化" (efficient_multimodal)。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with efficient inference and multimodal model priorities, offering practical speedups for long-context attention. Signals: efficient inference, sparse attention, GQA, long-context, 109B multimodal model

Abstract: Ultra-long-context capability is becoming indispensable for frontier LLMs: agentic workflows, repository-scale code reasoning, and persistent memory all require the model to jointly attend over hundreds of thousands to millions of tokens, yet the quadratic cost of softmax attention makes this untenable at deployment scale. We introduce MiniMax Sparse Attention (MSA), a blockwise sparse attention built upon Grouped Query Attention (GQA). A lightweight Index Branch scores key-value blocks and independently selects a Top-k subset for each GQA group, enabling group-specific sparse retrieval while maintaining efficient block-level execution; the Main Branch then performs exact block-sparse attention over only the selected blocks. Designed around a principle of simplicity and scalability, MSA is deliberately streamlined, making it straightforward to deploy efficiently across a broad range of GPUs. To translate sparsity into practical speedups, we co-design MSA with a GPU execution path that uses exp-free Top-k selection and KV-outer sparse attention to improve tensor-core utilization under block-granular access. On a 109B-parameter model with native multimodal training, MSA performs on par with GQA while reducing per-token attention compute by 28.4x at 1M context. Paired with our co-designed kernel, MSA achieves 14.2x prefill and 7.6x decoding wall-clock speedups on H800. Our inference kernel is available at: . A production-grade natively multimodal model powered by MSA has been publicly released at: .

</details>
