### [90] [SpatialClaw: Rethinking Action Interface for Agentic Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13673)
*Seokju Cho, Ryo Hachiuma, Abhishek Badki, Hang Su, Byung-Kwan Lee, Chan Hee Song, Sifei Liu, Subhashree Radhakrishnan, Seungryong Kim, Yu-Chiang Frank Wang, Min-Hung Chen*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (61.6) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SpatialClaw 提出一种无需训练的空间推理框架，通过将代码作为动作接口，让 VLM 代理在状态化 Python 内核中逐步执行感知与几何操作，在 20 个 3D/4D 空间推理基准上平均准确率 59.9%，超越此前方法 11.2 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具增强型空间推理代理受限于动作接口设计：单次代码执行在观察到中间结果前就提交完整分析策略，结构化工具调用则缺乏灵活组合能力。两者均难以应对开放式的复杂 3D/4D 空间推理任务。

Method: SpatialClaw 采用代码作为动作接口，维护一个状态化 Python 内核，预加载输入帧和感知/几何原语库。VLM 代理每步编写一个可执行代码单元，基于所有先前输出进行条件化执行，灵活组合和操作感知结果，适应中间文本/视觉观察和任务需求。

Result: 在 20 个静态和动态 3D/4D 空间推理基准上，SpatialClaw 平均准确率 59.9%，比最近的空间代理方法高出 11.2 个百分点，在 6 种 VLM 骨干网络上均有一致提升，无需任何基准或模型特定适配。

Conclusion: 代码作为动作接口的设计为 VLM 代理提供了更大的灵活性和适应性，能够有效处理开放式的复杂空间推理任务，是一种无需训练且通用的空间推理增强方法。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 VLM 在 3D/4D 空间推理中的能力，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的空间与 3D 多模态推理（spatial_3d_reasoning）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Strong spatial reasoning method but not aligned with user's priority subtopics on continuous models or generation. Signals: code-as-action interface, training-free spatial reasoning, stateful Python kernel, 20 benchmark evaluation, consistent VLM gains

Abstract: Spatial reasoning, the ability to determine where objects are, how they relate, and how they move in 3D, remains a fundamental challenge for vision-language models (VLMs). Tool-augmented agents attempt to address this by augmenting VLMs with specialist perception modules, yet their effectiveness is bounded by the action interface through which those tools are invoked. In this work, we study how the design of this interface shapes the agent's capacity for open-ended spatial reasoning. Existing spatial agents either employ single-pass code execution, which commits to a full analysis strategy before any intermediate result is observed, or rely on a structured tool-call interface that often offers less flexibility for freely composing operations or tailoring the analysis to each task. Both designs offer limited flexibility for open-ended, complex 3D/4D spatial reasoning. We therefore propose SpatialClaw, a training-free framework for spatial reasoning that adopts code as the action interface. SpatialClaw maintains a stateful Python kernel pre-loaded with input frames and a suite of perception and geometry primitives, letting a VLM-backed agent write one executable cell per step conditioned on all prior outputs, enabling the agent to flexibly compose and manipulate perception results and adapt its analysis to both intermediate text and visual observations and the demands of each problem. Evaluated across 20 spatial reasoning benchmarks spanning a broad range of static and dynamic 3D/4D spatial reasoning tasks, SpatialClaw achieves 59.9% average accuracy, outperforming the recent spatial agent by +11.2 points, with consistent gains across six VLM backbones from two model families without any benchmark- or model-specific adaptation.

</details>

### [91] [HYDRA-X: Native Unified Multimodal Models with Holistic Visual Tokenizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13289)
*Guozhen Zhang, Xuerui Qiu, Yutao Cui, Tianhui Song, Changlin Li, Junzhe Li, Tao Huang, Xiao Zhang, Yang Li, Jianbing Wu, Miles Yang, Zhao Zhong, Liefeng Bo, Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (97.15) - Top 1 精读候选

TL;DR: HYDRA-X 是首个在单一 ViT 中统一图像和视频 tokenization 的原生统一多模态模型（UMM），通过帧级因果时序注意力、分层时序压缩和轻量级解压缩器实现高效的视觉重建与语义感知，在 7B 规模上取得图像和视频理解与生成的强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型（UMM）通常依赖独立的图像和视频 tokenizer，缺乏统一的视觉 tokenization 方案。如何将时空重建能力高效注入原生 ViT，并在紧凑潜空间中嵌入图像级和视频级语义感知，是构建真正统一 tokenizer UMM 的核心挑战。

Method: (1) 帧级因果时序注意力：仅需帧级因果时序注意力即可实现视觉重建，全时空注意力反而会降低重建质量；(2) 分层时序压缩：多步分层压缩显著优于单步压缩；(3) 轻量级解压缩器：在联合图像-视频教师监督下对时序压缩特征进行上采样，在紧凑潜空间中强制互补语义结构；(4) 编辑流水线改进：将源-目标交互从 LLM 语义层移至 tokenizer 潜层，提升编辑一致性并加速收敛。

Result: HYDRA-X（7B 密集模型）在图像和视频的理解与生成任务上均取得了强性能，验证了统一 tokenizer 设计的有效性，为未来统一 tokenizer UMM 铺平了道路。

Conclusion: HYDRA-X 证明了在单一 ViT 中统一图像和视频 tokenization 是可行的，帧级因果时序注意力和分层时序压缩是关键设计选择，潜层编辑交互优于语义层交互。该工作为构建真正的原生统一多模态模型提供了重要基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 HYDRA-X，首个在单一 ViT 中统一图像和视频 tokenization 的原生统一多模态模型（UMM），实现理解与生成一体化。这直接对应 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下的 [CANONICAL] subtopic "统一多模态模型"（unified_multimodal_model），同时其视觉 tokenizer 设计也与 multimodal_generation 方向相关。

Importance reason: Directly advances unified multimodal models with a novel holistic visual tokenizer, aligning with top priority subtopics and boost keywords. Signals: unified multimodal model, holistic visual tokenizer, image and video tokenization, frame-level causal temporal attention, hierarchical temporal compression

Abstract: Holistic visual tokenizers are fundamental to unified multimodal models (UMMs) as they map diverse visual inputs into a unified representation space. In this paper, we present HYDRA-X, the first UMM that unifies image and video tokenization within a single Vision Transformer (ViT). Our design is driven by two core challenges: efficiently injecting spatiotemporal reconstruction capability into a native ViT, and embedding image- and video-level semantic awareness into the latent space. To address the first, comprehensive ablations reveal two key findings: (1) frame-level causal temporal attention suffices for visual reconstruction, whereas full spatiotemporal attention degrades it; and (2) hierarchical temporal compression substantially outperforms single-step alternatives. To tackle the second, we propose a lightweight decompressor that upsamples temporally compressed features under joint image-video teacher supervision, thereby enforcing complementary semantic structures within the compact latent space. Building on this holistic tokenizer, we further propose a principled improvement of the editing pipeline: source-target interaction should occur at the latent level inside the tokenizer rather than at the semantic level inside the LLM, substantially improving editing consistency and accelerating convergence. Instantiated at the 7B dense model, HYDRA-X achieves strong performance across image and video understanding and generation tasks, paving the way for future unified-tokenizer UMMs.

</details>


### [92] [InterleaveThinker: Reinforcing Agentic Interleaved Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13679)
*Dian Zheng, Harry Lee, Manyuan Zhang, Kaituo Feng, Zoey Guo, Ray Zhang, Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: InterleaveThinker 是一个多智能体流水线，通过规划器（planner）和评论家（critic）两个智能体，使任意现有图像生成器具备文本-图像交错生成能力。使用 SFT 冷启动和 GRPO 强化学习训练评论家进行单步指令修正，在交错生成基准上达到与 GPT-5 相当的性能，并显著提升基础模型在推理基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成器在单图生成和编辑上表现出色，但受限于架构无法实现文本-图像交错生成（interleaved generation），而这一能力在视觉叙事、引导和具身操作等场景中至关重要。即使最新的开源统一多模态模型（UMMs）在此任务上表现也有限。

Method: 提出 InterleaveThinker 多智能体流水线：(1) 规划器（planner agent）组织图像-文本输入序列，为每一步指示图像生成器需要执行的任务；(2) 评论家（critic agent）评估生成器输出，识别偏离规划指令的样本并修正指令以重新生成。构建 Interleave-Planner-SFT-80k 和 Interleave-Critic-SFT-112k 进行格式冷启动，再构建 Interleave-Critic-RL-13k 使用 GRPO 强化学习训练评论家的单步指令修正能力。提出准确率奖励（accuracy reward）和步级奖励（step-wise reward），使单步 RL 能有效引导整个生成轨迹。

Result: InterleaveThinker 在多种图像生成器上均提升性能。在交错生成基准上达到与 Nano Banana 和 GPT-5 相当的性能。在推理基准上显著增强基础模型，例如在 4-step FLUX.2-klein 上，WISE 和 RISE 指标均有大幅提升。

Conclusion: InterleaveThinker 通过多智能体流水线成功赋予现有图像生成器交错生成能力，无需修改生成器架构。该方法在交错生成任务上达到顶尖水平，并意外地提升了基础模型的推理能力，展示了交错生成与推理之间的潜在协同效应。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 InterleaveThinker，一个多智能体流水线，使现有图像生成器具备交错生成（文本-图像序列）能力，属于理解与生成一体化、Any-to-Any 多模态模型范畴。该论文同时涉及多模态生成方向（图像生成器增强），但核心贡献是统一多模态模型中的交错生成能力，因此主方向为多模态理解与生成，匹配现有 subtopic "统一多模态模型"（该 subtopic 在 multimodal_understanding_generation 和 multimodal_generation 下均有定义）。

Importance reason: Novel multi-agent pipeline enabling interleaved generation with RL fine-tuning, directly relevant to unified multimodal models and agentic generation. Signals: interleaved generation, multi-agent pipeline, GRPO reinforcement learning, unified multimodal model, planner-critic architecture

Abstract: Recent image generators have demonstrated impressive photorealism and instruction-following capabilities in single-image generation and editing. However, constrained by their architectures, they cannot achieve interleaved generation (text-image sequence), which has crucial applications in visual narratives, guidance, and embodied manipulation. Even the latest open-source Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) exhibit limited performance in this regard. In this paper, we introduce InterleaveThinker, the first multi-agent pipeline designed to endow any existing image generator with interleaved generation capabilities. Specifically, we employ a planner agent to organize the image-text input sequence, instructing the image generator on the required execution at each step. Subsequently, we introduce a critic agent to evaluate the generator's outputs, identify samples that deviate from the planned instructions, and refine the instructions for regeneration. To implement this pipeline, we construct the Interleave-Planner-SFT-80k and Interleave-Critic-SFT-112k to perform a format cold-start. Then we develop Interleave-Critic-RL-13k to reinforce the step-wise instruction correction capability within a generation trajectory using GRPO. Since a single interleaved generation trajectory may involve over 25 generator calls, optimizing the entire trajectory is computationally impractical. Therefore, we propose accuracy reward and step-wise reward, allowing single-step RL to effectively guide the entire generation trajectory. The results show that InterleaveThinker improves performance across various image generators. On interleaved generation benchmarks, it achieves performance comparable to Nano Banana and GPT-5. Surprisingly, it also significantly enhances the base model on reasoning-based benchmarks; for example, on 4-step FLUX.2-klein, we observe substantial gains on WISE and RISE.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-vlm_mllm_general'></div>

## 视觉语言模型与 MLLM [[Back]](#toc)

通用视觉语言模型、MLLM 架构、多模态指令微调与对话。

### [93] [PersonaDrive: Human-Style Retrieval-Augmented VLA Agents for Closed-Loop Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12616)
*Mahmoud Srewa, Praneetsai Iddamsetty, Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque, Salma Elmalaki*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (89.43) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PersonaDrive 提出一种检索增强的 VLA 驾驶智能体，利用风格指导的人类驾驶数据集中的演示作为上下文示例，通过离线三元组挖掘、轻量检索头训练和单 VLA 骨干微调，实现无需逐风格重训练即可切换驾驶风格（激进/中性/保守），在 Bench2Drive 上取得最优驾驶分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有闭环驾驶仿真中的非自车交通智能体行为单一（规则驱动或单模态学习），虽有工作通过后验标签或 LLM 推断奖励权重引入风格变化，但这些信号只是风格的代理而非人类实际按风格驾驶的演示。需要一种能基于真实人类风格驾驶数据来条件化 VLA 智能体的方法。

Method: 三阶段流水线：(i) 离线三元组挖掘：在风格指导的人类驾驶数据上，使用图像-文本联合相似度评分挖掘正负样本；(ii) 训练轻量检索头：融合冻结的视觉特征与小型控制编码器，构建逐风格数据库；(iii) 微调单一 VLA 骨干：将检索到的上下文点作为上下文行为演示，用于航点预测。推理时通过切换检索头查询的数据库即可切换风格，无需逐风格重训练。

Result: 在 Bench2Drive 上，PersonaDrive（无风格）驾驶分数比 SimLingo 高 4.6%，比 HiP-AD 高 2.5%；在风格条件下，每种风格均取得最高驾驶分数，最弱风格仍超过最强基线 DMW 5.4%；从保守到激进指令，平均速度和加速度分别提升 18% 和 25%。

Conclusion: PersonaDrive 通过检索增强和真实人类风格驾驶数据，实现了高效、可切换风格的 VLA 驾驶智能体，为闭环驾驶仿真提供了更真实、多样化的非自车交通行为建模方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 PersonaDrive，一种基于检索增强的 VLA（视觉-语言-动作）驾驶智能体，属于视觉语言模型在驾驶仿真中的应用。主要匹配方向为多模态理解与生成，具体归入 [CANONICAL] 的 vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM），因为其核心是 VLA 模型架构与多模态指令微调。同时与 world_model 方向下的 embodied_world_model 相关，因为涉及闭环驾驶仿真中的具身智能体行为建模。

Importance reason: Novel retrieval-augmented VLA driving agent with style-conditioned in-context learning, but only tangentially touches priority subtopics. Signals: retrieval-augmented VLA, style-conditioned driving, in-context learning, closed-loop simulation, Bench2Drive SOTA

Abstract: Closed-loop driving simulators typically populate their environments with non-ego traffic agents that behave largely the same way, produced either by rule-based traffic managers or by learned models trained toward a single behavioral mode. Recent work introduces style variation through post-hoc labels on observational data or LLM-inferred reward weights, but these signals act as proxies for what a style should reward rather than demonstrations of humans explicitly asked to drive in that style. We introduce PersonaDrive, a pipeline that conditions a vision-language-action (VLA) driving agent on retrieved demonstrations from a style-instructed human driving dataset, in which participants drive CARLA leaderboard routes under aggressive, neutral, and conservative instructions on a driver-in-the-loop rig. The pipeline has three stages: (i) offline triplet mining over per-style human driving data using a combined image-text similarity score; (ii) training a lightweight retrieval head that fuses frozen visual features with a small control encoder over per-style databases; and (iii) fine-tuning a single VLA backbone to treat retrieved context points as in-context behavioral demonstrations during waypoint prediction. At inference, the same backbone is conditioned on any style by swapping which per-style database the retrieval head queries, so selecting a style requires no per-style retraining while enabling human-style, style-diverse non-ego agents for closed-loop simulation. On Bench2Drive, PersonaDrive (no style) improves the driving score by 4.6% over SimLingo and 2.5% over HiP-AD, and under style conditioning attains the highest driving score in every style within a roughly 2% band (its weakest style surpassing the strongest baseline, DMW, by 5.4%), while average speed and acceleration rise by 18% and 25% from the conservative to the aggressive instruction.

</details>


### [94] [Teach-and-Repeat: Accurately Extracting Operational Knowledge from Mobile Screen Demonstrations to Empower GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12817)
*Yudong Zhang, Lei Hu, Daoyang Liu, Jiawei Liu, Yangfan Luo, Xingyu Liu, Zuojian Wang, Zhilin Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Teach VLM 模型，用于从手机屏幕演示视频中提取逐步操作知识（操作类型、目标UI元素、参数和执行顺序），并构建 Teach-and-Repeat 范式，将生成的操作知识作为可解释的过程参考来指导下游 GUI 执行智能体，在操作语义预测和 Android World 任务成功率上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在理解移动设备上的动态操作（从静态 UI 感知到动态动作理解）方面能力不足，难以从屏幕轨迹中准确推断底层操作，且缺乏对齐的训练数据。

Method: 提出 Teach VLM 模型，通过提取和分析演示视频中的操作相关关键帧，将手机屏幕轨迹转化为逐步的操作知识（自然语言描述的操作类型、目标 UI 元素、文本参数和执行顺序）。开发了系统化的数据飞轮用于可扩展的数据采集，并构建了中文手机屏幕教学基准。基于 Teach VLM 提出 Teach-and-Repeat 范式，将生成的操作知识作为可解释的过程参考来指导下游执行智能体。

Result: Teach VLM 在操作语义预测任务上显著优于强 VLM 基线，达到 SOTA 性能。在 Android World 实验中，Teach-and-Repeat 范式为下游智能体带来一致的任务成功率提升。

Conclusion: Teach VLM 和 Teach-and-Repeat 范式提供了一条从原始演示到可复用任务自动化的实用路径，展示了将视觉状态转换转化为可解释操作知识的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Teach VLM，一个用于从手机屏幕演示视频中提取操作知识的视觉语言模型，属于通用 VLM 在 GUI 智能体场景中的应用。映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）。

Importance reason: Focuses on GUI agent knowledge extraction from mobile screens, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: GUI agent, operational knowledge extraction, mobile screen demonstration, VLM for action comprehension, Teach-and-Repeat paradigm

Abstract: Understanding the digital world on mobile devices is shifting from static UI perception to dynamic action comprehension. This capability enables models to convert visual state transitions into operational knowledge, defined as short natural-language sentences that describe action types, target UI elements, textual arguments, and execution orders. However, due to the highly diverse and heterogeneous UI designs across applications, existing vision-language models (VLMs) struggle to accurately infer these underlying operations. To bridge this gap, we introduce Teach VLM, a core model designed to translate mobile screen trajectories into step-wise operational knowledge by extracting and analyzing operation-related keyframes from demonstration videos. To address the scarcity of aligned training data, we develop a systematic data flywheel for scalable data acquisition. We further introduce a novel Chinese Mobile Screen Teach Benchmark for fine-grained evaluation. Building upon Teach VLM, we propose the Teach-and-Repeat paradigm, where the generated operational knowledge serves as an interpretable procedural reference to guide downstream screen-based execution agents. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that Teach VLM significantly outperforms strong VLM baselines, achieving state-of-the-art performance in operation semantics prediction. Furthermore, experiments in Android World show that our paradigm yields consistent Task Success Rate improvements for downstream agents. Together, Teach VLM and the Teach-and-Repeat paradigm offer a practical pathway from raw demonstrations to reusable task automation.

</details>


### [95] [OpenMedQ: Broad Open Pretraining for Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12953)
*Ibrahim Gulluk, Max Van Puyvelde, Olivier Gevaert*

Main category: cs.LG, eess.IV, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (65.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: OpenMedQ 是一个在最大规模完全开源医学混合数据集（14个数据集，约335万样本）上预训练的医学视觉语言模型，在 PathVQA 和 VQA-MED 上达到 SOTA 或最佳 BLEU-1 分数，其视觉编码器在8个未见医学分类任务上取得最高平均 macro-F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学 VLM 通常依赖私有数据或受限数据集，缺乏完全开源且大规模预训练的基线模型。作者旨在构建一个完全开源、数据规模最大、覆盖多种医学模态（病理、放射、显微镜等）的医学 VLM 预训练基线，以促进社区的可复现研究。

Method: 收集14个公开医学数据集（约335万样本），涵盖病理、放射、显微镜和纯文本临床 QA。基于这些数据对视觉语言模型进行预训练，并开源代码和交互式 demo。

Result: 在 PathVQA 上达到 BLEU-1 75.9，超越 Med-PaLM M 系列（最大562B参数）；在 VQA-MED 上 BLEU-1 64.5 与最佳结果持平。视觉编码器在8个未见医学分类任务上平均 macro-F1 0.757，优于 BiomedCLIP、PMC-CLIP、PubMedCLIP 等基线。

Conclusion: OpenMedQ 证明了大规模开源医学数据预训练的有效性，为医学 VLM 社区提供了一个可复现的强基线，并展示了在多种医学模态上的优异迁移能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出医学视觉语言模型 OpenMedQ，属于通用视觉语言模型（VLM/MLLM）范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong medical VLM benchmark but outside user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: Medical VLM, SOTA on PathVQA, fully-open dataset, vision encoder transfer, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: We present OpenMedQ, a medical vision-language model pretrained on the broadest fully-open medical mix to date: 14 datasets totaling ~3.35M pretraining samples spanning pathology, radiology, microscopy, and text-only clinical QA. OpenMedQ reaches state-of-the-art BLEU-1 on PathVQA (75.9), beating Med-PaLM M variants up to 562B parameters (~80x larger), and matches the best reported VQA-MED BLEU-1 (64.5). Its vision encoder, transferred to 8 unseen medical classification benchmarks under an identical downstream recipe, obtains the highest average macro-F1 (0.757) among BiomedCLIP (0.745), PMC-CLIP (0.745), PubMedCLIP (0.746), and a from-scratch baseline (0.616). We release our code and an interactive demo is publicly available as a reproducible baseline for the community.

</details>


### [96] [M*: A Modular, Extensible, Serving System for Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12688)
*Atindra Jha, Naomi Sagan, Keisuke Kamahori, Irmak Sivgin, Rohan Sanda, Steven Gao, Mark Horowitz, Luke Zettlemoyer, Olivia Hsu, Jure Leskovec, Baris Kasikci, Stephanie Wang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.DC, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (93.17) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: M* 是一个模块化、可扩展的复合多模态模型服务系统，通过将模型表示为数据流图（Walk Graph 抽象），支持任意组件组合、灵活集群部署和模型无关的分布式优化，在文本到图像、文本到语音和机器人规划等任务上显著优于现有系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型服务框架（如 vLLM）基于对模型结构的狭隘假设设计，无法有效支持日益流行的复合多模态架构（如视觉编码器+语言主干+扩散/flow head+音频编解码器+动作生成器+世界模型预测器等）。亟需一个通用的服务系统来高效部署这些多样化模型。

Method: 提出 M* 通用服务系统，核心是 Walk Graph 抽象——将复合模型表示为数据流图，请求处理转化为图遍历。该抽象支持：(1) 任意模型组件的模块化组合；(2) 组件在物理集群上的灵活放置；(3) 模型无关的分布式运行时优化（如流水线并行、自动调度）。系统支持多种复合模型家族的统一描述和服务。

Result: 在 BAGEL 模型上，文本到图像任务相比 vLLM-Omni 端到端延迟降低 20%；在 Qwen3-Omni 上，文本到语音任务实时因子降低 2.9 倍，吞吐量提升 2.7 倍；在 V-JEPA 2-AC 机器人规划任务上，性能提升高达 12.5 倍。

Conclusion: M* 证明了通过模块化数据流图抽象可以高效服务多样化的复合多模态模型，为未来复杂模型的高效部署铺平了道路，且对开发者只需最小的工作量。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 M*，一个面向复合多模态模型（包括 VLM、Omni 模型、世界模型等）的模块化服务系统，属于多模态模型架构与系统优化方向。最佳匹配方向为多模态理解与生成，复用其下 [CANONICAL] 的 vlm_mllm_general 子主题，因为论文核心关注多模态模型的通用服务架构，涵盖 VLM、Omni 模型等。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient serving of unified multimodal models (MLLM) with a modular system, aligning with high-priority subtopics and boost keywords. Signals: unified multimodal serving, modular composite model, efficient inference, distributed runtime, MLLM system

Abstract: We are entering a new era of composite model architectures that integrate diverse components such as vision encoders, language backbones, diffusion and flow heads, audio codecs, action generators, and world-model predictors. Such architectures underpin a broad class of multimodal models, including unified multimodal models, omni models, speech-language models, vision-language-action policies, and world models. However, existing model serving frameworks were built on narrow assumptions about model structure, making them ill-suited to accommodate this new architectural diversity. Here we present M*, a universal serving system for efficient serving of composite AI models. M* represents models as dataflow graphs, processing requests spanning diverse modalities and tasks as traversals over these graphs. The core insight is a modular abstraction that supports arbitrary composition of model components, flexible placement onto a physical cluster, and model-agnostic optimizations within a distributed runtime. We call this abstraction the Walk Graph and show how it can concisely capture composite models from a broad range of families. We instantiate M* on representative models and find that it achieves, on average, 20% lower end-to-end latency than vLLM-Omni for text-to-image workloads on BAGEL, while delivering up to 2.9x lower real-time factor and 2.7x higher throughput for text-to-speech workloads on Qwen3-Omni. M* also outperforms the V-JEPA 2-AC rollout baseline for robotic planning by up to 12.5x. Thus, our work paves the road towards more efficient serving of complex models with minimal developer effort.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_vqa_doc'></div>

### [97] [Magnifying What Matters: Attention-Guided Adaptive Rendering for Visual Text Comprehension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12898)
*Shenglai Zeng, Qirui Wang, Kai Guo, Xinnan Dai, Xianxuan Long, Hui Liu*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过实证研究发现VLM在视觉文本理解中存在"定位而不利用"的现象：中间到后期层的注意力能定位文本证据但与答案正确性脱钩。基于此提出AGAR方法，利用VLM自身注意力识别重要视觉patch并重新渲染放大，无需训练即可提升VLM在VTC任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VTC流水线将渲染和布局视为固定的、与内容无关的预处理步骤，缺乏对VLM内部如何处理可视化文本的机制理解。作者希望揭示VLM处理视觉文本的内部机制，并据此设计无需训练、模型无关的改进方法。

Method: AGAR（Attention-Guided Adaptive Rendering）：利用VLM中间到后期层的注意力识别top-K重要的视觉patch，将其映射回文本跨度，重新渲染页面时放大这些跨度，然后重新推理答案。该方法无需训练、模型无关。

Result: 在9个VTC基准（短文本、长上下文、多页记忆QA）和4个VLM骨干上，AGAR作为即插即用增强持续提升现成VLM的性能，可与VLM后训练组合获得进一步增益，且在视觉和文本输入退化下保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: VLM在VTC任务中存在"定位而不利用"的机制现象，而基于注意力引导的自适应渲染可以有效缓解这一问题，为理解VLM内部机制和提升VTC性能提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉文本理解（VTC），涉及将文本渲染为图像供VLM阅读，用于长页OCR和多页记忆QA等任务，属于视觉问答与文档理解范畴，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"。

Importance reason: Novel mechanistic insight into VLM visual text processing, but tangential to user's priority on continuous/multimodal models and generation. Signals: VLM attention analysis, training-free enhancement, visual text comprehension, localization-without-utilization, plug-and-play improvement

Abstract: Visual Text Comprehension (VTC) renders text into images for a vision-language model (VLM) to read, sidestepping LLM context-window limits and powering applications from long-page OCR to multi-page memory QA. Yet existing VTC pipelines treat rendering and layout as a fixed, content-agnostic preprocessing step and offer little mechanistic understanding of how VLMs internally process visualized text. Through a focused empirical study on VTC QA tasks, we reveal that VLMs exhibit a localization-without-utilization regime: evidence-localizing attention emerges sharply in the middle-to-late layers and is largely decoupled from answer correctness, yet simply enlarging the localized spans on the rendered page recovers a large fraction of the failures. Building on these observations, we propose AGAR (Attention-Guided Adaptive Rendering), a training-free, model-agnostic method that leverages a VLM's own middle-to-late layer attention to identify the top-K important visual patches, maps them back to word spans, and re-renders the page with those spans enlarged before re-inferring the answer. Extensive experiments across nine VTC benchmarks (short-form, long-context, and multi-page memory QA) and four VLM backbones show that AGAR (i)consistently improves off-the-shelf VLMs as a plug-and-play enhancement, (ii)composes with VLM post-training to yield further gains, and (iii)remains robust under both visual- and text-side input degradation.

</details>


### [98] [PP-OCRv6: From 1.5M to 34.5M Parameters, Surpassing Billion-Scale VLMs on OCR Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13108)
*Yubo Zhang, Xueqing Wang, Manhui Lin, Yue Zhang, Penglongyi Deng, Ting Sun, Tingquan Gao, Zelun Zhang, Jiaxuan Liu, Changda Zhou, Hongen Liu, Suyin Liang, Cheng Cui, Yi Liu, Dianhai Yu, Yanjun Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PP-OCRv6 是一个轻量级 OCR 系统，通过统一的 MetaFormer 骨干网络和结构重参数化设计，在 3450 万参数下超越千亿级 VLM（如 Qwen3-VL-235B、GPT-5.5）的 OCR 性能，同时支持从服务器到边缘的多场景部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在通用视觉语言任务上表现优异，但在专用 OCR 场景中存在幻觉、定位不精确和计算成本过高的问题。作者希望设计一个轻量级、高精度且高效的 OCR 系统来弥补这一差距。

Method: PP-OCRv6 围绕统一的 MetaFormer 风格构建块重新设计了骨干网络、检测颈部和识别颈部，采用结构重参数化解耦空间 token 混合与通道混合，并通过任务特定的 stride 配置同时支持检测和识别任务。提供 medium/small/tiny 三个模型层级，覆盖从服务器到边缘的部署场景。

Result: PP-OCRv6_medium 在内部基准上达到 83.2% 识别准确率和 86.2% 检测 Hmean，分别超过 PP-OCRv5_server 5.1% 和 4.6%，同时以数量级更少的参数超越 Qwen3-VL-235B、GPT-5.5 和 Gemini-3.1-Pro。tiny 版本在 Intel Xeon CPU 上推理速度比 PP-OCRv5_mobile 快 3.9 倍，精度相当。

Conclusion: PP-OCRv6 证明了通过架构创新与数据驱动优化的结合，轻量级专用 OCR 系统可以在精度上超越大规模通用 VLM，同时保持高效推理，为 OCR 领域的实际部署提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出PP-OCRv6轻量级OCR系统，专注于文档OCR识别与检测任务，属于文档理解与OCR方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解" (multimodal_vqa_doc)。

Importance reason: Strong engineering contribution for OCR but outside user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: Lightweight OCR, surpasses billion-scale VLMs, MetaFormer backbone, structural reparameterization, multi-tier deployment

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved impressive results on general vision-language tasks, yet they suffer from hallucination, imprecise localization, and prohibitive computational cost when applied to dedicated OCR scenarios. This paper presents PP-OCRv6, a lightweight OCR system that combines architectural innovation with data-centric optimization. PP-OCRv6 redesigns the backbone, detection neck, and recognition neck around a unified MetaFormer-style building block with structural reparameterization, decoupling spatial token mixing from channel mixing and supporting both tasks through task-specific stride configurations. Three model tiers (medium, small, tiny) share the same block primitives, covering deployment scenarios from server to edge. On our in-house benchmarks, PP-OCRv6_medium achieves 83.2% recognition accuracy and 86.2% detection Hmean, outperforming PP-OCRv5_server by +5.1% and +4.6% respectively while surpassing Qwen3-VL-235B, GPT-5.5, and Gemini-3.1-Pro with orders of magnitude fewer parameters. The tiny tier achieves 3.9$\times$ faster inference than PP-OCRv5_mobile on Intel Xeon CPU while maintaining comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [99] [Zero-Shot Captioning for Cultural Heritage: Automated Image Analysis of Traditional Indonesian Clothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13275)
*Anugrah Aidin Yotolembah, Novanto Yudistira, Gembong Edhi Setyawan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (46.5) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Custom ZeroCLIP，一个检索增强的视觉语言框架，用于印尼传统服饰的零样本图像描述生成。模型在 24 个已知省份上训练，在 8 个未见省份上零样本评估，结合 CLIP、BERT 和 LSTM 解码器，通过检索增强实现文化词汇恢复，CLIPScore 达 0.8536。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化遗产领域的图像描述生成面临标注数据稀缺和领域专业词汇（如传统服饰名称）难以泛化的挑战。现有零样本方法在低资源文化遗产场景下表现不佳，无法准确识别和描述具有文化特异性的视觉概念。

Method: 提出 Custom ZeroCLIP 框架：使用冻结的 CLIP ViT-B/32 图像编码器和文本编码器提取视觉与文本特征，结合 BERT 文本编码器增强语义理解，通过 LSTM 解码器生成描述。采用省份级别的归纳式零样本协议，训练时仅使用已知省份数据，推理时通过检索训练省份的描述来辅助未见省份的图像描述生成。

Result: 在 8 个未见省份上，Custom ZeroCLIP 达到 CLIPScore 0.8536、BLEU-4 0.3342、METEOR 0.4859，优于现有基线。消融实验表明检索增强带来 19.3% 的 METEOR 提升，人工评估确认了更强的文化准确性和流畅性。

Conclusion: 检索增强的域适应方法能有效提升低资源文化遗产场景下的零样本图像描述生成质量，尤其在文化特有词汇的恢复方面效果显著。该工作为文化遗产数字化保护提供了可行的自动化方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究零样本图像描述生成（zero-shot captioning），属于视觉问答与文档理解（VQA/captioning）范畴。虽然面向文化遗产领域，但核心任务是图像描述生成，与 canonical subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）中的 captioning 方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on a niche cultural heritage captioning task with no overlap with user's priority subtopics or boost keywords. Signals: Zero-shot captioning, Cultural heritage, Indonesian clothing, Retrieval-augmented, Low-resource domain

Abstract: This paper presents Custom ZeroCLIP, a retrieval-augmented vision-language framework for zero-shot captioning of Indonesian traditional garments. The dataset contains 3,800 expert-annotated images from all 38 Indonesian provinces. Using a province-level inductive zero-shot protocol, the model is trained on 24 seen provinces, validated on 6 seen provinces, and evaluated on 8 unseen provinces. The framework combines a frozen CLIP ViT-B/32 image encoder, a CLIP text encoder, a BERT text encoder, and an LSTM caption decoder. During inference, unseen-province labels and captions are unavailable, and retrieval uses only captions from training provinces. No unseen-province image, label, or caption is used during training, validation, or retrieval-bank construction. Custom ZeroCLIP achieves a CLIPScore of 0.8536, BLEU-4 of 0.3342, and METEOR of 0.4859, outperforming existing baselines. Ablation results show that retrieval improves cultural vocabulary recovery with a 19.3\% METEOR gain, while human evaluation confirms stronger cultural accuracy and fluency. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of retrieval-augmented domain adaptation for culturally grounded caption generation in low-resource heritage settings. The dataset is publicly available at .

</details>

## 视频理解与时序推理 [[Back]](#toc)

视频 LLM、视频问答、时序定位、长视频理解、第一视角视频。

### [100] [OR-Action: Multi-Role Video Understanding with Fine-Grained Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13332)
*Felix Tristram, Ege Özsoy, Christian Benz, Marcel Walch, Ghazal Ghazaei, Nassir Navab*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 普通相关 (44.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 OR-Action，首个基于公开手术室数据集的细粒度多角色动作理解基准，并引入纯视觉时序模型，通过多视角到单视角特征对齐策略提升单视角多角色动作识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术室活动理解对于实现工作流感知辅助至关重要，但现有场景图方法缺乏显式时序建模，难以将帧级关系预测转化为时间上连续的细粒度动作。

Method: 基于公开的 ego-exocentric OR 数据集，通过从场景图状态变化中蒸馏生成密集动作片段，构建动作中心基准。提出纯视觉时序模型，并引入多视角到单视角特征对齐策略，提升单视角多角色动作识别性能。

Result: 实验表明现有场景图预测方法难以建模时序结构，即使加入 GNN 显式建模效果仍有限。提出的纯视觉时序模型在使用全部 ego 视频时显著优于图方法，多视角到单视角特征对齐策略有效提升了单视角性能。

Conclusion: OR-Action 基准为手术室理解提供了更合理的时序评估框架，纯视觉时序模型在细粒度动作识别上优于图方法，多视角特征对齐可减少对大量 ego 视频采集的依赖。

Classification reason: 该论文关注手术室视频中的多角色细粒度动作理解与时间建模，属于视频理解与时序推理方向，与现有 canonical subtopic "视频理解与时序推理" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on surgical video understanding, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and has negative keywords like medical diagnosis. Signals: Surgical domain, fine-grained action recognition, multi-role video understanding, scene graph, temporal modeling

Abstract: Fine-grained understanding of operating room (OR) activity could enable workflow-aware assistance, yet remains difficult due to clutter, occlusions, and limited sensing. The prevailing approach to model this environment is scene graphs as an interpretable representation of OR interactions. Converting their frame-wise relational predictions into temporally extended, fine-grained actions however, is challenging without explicit temporal modeling. To enable a principled temporal evaluation of current OR understanding methods, we introduce the first action-centric benchmark built on a publicly available ego-exocentric OR dataset by defining a fine-grained, multi-role action taxonomy and generating dense action segments via distillation from ground-truth scene graph state changes. Experiments on this benchmark show that current scene graph prediction methods struggle to model temporal structure, even when adding explicit modeling through Graph Neural Networks. We therefore introduce a vision-only temporal model that outperforms graph-based methods significantly when using all available egocentric video as input. Building on this model we also introduce a novel multi- to single-view feature alignment strategy that improves single-view performance on multi-role action recognition, mitigating the need for extensive egocentric video capture. Benchmark and code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>

## 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维 [[Back]](#toc)

多模态模型在连续 latent 中进行 reasoning、planning、visual thought 或 chain-of-continuous-thought。

### [101] [LaME: Learning to Think in Latent Space for Multimodal Embedding via Information Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13061)
*Peixi Wu, Biao Yang, Feipeng Ma, Bosong Chai, Bo Lin, Wei Yuan, Fan Yang, Tingting Gao, Hebei Li, Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维

Importance: 值得关注 (72.15) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LaME 提出了一种基于信息瓶颈的潜空间推理多模态嵌入方法，使用 K 个可学习 reason tokens 在单次前向传播中完成推理，避免了传统文本 CoT 的高计算开销和对 CoT 标注质量的依赖，在保持竞争力的同时实现 60 倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理驱动通用多模态嵌入方法依赖文本 Chain-of-Thought (CoT) 推理，存在两个核心问题：(1) 自回归 CoT 推理计算成本高，不适合低延迟检索场景；(2) 嵌入性能与 CoT 标注质量高度耦合，大规模训练不可靠。这引发了一个根本性问题：文本 CoT 是否是嵌入任务的最优推理形式？能否在潜空间中完成有效的嵌入推理？

Method: LaME 将面向嵌入的潜空间推理建模为弱监督信息瓶颈问题。具体地：(1) 使用 K 个可学习的 reason tokens 作为固定容量的信息瓶颈，在单次前向传播中完成所有推理；(2) 设计两种弱监督信号，在结构上解耦对比学习和自回归目标，消除对 CoT 标注的依赖；(3) 采用两阶段训练流程确保稳定收敛。

Result: 在 MMEB-v2 和 MRMR 基准上，LaME 取得了有竞争力的性能，超越了部分显式 CoT 模型。同时，推理速度比显式 CoT 方法快 60 倍，比潜空间基线快 2 倍，吞吐量与判别式嵌入模型相当。

Conclusion: LaME 验证了潜空间推理作为文本 CoT 替代方案的可行性，在保持嵌入性能的同时大幅提升推理效率，为高效多模态嵌入提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出在潜空间中进行多模态嵌入推理（Latent Reasoning），使用可学习的 reason tokens 作为信息瓶颈，替代传统的文本 CoT 推理。这与 [CANONICAL] 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维（continuous_multimodal_reasoning）高度匹配，该 subtopic 定义即为"多模态模型在连续 latent 中进行 reasoning、planning、visual thought 或 chain-of-continuous-thought"。同时论文也涉及连续语言/多模态模型方向。

Importance reason: Directly addresses continuous latent reasoning for multimodal embedding with 60x speedup, aligning with top-priority continuous language/multimodal model and efficient inference subtopics. Signals: latent space reasoning, information bottleneck, 60x inference acceleration, eliminates CoT annotation dependency, single forward pass reasoning

Abstract: Reasoning-driven universal multimodal embedding has advanced rapidly by introducing Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning into the embedding pipeline. Despite the strong performance across both general and complex tasks, this paradigm suffers from two core limitations: (i) autoregressive CoT reasoning incurs high computational cost, making it impractical for low-latency retrieval; and (ii) embedding performance is heavily coupled with CoT annotation quality, making large-scale training unreliable. These raise fundamental questions: Is textual CoT the optimal form of reasoning for embedding, and can effective embedding reasoning be accomplished in latent space? To this end, we propose LaME (Latent Reasoning Multimodal Embedding), which formulates embedding-oriented latent reasoning as a weakly supervised information bottleneck. LaME employs K learnable reason tokens as a fixed-capacity bottleneck, completing all reasoning within a single forward pass. The two weak supervision signals structurally decouple contrastive from autoregressive objectives and eliminate dependence on CoT annotations, while a two-stage training pipeline ensures stable convergence. Experiments on MMEB-v2 and MRMR show that LaME achieves competitive performance, surpassing some explicit CoT-based models, while delivering 60x faster inference than explicit CoT methods and 2x faster than latent baselines with throughput comparable to discriminative embedding models. Code will be released.

</details>
